Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 351: Everything but Breathing Is a Lie.
-----------------------------------------------
Bisain was eating at the inn he always frequented.

As always, the Demonic Thirteen were scattered here and there around him, guarding Bisain.

One martial artist came, reported something to Irang, and left.

Irang relayed the contents to Bisain.

“It is a report from the Young Cult Leader’s side.”

Earlier, he had received a report that Geommugeuk and Pyogigwang were being attacked and pursued by assassins. The reason he had not been greatly nervous despite such a report was that the one protecting them was Geommugeuk. As expected, the outcome matched his expectations.

“The assassins have been annihilated.”

Irang reported the details.

“Upon examining the corpses, the assassin organization mobilized this time was the Jiuchuan Hui (九泉會). With all the mobilized assassins dead, they have suffered a heavy blow.”

Bisain was angry not because they had tried to kill Pyogigwang. He was angry because they had tried to kill him despite knowing that Bisain himself intended to protect him.

Geommugeuk, who was tasked with guarding Pyogigwang, was wearing the uniform of the Demonic Thirteen. In other words, the enemy had sent assassins despite clearly knowing that Bisain’s own subordinate was protecting him.

*‘How dare they try to kill even my subordinate?’*

This was ignoring the authority of the Young Alliance Leader—namely, himself—and challenging him head-on.

“Can we not find out who commissioned them?”

Irang shook his head with a troubled expression.

“The Jiuchuan Hui is famous for not discriminating in its targets and for not investigating its clients.”

That was why they were so popular in the assassins’ world. Based on that popularity, they had grown into an organization counted among the greatest in the Central Plains.

“What of the Young Cult Leader?”

“He has hidden his tracks together with Pyogigwang, who was with him.”

There was no way Geommugeuk could have fallen to the assassins, so he would be somewhere safe with Pyogigwang.

Since it had been revealed that the organization using children was not something the Demonic Alliance Leader had ordered to be created, the information Geommugeuk would bring back had become crucial.

“This was not something to be entrusted to the Young Cult Leader alone.”

To Irang’s words, Bisain gave no answer. It was hardly normal to explain his faith to Irang. The friendship with Geommugeuk was perhaps something he would have to keep alone in his heart for a lifetime.

Just then, the proverbial tiger who appeared at the mere mention sent Bisain a voice transmission.

—You should not say such things. You ought to say, ‘I believe in my friend, the Young Cult Leader.’

A smile formed at the corner of Bisain’s lips. The truth was, even while eating, he had been waiting for just such a voice transmission.

—Why not come here instead of sending a voice transmission? You are a full-fledged member of the Demonic Thirteen yourself.

It was a rare joke from Bisain, but this was not a situation to take as a joke.

—I wish I could, but that will not be possible for the time being. They sent assassins as soon as they learned that Pyogigwang had made contact with you. Having failed to kill Pyogigwang, they will be watching you now. Suspecting that you may have uncovered the secret, they could be observing you from somewhere even now.

Bisain’s expression hardened slightly. This was a fight of a different nature than the battles he had fought to become the successor. He was in an even fouler mood at the enemy’s provocation.

—Let us meet at the cliff later.

—Let us.

—Be careful of tails when you come.

Once Geommugeuk’s presence vanished, Bisain put down his chopsticks and rose from his seat. He had even finished all the side dishes he normally would not touch.

* * *

Several dozen martial artists were running in the Alliance Hall training ground. They were running without using internal energy, relying solely on their basic stamina.

People began collapsing from the continuous running that pushed past their limits.

“You applied for the Black Dragon Squad, did you not? If you give up here, you cannot get into the Black Dragon Squad.”

The one who spoke while looking down at the martial artist lying on the ground gasping for breath was Instructor Hwang Seokgyeong.

To enter the organization one aspired to, martial arts skill was important, but the strong stamina and mental strength to match were also necessary. If they could not pass this test, they would be assigned to a place lower than their desired organization.

“I cannot do this anymore. I am sorry.”

Then Hwang Seokgyeong whispered something into his ear. The man who had been on the verge of death got up and ran again.

Instructor Cha, a colleague who had been watching this, approached and asked.

“What did you say to him this time?”

Hwang Seokgyeong answered with a smile.

“I told him that if he acted weak, some other bastard would steal his betrothed back home.”

Instructor Cha exclaimed in admiration.

“As expected. You have even memorized the personal details of all the new recruits who joined the Alliance this time.”

This was one of the reasons Hwang Seokgyeong had made a name for himself as the best instructor. He knew everything about the dozens of new martial artists who entered every month. He had memorized where they were from, their family relations, their personalities and hobbies, and which places they had applied to.

There were several reasons he had made a name for himself as the Fighting King, but the decisive reason was that he was intelligent. He was strong because he was smart.

“I say, Instructor Hwang. It is not as though you receive more pay for working this hard, so take it easy.”

Hwang Seokgyeong simply smiled a kind smile.

Instructor Cha did not know. He did not know how different this instructor persona was from his true nature, that everything except breathing was a lie and an act.

When training ended, the martial artist who had heard the whisper earlier ran over to express his gratitude.

“Thanks to you, I did not give up and succeeded. Thank you.”

Hwang Seokgyeong patted his shoulder with a kind face.

“You must triumph and return home in glory.”

“I will do my best.”

No matter who looked, he seemed like an instructor who truly cherished and liked his juniors.

But the moment he left the training ground, he became not an instructor but the Fighting King. A voice transmission flew in from somewhere.

—The assassins are all dead, and Pyogigwang has disappeared.

He stopped walking for a moment at the unexpected result.

—Did you not say one of the Demonic Thirteen was protecting him?

—That is correct.

—And yet all the assassins are dead?

—Yes, that is correct.

The Fighting King began walking again. He had mobilized external assassins this time because he had had a bad feeling and had not used his own people.

—Did the Young Alliance Leader lay a trap? If so, he may have already noticed my existence.

The Fighting King’s judgment was accurate.

—Prepare them.

—Who is the target?

And he made a decision that would decide the fate of the unorthodox Murim without a moment’s hesitation.

—Bisain.

* * *

That night, when Bisain arrived, Geommugeuk was sitting on the edge of a cliff.

“Where is Pyogigwang?”

Bisain asked, sitting down beside Geommugeuk. In the past, he had always stood by his side, but now he naturally sat in the place beside him.

“I came from watching him live the life of Jei.”

Bisain looked at Geommugeuk as if wondering what he meant, then asked with an expression that said, *Could it be?*

“Surely Leader Pyo did not leave?”

Geommugeuk nodded.

“Is he not the only one who knows the mastermind at present?”

There must have been a reason he had let him go, but Bisain could not understand. Geommugeuk of all people should know that fact well.

Soon, the reason that would shock Bisain was revealed.

“Before leaving, he told me who the mastermind is.”

Bisain stared at Geommugeuk in silence.

Yes, he had believed Geommugeuk would find out the mastermind. That was why he had entrusted Pyogigwang to him.

But he had never imagined he would find out in just one day.

If it were anyone else, they would think it was a lie. They would think he had used some sorcery to find out.

But Bisain could be certain. Pyogigwang must have truly wanted to tell him from the heart. And in just one day.

“You truly are….”

He could not think of the words to express it. Of course, Geommugeuk was not one to miss this moment.

“Your wonderful friend.”

Bisain simply hoped this feeling would not change. Not yet—no, even in the future, he could not fathom defeating this person. Now, not even his pride was hurt by it.

And his surprise was only beginning.

“Who is the mastermind?”

“Do you know Hwang Seokgyeong?”

Now Bisain turned fully toward Geommugeuk. It was a name he had never imagined.

“Surely you do not mean Instructor Hwang of the Alliance Hall?”

“So you know him.”

He had predicted several names that could be the mastermind, but Instructor Hwang was someone he had not expected at all.

“This is hard to believe. Truly hard to believe.”

Hwang Seokgyeong was the model instructor of the Alliance Hall, with a good reputation, good skill, and even a good personality.

Geommugeuk understood Bisain’s surprise. If even he had found it unexpected, how surprised must Bisain be?

But in this situation, the problem was not that an Alliance Hall instructor was the mastermind. It was the fact that the mastermind was the Fighting King.

“Today I observed him secretly, and he was hiding his martial arts.”

He did not speak ambiguously, saying it seemed like he was hiding them. He stated it as a certainty. Because he was an opponent Bisain truly had to be careful of.

“Then he must be a member of that hidden force you spoke of.”

“I believe so.”

“I will capture him immediately and interrogate him.”

Geommugeuk stopped Bisain, who was trying to get up at once.

“His strength is no ordinary matter. You must never try to face him alone. I do not think it would be easy even if you and the Demonic Thirteen attacked together.”

“!”

Bisain looked at Geommugeuk with an expression of disbelief. He thought it might be a joke, but it was not. Geommugeuk was looking at him with a more serious expression than ever.

“The problem is that this is not the matter of one man alone. The Lord of the Liu Sword Gate said they had been given vested rights, so there must be a powerful figure within the Alliance who has joined hands with Instructor Hwang. It could be one person, or several. There is no need to be so surprised. The one who infiltrated the Murim Alliance invested a full ten years. I do not think this man is very different.”

Bisain realized this matter was far more serious and dangerous than he had thought. If it had not been for Geommugeuk, this conspiracy would have continued. The more he thought about it, the more chilling it became.

“He must know by now that they failed to kill Pyogigwang. Will he not flee then?”

If it were anyone else, perhaps. But the Fighting King was someone with overwhelmingly superb martial arts and strong pride in that strength. He was not the type to make running away the solution to a problem.

“If you consider the time he has invested in the Demonic Alliance, he will not back down easily. He will be watching to see how you respond.”

Thinking that there were those who dared to scheme against the Demonic Alliance, fighting spirit boiled up in Bisain’s chest.

But he had to remain calm. The instructor’s strength was said to be far superior to his own.

If the opponent was an expert of that level, asking the Demonic Alliance Leader for help was the wisest choice.

Realistically, it was the best plan, but emotionally, it was the worst.

Because it meant asking the Alliance Leader to handle something he could not handle himself.

Another problem was those who had joined hands with him. Somehow, he had to find out who they were. He could not sleep peacefully with such people nearby.

“If we capture him alive, will he not confess who he is working with?”

To Bisain’s question, Geommugeuk shook his head.

“He will not confess. No, judging by his strength, capturing him alive will likely be impossible.”

Geommugeuk emphasized the Fighting King’s strength once again. Since Bisain was now directly entangled with him, he was warning him to be careful.

After pondering for a moment, Bisain spoke carefully.

“Can you help me just one more time?”

“If it is the Young Alliance Leader of the Demonic Alliance, I can gladly invest. Repay me generously with interest later. What do you need me to do?”

Bisain made a truly unexpected request.

“Would you care to tour the Alliance Hall?”

It meant entering as a new recruit to get close to Instructor Hwang.

“New martial artists enter the Alliance Hall every month. If it is you, could you not approach Instructor Hwang and find out who he is working with?”

This was by no means a request an ordinary person could fulfill. No, not even Jin Hagun, the Lord of the Demon Extermination Corps, could have made such a request.

The mission itself was of extreme difficulty, for an indeterminate amount of time. This was something neither he nor anyone else could do.

However, perhaps if it were someone who could open Pyogigwang’s mouth in just one day.

“He will be focusing on my movements. He would never expect a new recruit to approach him, so you would be striking at his core from within.”

Geommugeuk, who had always answered positively, telling him to trust him, shook his head this time.

“I am not confident I can do that.”

He knew to some extent what kind of person the Fighting King was. He was smarter than anyone. If an eye-catching new recruit appeared in this situation, he would be suspicious from the start. Perhaps at another time, but in the current situation, it was absolutely not easy.

Bisain made a strange expression.

“Why do you look at me like that?”

“Because you seem human too.”

“You have not regarded me as human until now?”

“You would have had to show me a human-like appearance first.”

“I have clung to you so much, asking to be friends, and you say I do not seem human! That is what is truly inhuman!”

After that bout of lighthearted banter, Geommugeuk revealed the reason for his refusal.

“I do not think our opponent will give us that much time.”

Bisain was curious. What had he seen in Hwang Seokgyeong to be so cautious? Why did he think so highly of him?

“When you return, wear the best protective armor you have. You may wear two layers, even three.”

Geommugeuk had surprised him several times today, but he was most surprised by these words.

“Surely? You think he will try to kill even me? Is he truly that reckless?”

The reason Geommugeuk had warned him again and again was to say these very words.

“Yes. If I were him, I would kill you first.”

Absolute Regression — Chapter 352

Chapter 352: Even Back From the Dead, You Have to Crack Jokes.
--------------------------------------------------------------
Amid the silence, this was the thought that surfaced in Bi Sa-in’s mind.

‘If you were him, I would surely be dead.’

No matter how strong and terrifying Hwang Seok-gyeong might be, he could not be as much as you.

For Geom Mugeuk standing as an enemy was frightening just to imagine.

“If I’m fated to die anyway, would wearing two or three layers of protective armor save me?”

“If you were fated to die anyway, you wouldn’t have met me. So wear as many layers as you can.”

Their gazes tangled in midair. Bi Sa-in felt it. Geom Mugeuk was sincerely worried that he might die.

“Wouldn’t it be better if I died? The Sado Alliance would fall into chaos.”

At those words, spoken without meaning them, Geom Mugeuk made him face reality.

“I don’t want to say this to you, but even if you die, the Sado Alliance won’t fall into chaos. The same would be true if I died, or if our friend from the Murim Alliance died.”

He was right. If the Cult Leader or the Alliance Leader died, perhaps, but the death of a successor would not be enough to plunge the Sado Alliance into chaos.

“If anything, it would unite them with the will for vengeance and punishment, and the Sado Alliance would become stronger than usual. What do you say? Will you sacrifice yourself once for the Sado Alliance?”

“I decline.”

Bi Sa-in briefly imagined what would happen after his death. The Thirteen Wolves of the Sado would grieve, and among them, First Wolf would be the most sorrowful. What about the Alliance Leader? How much would he mourn? The fact that he could not quite picture that reaction made him think that he still did not know the Alliance Leader well enough.

The third person who came to mind was Geom Mugeuk.

‘Would this man grieve if I died?’

Just as he wondered why he was having such useless thoughts, Geom Mugeuk abruptly said,

“If you die, I’ll avenge you. I’ll chase them to the ends of the Central Plains, and I won’t leave a single bastard involved in this alive.”

Truly, how was he so good at seeing through people’s hearts?

“Thanks, even if it’s only words.”

A faint smile brushed across Bi Sa-in’s hideous face. For some reason, he felt that if it were Geom Mugeuk, he really would dig to the very end for revenge.

“You look good when you smile exactly like that. If you ever come to like a woman later, smile at her like that from time to time.”

“More nonsense!”

They were exchanging jokes in a situation where he might die. Truly, this would never have been possible if it were anyone other than Geom Mugeuk.

‘I could die?’

It would be a lie to say he had never thought that. But after becoming the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance, he had grown careless without realizing it.

Who would dare try to kill me?

Now it was time to change that thought.

How dare I, living as a martial artist, allow myself such carelessness?

Moreover, if Geom Mugeuk had not told him this directly, he never would have known.

“Very well. I’ll prepare for his ambush.”

“Make sure you wear protective armor.”

“I’ll wear two layers.”

Seeing that Bi Sa-in had accepted it sincerely, Geom Mugeuk felt relieved. Of course, his prediction might be wrong, but there was no harm in being careful in all things.

“Among the official attire of the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado, is there an outfit with a mask?”

“There is.”

Most escort warriors belonging to large organizations had masked attire. They were people who had to secretly protect their target while concealing their identities.

“From now on, have them wear that, and give me a set. And take one person out from among the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado. I’ll take that place.”

“Are you really going this far?”

“I am. Why? Do you dislike it?”

“I don’t mind, but I feel sorry.”

“I’ll collect the interest properly later.”

What Bi Sa-in wanted to ask was this.

“You are the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult. If it becomes known that you served as my subordinate, it won’t be good for you.”

This was different from what had happened earlier with Jin Ha-gun and the Fourteenth and Fifteenth Wolves of the Sado. Right now, he was truly trying to protect him in earnest.

“It is not the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult going under the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance. It is Geom Mugeuk going to Bi Sa-in.”

“!”

Bi Sa-in sensed it instinctively. These words of Geom Mugeuk’s would not be forgotten for a very long time.

“I haven’t done anything for you that would make you go this far for me.”

“If you place a scale between people, that makes you a merchant at a business partner, not a friend. In relationships between people, one of the two is bound to like the other more. That’s me. Me.”

He was truly grateful for Geom Mugeuk, who said such things, and he liked him for it.

From the moment he was born until he became the successor of the Sado Alliance, he had never once thought he would have a friend. He had believed it could not possibly happen.

Geom Mugeuk smiled and added,

“Actually, this is for me. If someone other than you becomes the Sado Alliance Leader, I might die of frustration. If someone like you is the Alliance Leader, my happiness level will go up. So don’t worry about it. Just think I’m doing it because I like it.”

That was why he liked him even more. Geom Mugeuk seemed to make a show of every little thing, yet in truth, he was someone who did not enjoy making a show of things.

‘I’ve lost.’

Bi Sa-in admitted it. Even if he had come under him, Bi Sa-in had lost. Once he acknowledged it, his heart felt at ease.

The two of them stood like that on the windy cliff for a long while without speaking.

* * *

The next day, two things had changed.

The attire of the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado guarding him had changed, and among those masked men, one was Geom Mugeuk.

“Isn’t the mask a little uncomfortable? I think it would be better to change the cloth on the inside to something a bit softer.”

At those words, Seventh Wolf nodded as if he agreed, only to avert his gaze at First Wolf’s reproachful look.

First Wolf did not like that Geom Mugeuk had come all the way into the inner court of the Sado Alliance. If it had not been for Bi Sa-in’s protection, he never would have allowed it.

Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sa-in exchanged sound transmissions whenever they had the chance.

—Wouldn’t you be unnecessary inside the alliance?

—I actually think this place is the most dangerous. Isn’t it only natural for killing to take place where one considers oneself safest?

Bi Sa-in looked out the window. Thinking that those trying to kill him were here made the familiar scenery of the Sado Alliance, which had always given him comfort, feel strangely unfamiliar today.

First Wolf said to Bi Sa-in,

“Let us go. There is a meeting with the division leaders.”

As he left his residence, he sent a sound transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—Are you really going to follow me all the way to the meeting place?

—I must.

—When it comes down to it, aren’t you doing this to steal our alliance’s secrets?

—You only realized that now?

Geom Mugeuk truly intended to follow him around all day. If the opponent had not been the Fighting King, he would not have done this. The Fighting King he knew truly loved fighting, and he hated losing as much as he loved it.

The plot he had been carrying out within the Sado Alliance would also be a kind of battle to him.

He would never try to lose this battle, and in order not to lose in the current situation, he had to eliminate Bi Sa-in. Because he was the person most directly threatening him.

Before the fire spread to the Sado Alliance Leader, eliminating Bi Sa-in would be the surest choice for victory.

First Wolf sent a sound transmission to Bi Sa-in.

—Young Alliance Leader, I know you trust the Young Cult Leader, but you must not show him the inner workings of our alliance like this. If the Alliance Leader learns of it later, it will become a problem.

—I know very well what you are worried about, First Wolf. But just this once, trust my judgment.

First Wolf looked at Bi Sa-in. At the very least, he did not feel that Bi Sa-in’s eyes were being swayed by Geom Mugeuk, so First Wolf nodded. He intended to pay even closer attention himself. There could be nothing more foolish than completely trusting a demon.

And so Bi Sa-in attended the meeting.

When placing the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado outside the meeting hall, First Wolf stationed Geom Mugeuk as far away as possible. It was so he would not be able to hear the conversation inside.

Geom Mugeuk obediently followed First Wolf’s instruction.

After the meeting ended and Bi Sa-in came out, he saw Geom Mugeuk standing at the end of the corridor. He could understand what had happened without needing an explanation.

—Please understand. First Wolf is an extremely rigid man.

—I understand.

Bi Sa-in moved as he usually did.

For one meal a day, he always ate at the same inn. Today was no different.

While eating, he sent a sound transmission to First Wolf.

—What happened on Hwang Seok-gyeong’s side?

They had already sent eyes to keep watch on that side as well.

—Nothing is different from usual. If anything, he is undergoing even more intense training than usual.

If he had not trusted Geom Mugeuk, he might have thought Hwang Seok-gyeong had been framed, so normally was he behaving.

Bi Sa-in glanced at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk had been standing near the entrance of the inn, clearly looking outside.

—You are looking at me with suspicion again. I told you to trust me.

—How can I not be suspicious when I have taken a man with eyes in the back of his head as my subordinate?

Geom Mugeuk turned to look at him. The two eyes above the mask were smiling.

—Aren’t you tired?

—I’m tired to death. Earlier, I just roughly had a meal standing up. From now on, I should treat my guards better.

—Come here and eat with me.

—I refuse. I’ll keep suffering and hold it over you for a long, long time. Every time we meet, I’ll talk about this time.

They finished their meal and returned. Nothing happened that day.

Nothing happened the next day either. Hwang Seok-gyeong also went about his days no differently from usual.

And so three days passed.

Today as well, Bi Sa-in was eating at the inn he always visited.

—The Young Cult Leader may have judged wrongly.

Bi Sa-in did not react to First Wolf’s sound transmission. Today too, Geom Mugeuk was standing near the entrance, looking out at the scenery.

Beyond Geom Mugeuk’s shoulder, he could see passersby coming and going, merchants calling for customers, and children running about.

‘Yes, he’s human too. He can’t be perfect.’

After finishing the meal, Bi Sa-in and the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado left the inn.

As they were returning to the alliance, a girl carrying a basket of flowers approached. She was a girl he had not seen before.

“Buy some flowers. I’ll give you flowers cheap.”

The Thirteen Wolves of the Sado became wary of her.

One warrior among the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado went over and examined the girl. From outward appearances, there were no traces of her having learned martial arts. The basket she carried also contained nothing but flowers. She was, in every way, an ordinary flower-selling girl.

When Bi Sa-in passed in front of her, the girl held out a single flower.

He was about to pass by, but Bi Sa-in turned toward the girl.

“How much for one flower?”

“For an honored person, I’ll give it to you for free.”

“Do you know who I am?”

“You’re the Young Alliance Leader, aren’t you? Father told me to be polite if I met you.”

“Where is your father?”

“He’s sick. He’s lying at home.”

“Give me all the flowers you’re selling.”

“Really?”

The girl’s eyes widened in surprise.

“I’ll buy them especially to commemorate our meeting today.”

He was just about to take out money and hand it to her.

“I’ll give you this flower too.”

The girl took out the flower tucked in her hair and was about to put it into the basket.

Tak.

First Wolf swiftly sealed the girl’s paralysis point and snatched away the flower she was holding.

“It is the Yin-Yang Absolute Poison Grass. If the yin poison grass that was stuck in this child’s hair and the yang poison grass in the basket are combined, they instantly emit a fragrance containing deadly poison.”

Everyone looked at the girl.

Before they knew it, the girl’s face had turned pitch-black, and she was collapsing limply.

After examining her, already dead, First Wolf reported with a grim expression.

“It seems they arranged it so that the moment she removed the flower from her hair, she would be poisoned by a lethal toxin.”

Whether of her own will or by someone else’s, it was a vicious killing attempt that had thrown away a life.

Just then, while everyone’s attention was on the girl.

“Watch your back!”

The one who shouted was Geom Mugeuk.

When everyone’s eyes were on the girl, in that fleeting gap, a passerby standing behind Bi Sa-in had launched an attack.

Kwaaaaaang!

Along with an explosion, dozens of steel-needle-like hidden weapons shot out all at once from the cylindrical device in his hand.

Shwish-shwish-shwish-shwish-shwish-shwish-shwish!

Geom Mugeuk crossed the space with Blink Step and swung his sword, deflecting the hidden weapons.

That was not the only attack. A man who had been looking at a peddler’s wares across the street turned and launched hidden weapons. This time too, with an explosion, hidden weapons fired out.

The Thirteen Wolves of the Sado threw themselves forward and swung their swords.

From the rooftop on the opposite side, another man revealed himself and was about to fire a hidden weapon.

Seogeok!

The arm holding the hidden weapon tube was severed and fell.

Geom Mugeuk, who had flown up before anyone knew it, had cut off his arm.

Geom Mugeuk had deflected every hidden weapon that had flown in earlier, killed the attacker as well, and then charged straight onto the roof. His movements had been so fast that those watching had mistaken it for someone else blocking them.

The ambusher whose arm had been severed tried to draw another hidden weapon with his remaining hand, but what he had failed to accomplish with two arms could not be done with one.

Puuuk!

The Black Demon Sword pierced through his neck.

When Geom Mugeuk landed on the ground, all the assassins had already been subdued. The Thirteen Wolves of the Sado had formed a ring around Bi Sain, and in the center of them, Ilrang was shouting.

“Young Alliance Leader!”

A hidden weapon was lodged in his chest. All the hidden weapons had been concentrated on Bi Sain, and because they had come flying in so swiftly from such close range, they had been unable to deflect them all.

“I’m all right.”

When they loosened Bi Sain’s robe, he was wearing protective armor underneath. And truly, not one layer, but two. The hidden weapon had lodged there without piercing through the two layers of armor.

Three of the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado who had blocked the hidden weapons were injured. However, all of them had avoided fatal wounds. They, too, had all been wearing protective armor.

Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sain’s eyes met.

—Well done.

—Because of your nagging, I borrowed protective armor from the Sado Alliance treasury and had the Thirteen Wolves wear it too.

In the end, it had truly been the right choice.

—What about me? You gave it to everyone but me? You should’ve given me one too!

—I figured you’d manage to block things on your own. We were short on armor as well.

—When you’re wearing two layers yourself?

—You told me to wear two layers!

And so the two of them joked around. Even after surviving a brush with death, Geom Mugeuk was still a man who had to joke around.

Soon, Geom Mugeuk put away his playfulness and spoke calmly.

“The flower-selling girl was bait to draw our attention. They meant to attack when our eyes were drawn to her.”

“How did you know that?”

Because the opponent was the Fighting King. If they were people acting under his orders, they would not have been so sloppy.

“I had a bad feeling.”

Bi Sain realized that Geom Mugeuk had been right. The opponent truly intended to kill him.

‘That mad bastard! He really tried to kill me? And right in front of the main alliance?’

If not for Geom Mugeuk just now, he might have been struck down. Even if he himself had survived, the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado would likely have suffered greatly.

The Thirteen Wolves of the Sado knew this as well, so they bowed their heads to Geom Mugeuk in gratitude. Ilrang, who had looked unfavorably upon Geom Mugeuk, was among them.

Bi Sain revised the thought he had earlier. That man was human too, so he could not be perfect? Not at all.

‘This man is perfect. He’s not human.’

Ilrang retrieved the hidden weapons the enemies had used.

“The hidden weapon they used is the Blood Heaven Torrential Rain, designated by the entire murim as a forbidden hidden weapon.”

Not only was it impossible to obtain through ordinary channels, but three Blood Heaven Torrential Rains—things that could only be acquired by paying an enormous sum—had been used.

They had tried not merely to kill him, but to kill him using forbidden hidden weapons? The opponent was someone with not a shred of pride or honor as a martial artist. Someone who cared only about achieving the purpose of slaughter.

Bi Sain looked at Geom Mugeuk with an angry expression.

Geom Mugeuk said nothing, his expression saying he would follow Bi Sain’s decision. Bi Sain did not thank him for saving his life. He intended to settle the debt of gratitude all at once after this matter was over.

“To use forbidden hidden weapons… He lacks even the basics of a martial artist.”

Bi Sain turned his steps toward the alliance. Since they had received the other side’s greeting, it was only right for them to go return one.

“Let us go see whether our Four Alliance Hall teaches the basics properly.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 353

Chapter 353: Your First Sigh.
-----------------------------
Hwang Seokgyeong was surrounded by martial artists.

“I’m grateful for everything you’ve done.”

“If it weren’t for you, Instructor, we wouldn’t have made it.”

“I’ll be sure to visit you again.”

Today was the day this batch finished their training at Samaeng Hall and departed for the various organizations of the Sado Alliance.

Among the many instructors at Samaeng Hall, Hwang Seokgyeong was by far the most popular.

“Do not forget that you are the pillars of the Sado Alliance. I hope you endure well, even when hardship comes!”

“Yes! Instructor!”

As the martial artists left, his colleague, Instructor Cha, came up to him.

“As expected, you’ve received the most gifts again this time.”

Hwang Seokgyeong’s arms were full of gifts from the departing martial artists. Unable to carry them all, piles were stacked beside him as well.

These connections did not end here. Many martial artists who left this place came to find Hwang Seokgyeong whenever they faced difficulties, and each time, he gave them generous advice.

“If I’d met an instructor like you, I might not still be working as an instructor here.”

“What’s wrong with being an instructor?”

“Aren’t you bored? The same words, the same tasks, every time.”

Then Hwang Seokgyeong asked Instructor Cha, “Do you know how many martial artists have passed through our hands?”

“It must be a staggering number.”

“Yes, so many people know us. Aren’t we perhaps the most recognized individuals within the Sado Alliance? Where else is there such great power?”

“What great power? We’re just instructors at Samaeng Hall. If we had risen to become Branch Lords or Sector Lords, that would be different. Who acknowledges us?”

“Why do you think we can’t rise? Is there any law saying we must remain instructors forever?”

“Fine, you go and make a success of yourself. Become a Branch Lord, become a Sector Lord.”

“Become a successor, too.”

“What? Even as a joke, don’t make jokes like that. Well, I’m off. Enjoy your vacation.”

The instructors were on vacation until the next batch arrived.

As soon as Instructor Cha left, a subordinate’s transmission arrived as if it had been waiting. This was the moment Hwang Seokgyeong became Tu Wang.

—They failed.

At his subordinate’s report, Tu Wang’s expression hardened.

—The Young Lord? How badly is he hurt?

—He wasn’t hurt at all.

—Then how many of the Thirteen Wolves of Sado died?

He had assumed it was the result of sacrificing themselves to save the Young Lord.

—None died.

At the incomprehensible report, Tu Wang’s voice gradually rose.

—What expert intervened?

—None. It is said that only the Thirteen Wolves of Sado were present.

—Nonsense!

The Thirteen Wolves of Sado blocked their combined assault? Luck could be a factor. But an assassination employing three Blood Heaven Tempests was something even the heavens couldn’t block.

Tu Wang recalled the previous incident where they had failed to kill Pyo Gigwang and the assassins were annihilated.

—Someone definitely intervened.

Such a result was impossible otherwise.

—What should we do?

Just as he was pondering, an urgent transmission from his subordinate arrived.

—The Young Lord has come.

Tu Wang’s gaze turned toward a man organizing training equipment across the training ground. The subordinate Sahon, with whom he had been exchanging transmissions until moments ago, was precisely that man.

He lived doing odd jobs at Samaeng Hall, appearing to have learned only third-rate martial arts, yet no one had realized his true self.

Beside the bowed Sahon, Bisain revealed himself.

Behind him, walking with pride, the Thirteen Wolves of Sado followed. Bisain exuded a fierce killing intent as intimidating as his appearance.

The following Thirteen Wolves of Sado were all wearing masks, so their expressions couldn’t be discerned.

As they passed, Sahon subtly raised his head to look at their backs. All thirteen—not a single one among them was injured.

He couldn’t understand it even thinking about it himself. How in the world had they blocked that attack?

Right then, one of the Thirteen Wolves of Sado walking at the very back turned around.

Sahon hurriedly lowered his head to avoid that gaze.

When he raised his head again, the Wolf who had turned around was already facing forward and walking on.

‘What? Did he sense my gaze?’

That was impossible. There was no way the Thirteen Wolves of Sado could have felt that covert gaze.

Sahon pulled his cart and quickly left the area. With the Young Lord having arrived, a handyman loitering about was a suspicious sight no matter who saw it.

Tu Wang set down the gifts he had been holding and politely received Bisain.

“What brings the Young Lord here?”

He wore the appearance of an instructor surprised by the Young Lord’s sudden visit. He truly felt like someone completely unrelated to this matter.

While coming here, Bisain had been curious how Hwang Seokgyeong would react. He had thought Tu Wang would flatly deny everything, but he hadn’t expected him to receive him so naturally.

“Instructor Hwang, it has been a while. When was the last time we met?”

“I briefly greeted you at the New Year’s banquet this year.”

“Ah, that’s right.”

Until that moment, he had never dreamed he would reunite with him like this.

You’re really the mastermind behind this incident? You’re the one who gave those people their orders earlier?

“Please, come inside.”

“It’s quite all right. I was passing by and thought I would see your face for a moment.”

Tu Wang’s gaze shifted to the Thirteen Wolves of Sado who had accompanied him.

Geom Mugeuk stood hidden behind the other Wolves, not revealing his own killing intent.

Therefore, Tu Wang failed to notice that a master of Geom Mugeuk’s caliber had joined the Thirteen Wolves of Sado.

‘Truly, not a single injured person?’

He felt an inexplicable sense of crisis, but Tu Wang remained unshaken. Nothing excited him as much as the thrill that danger provided.

‘The Young Lord has come believing I am the mastermind.’

The fact that he had come here after never visiting once in his entire life meant as much.

A probing strike? Or a declaration of war?

‘Was he originally this kind of character?’

The Bisain he had analyzed was not this kind of personality or type.

“What are all these?”

Bisain asked, looking at the gifts on the floor.

“They are from this batch of trainees as they departed.”

Bisain picked up one of them.

“May I open it?”

“Of course.”

Bisain tried to open it, then handed it to Tu Wang.

“It seems dangerous.”

“What do you mean?”

“Wouldn’t a good training instructor have enemies? Might someone not hold a grudge and try to poison you?”

At Bisain’s joke, Tu Wang put on a good-natured smile.

“I don’t think so. Once training ends, there is no one more memorable than the instructor.”

Judging by his current appearance alone, Instructor Hwang was truly a friendly man far removed from conspiracy.

“Are you satisfied with your work as an instructor?”

“I consider it my calling.”

Listening to their conversation, Geom Mugeuk suddenly had this thought:

‘Why did it have to be an instructor?’

There must have been many ways to infiltrate the Sado Alliance and establish a position. With Tu Wang’s skill, he could have risen to a Sector Lord by now. Saying it was merely to remain inconspicuous didn’t fit, as there was no one who didn’t know Instructor Hwang of Samaeng Hall.

While Geom Mugeuk was lost in thought, Bisain and Tu Wang’s conversation continued.

“I was ambushed today.”

“What? An ambush? Are you unharmed?”

Tu Wang was startled.

“Fortunately, I am fine.”

“How dare they!”

“I do not know their identities, but they were ones who used metal hidden weapons.”

“Vile! Shameless scoundrels who know nothing of a martial artist’s honor! They are trash!”

Seeing him call himself trash so calmly, Bisain could confirm that the man before him was no ordinary person.

“Daring to attempt to kill the Young Lord, they must certainly be captured and executed by dismemberment. The Young Lord is the pillar and future of the Sado martial world.”

In truth, Bisain had expected a different kind of encounter. Something suspicious about him, some strange words left behind, some strange aura revealed. A situation where he might shout, “How dare you try to kill me?” He had even worried about what he would say then.

He had thought there would be some special circumstance. Even if there weren’t, he had thought he would find one himself.

But his opponent revealed nothing of his true nature. He was perfectly Instructor Hwang himself. As if to say, “You are mistaken.”

How should one deal with such a person?

Bisain felt it—that he was still lacking. He could only think to say, “It was you, wasn’t it?” but if it were Geom Mugeuk, he would have spoken words to turn the situation to his advantage.

He thought that he shouldn’t have rushed here so emotionally. Because he was not Geom Mugeuk.

If the charge was wrong, he had to at least retreat properly.

Bisain bade Tu Wang farewell with a smile.

“On the day I was showered with assassins’ hidden weapons, our Instructor received a pile of gifts. It makes me think I must live harder.”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk and Bisain stood side by side on a cliff.

Despite Illang’s dissuasion to be careful given the times, Geom Mugeuk had come here alone with him.

Was there power in a place?

Standing here with Geom Mugeuk, Bisain felt he became more honest. Bisain felt that now was the time he needed to be more honest than ever.

“I will never forgive the bastard trying to snatch my rice bowl. Of course him, and everyone involved—not a single one will be left alive. What else would I be if not the Young Lord of the Sado Alliance?”

He meant it. And he wanted to ask Geom Mugeuk.

“I am a more selfish and wicked person than you think. Will you still be my friend? Don’t say later that you didn’t know I was such a wicked bastard.”

Geom Mugeuk knew exactly where Bisain’s distress, pouring out in many words, originated from.

“Do you regret visiting Instructor Hwang?”

After a brief pause, Bisain asked, “If it were you, would you have gone to see him?”

This was what Bisain had wanted to ask.

“Even if I were to go, at least I wouldn’t have gone today.”

“May I ask why?”

“Because decisions made while angry are mostly regretted.”

“I thought as much.”

Anger had boiled over at the fact that they had tried to kill him, driving him to visit Tu Wang, but once he had gone, he regretted it. Nothing had happened; he had simply been swept away by momentum.

“Then why didn’t you stop me?”

“Because you had to experience it firsthand to truly feel it.”

How cold reality was. How even ideals boiling like magma could become mere delusions and drift away in icy water before it.

As Bisain let out a light sigh, Geom Mugeuk smiled and spoke.

“Your first sigh.”

It was not the first sigh shown to Geom Mugeuk. It was the first sigh in front of others. Bisain conspicuously let out another, larger sigh. He showed with that sigh how close they had become.

Geom Mugeuk laughed and comforted him.

“It’s fine. We know about each other, after all. We should at least greet each other properly before fighting. And we learned something from this visit, too.”

“What is it?”

He said it so casually that Bisain thought it was nothing important.

“I learned what his goal is.”

The surprised Bisain nearly blurted out, “What? You crazy bastard!” How could he have learned the bastard’s goal from today’s meeting? Had he secretly stolen and read a diary?

“Did we meet someone else?”

Because he hadn’t noticed anything at all.

Geom Mugeuk pointed out something he had never considered.

“Looking at those piled-up gifts, a doubt arose. Why is he building such a reputation? If one wants to scheme in secret, it is better not to stand out. Would someone plotting such a grand conspiracy go out of his way to expend such effort treating the martial artists at Samaeng Hall well?”

Hearing this, Bisain also found it strange. Anxious that some astonishing conclusion might come from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth, he inwardly grew nervous.

“Over the years, the martial artists he trained must now be beginning to take key positions in the Sado Alliance. There will be squad leaders, and great lords. Those who rose quickly might have become Branch Lords.”

Bisain asked cautiously, “So what is his goal?”

Geom Mugeuk stared straight at Bisain and answered.

“You.”

Bisain couldn’t say anything for a moment. At first, he didn’t know what those words meant. Then, one thought struck his mind.

“Don’t tell me—he aims for the successor’s position?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded and spoke.

“He is trying to become the Lord of the Sado Alliance.”

“!”

He had never imagined that the mastermind behind this incident, that Hwang Seokgyeong, would be aiming to become the Alliance Lord. The distance between an instructor and the Alliance Lord was too vast, even to speak of distance.

“The reason he tried to kill you mercilessly is probably because it is something he would have had to do eventually anyway.”

If his opponent had been anyone other than Geom Mugeuk, he would have exploded in anger, asking what kind of ridiculous nonsense that was.

“Is such a thing possible?”

Bi Sa-in’s voice trembled.

“That must have been the plan originally. I don’t know how many years later it would have been, but he would kill you, the heir, and even assassinate the Sado Alliance Leader. I don’t know by what means. In any case, once the leader’s seat was empty, he would reveal his overwhelming martial prowess and challenge for the position. By then, there would be almost no masters left who could stop him.”

“One cannot become the alliance leader by martial arts alone. The alliance members would not acknowledge him.”

“Which alliance members do you mean? The powerful figures he is currently in league with? Or the martial artists he has produced?”

“!”

“The opinion and atmosphere within the alliance will support him. Not as some mere instructor, but as an instructor respected by all, he will reveal the strength he has hidden and rise as a hero.”

Bi Sa-in’s heart sank.

“He will not be able to kill the Alliance Leader.”

“You know it is not easy to stop a hidden blade, don’t you? Especially if it is a blade that has been honed and bided its time for a very long while. Of course, that is only my conjecture. I could be wrong.”

Bi Sa-in did not think Geom Mugeuk’s conjecture was absurd. The man had already tried to kill him, the Young Alliance Leader, without a shred of hesitation.

Bi Sa-in sank into thought alone for a long while. Geom Mugeuk offered no advice. This was something he had to decide and resolve himself.

At last, Bi Sa-in reached a conclusion.

“I will not ask the Alliance Leader for help.”

He had made up his mind to handle this matter with his own strength.

“If I happen to die, be sure to tell the Alliance Leader the truth behind this matter.”

“I will.”

Bi Sa-in said to Geom Mugeuk,

“At a time like this, aren’t you supposed to say something like, ‘You won’t die’?”

Geom Mugeuk did not give him the words he wanted.

“With this decision, you may die.”

He meant it sincerely. Their opponent was the Battle King. No matter what process they went through, it was a fixed future that, in the final moment, he would have to cross blades with the Battle King.

“Why aren’t you trying to stop me?”

Because overcoming this yourself will make you stronger.

“Because it isn’t my life?”

Bi Sa-in laughed. It was the loudest he had laughed since they had met.

“That laugh just now was good. When you meet a woman, be sure to laugh like that.”

“While spouting that ridiculous nonsense, will you watch to the end and see just how tenacious my life is?”

“Watching a fight is always entertaining.”

“His efforts to take my bowl from me were admirable, but at least when it comes to luck, it seems he cannot match me.”

Bi Sa-in looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Contrary to his words about merely watching, Geom Mugeuk had been fighting alongside him from beginning to end. Wishing for his growth, he waited for him, endured with him, and pushed him so that he could become the protagonist and resolve it himself.

With such luck having come to him, with such a friend beside him, he could not lose to that bastard.

“Let’s go. I don’t know about later, but for now, there are still people who would jump in outrage at the thought of an instructor becoming the alliance leader.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 354

Chapter 354: It Dies Only If Left Alone.
----------------------------------------
When Bi Sain left, his subordinate Sahon approached the Battle King, pulling a cart.

“I will carry your things for you.”

As Sahon loaded the gifts from the martial artists onto the cart, the Battle King sat down on the edge as well.

Thus, as they left the training ground, the two began conversing through sound transmission.

—So the Young Alliance Leader noticed after all?

The Battle King gave a slight nod. He had already expected as much when the assassins failed to kill Pyo Gigwang.

—Then why did he come to see you?

—A declaration of war. He wanted to boast, too. The likes of the assassins you sent won’t work on me.

—He is still young.

A sneer formed on Sahon’s lips. The Battle King, however, still had a question in his mind.

—He has clearly changed from before.

He simply could not pinpoint exactly what it was.

—Have you found out how he survived today’s ambush?

At Sahon’s question, the Battle King shook his head.

—Could the Alliance Leader be helping him?

—It is not the Alliance Leader. If the Alliance Leader had intervened, he would not have handled things in this way.

—Then the Young Alliance Leader will undoubtedly seek help from Goeak and Gwangseom.

Goeak and Gwangseom. They were among the Seven Great Masters of the Sado, experts Bi Sain had once brought along to a gathering of the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions in order to flaunt his power.

—Most likely.

—If we give him time, it will become disadvantageous for us. Before he meets them, I will lead my men and deal with him myself.

—There is no need.

The Battle King lay down among the baggage piled on the cart and said something Sahon could not understand.

—Only if we leave him be will the Young Alliance Leader die.

* * *

After descending from the cliff, Bi Sain immediately sent off a letter, then hired a coachman and carriage. To avoid drawing attention, he left the Thirteen Wolves of Sado behind and headed to his destination with only Geom Mugeuk.

Bi Sain silently gazed at the scenery outside.

He was calming his mind ahead of the great battle to come. He was prepared for death, and this would be more dangerous than any fight he had faced until now.

Of course, part of him wanted to avoid it. In one corner of his heart, a voice kept whispering for him to run to the Sado Alliance Leader.

—Have you already forgotten? You regretted the decision you made earlier in anger, didn’t you? This is the same. Once it passes, you’ll regret this choice. Go now, tell the Alliance Leader, and resolve this matter.

With complicated feelings, Bi Sain looked at Geom Mugeuk.

As always, Geom Mugeuk sat quietly with his eyes closed.

Then Geom Mugeuk suddenly spoke.

“Are you regretting it again?”

“You seem to have eyes on the back of your head, and eyes on your eyelids too.”

“That makes me sound like a monster. Just say I see with my heart.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly opened his eyes.

“Whenever you ride in a carriage, you close your eyes and seem to be reciting something. What exactly are you training?”

“I am training the Heavenly Demon Body Protection Art.”

Bi Sain was inwardly flustered, not having expected him to mention the Heavenly Demon’s exclusive martial art.

“Is it all right to tell me such a secret?”

“What of it? It’s not as if I’m telling you the formula. What’s wrong with telling you the name of a martial art?”

There was a realm that could not be imitated no matter how much one learned of it in words. In the end, a single sentence slipped out before he knew it.

“I lost.”

“I think it is fine to admit it like that.”

“So? You want me to be dragged around by you?”

“What of it? While being dragged around, you can look at the stars, the birds, and the clouds. The person doing the dragging may swagger and act superior at first, but with time, he’ll get tired too. He’ll start thinking, Won’t someone drag me for a while?”

Bi Sain wore an expression that said he was truly impossible.

“You say that so easily, when you’ve never lost to anyone.”

“There is my father, isn’t there? Since he reads everything anyway, I don’t bother trying to hide anything in front of him. Ah, you should see my father laughing at me.”

It was hard to believe there was someone who could drive even this Geom Mugeuk onto the defensive. Then again, since it was Geom Mugeuk’s father, perhaps that was possible.

“What kind of person is your father?”

“You people probably have more information about him than I do.”

At those words, half jest and half truth, Bi Sain smiled faintly.

“I’ll introduce you next time. Meet him in person and judge for yourself.”

Would the day truly come when he greeted the Heavenly Demon?

“You’re getting serious again. It won’t be the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance greeting the Heavenly Demon. You’ll be greeting your friend’s father.”

A friend’s father.

It was a phrase as unfamiliar in his life as the word friend itself.

“By the way, why do you not ask whom we are going to meet?”

Because I already know. Geom Mugeuk had guessed whom Bi Sain was going to ask for help.

“Which of the two are we going to meet first?”

Bi Sain shook his head.

“Do you know that? At times like this, you really seem inhuman.”

After saying it, he felt sorry. Would someone inhuman be helping him like this?

“Let me ask just one more thing.”

“Go ahead.”

“Do you think Senior Goeak and Senior Gwangseom will be insufficient?”

When he had seen Instructor Hwang, his skill had seemed like that of an instructor of the Sado Alliance Academy.

In other words, it meant he possessed strength that overwhelmed Bi Sain.

Geom Mugeuk answered honestly.

“If the two join forces, perhaps. But if either fights him alone, there is absolutely no chance of victory.”

He had expected it to some extent, but Bi Sain could not help being shocked. An opponent who required two of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado to join forces.

Then Geom Mugeuk added something even more astonishing.

“Even the two of them will have a hard time if they are not in harmony.”

* * *

The carriage arrived at the appointed place.

“Young Alliance Leader, welcome.”

“Thank you very much for coming all this way.”

“Gwangseom is waiting inside. Come, let us go in.”

They were two of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado who had deep ties with the Sado Alliance. More precisely, they were followers of the Sado Alliance Leader.

Goeak glanced over and confirmed that there was only one attendant.

“You came quietly.”

He had met Geom Mugeuk before, but he did not recognize him here and now.

Geom Mugeuk was wearing a mask and had lowered his head, avoiding eye contact. On top of that, he revealed only about the same presence as one of the Thirteen Wolves of Sado.

Since he could not possibly imagine that the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult had become one of the Thirteen Wolves of Sado and was guarding Bi Sain, Goeak failed to recognize Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk entered the room with them as well. Since the Thirteen Wolves of Sado always moved with the Young Alliance Leader, no one paid it any mind.

Inside the room, a drinking table had been prepared, and Gwangseom was waiting.

“Senior, thank you for coming.”

Gwangseom was originally a man of few words, so he simply lowered his head slightly in greeting.

As Bi Sain sat at the drinking table, he sent a sound transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—Will you reveal your identity and sit with us?

—I am fine. I will stay here.

Geom Mugeuk stood a short distance behind Bi Sain.

“We were having a drink first while waiting. Come, Young Alliance Leader, have a cup as well.”

They drank and exchanged light conversation. They spoke of how they had been, of the Sado Alliance Leader, and of various incidents that had recently taken place in the martial world.

Then Gwangseom offered wine to Geom Mugeuk as well. Geom Mugeuk turned his head and drank beneath his mask.

Bi Sain was inwardly impressed.

‘The Young Cult Leader has enough skill to conceal his presence even before them.’

After several rounds of wine, the real conversation began.

“So, Young Alliance Leader, why did you ask to see us?”

“There is something I wish to request of you two seniors.”

Bi Sain was cautious.

Even Goeak, who was treating him with a pleasant face now, was an absolute master infamous in the martial world for his eccentric and cruel temperament.

Gwangseom, too, merely seemed less wicked because his nature was taciturn; he was a man whose sword was faster than his words.

The reason they showed him courtesy was out of respect for the Sado Alliance Leader. Bi Sain had not yet built personal friendship or trust with them.

“This time, I discovered a certain hidden force threatening our alliance.”

At those words, the gazes of Goeak and Gwangseom sharpened.

“Who are they?”

“The leader is Hwang Seokgyeong, an instructor at the Sado Alliance Academy.”

When Hwang Seokgyeong’s name was revealed, the two wore baffled expressions. They seemed more surprised by the fact that it was Hwang Seokgyeong than by the existence of a hidden force threatening the Sado Alliance.

“If you know who the enemy is, you can simply eliminate him. Why did you ask to meet us?”

“He is hiding his true strength.”

“To the point that we are needed?”

“Yes. Both of you must go.”

For a moment, silence flowed. Bi Sain conveyed the enemy’s strength without concealing it. He did not make the foolish mistake of downplaying Hwang Seokgyeong’s ability in a pointless attempt to preserve the two men’s face.

Goeak asked, half in doubt.

“Does the Alliance Leader know?”

“He does not yet know.”

“Why have you not told him?”

From here on, persuasion was important.

“To begin with, he became entangled with me. He even tried to kill me.”

“He tried to assassinate you?”

“That is correct. That is why I want to end him with my own hands.”

Goeak stared at Bi Sain.

“You want to handle this matter well and gain the Alliance Leader’s recognition.”

Bi Sain answered this honestly as well.

“That is correct.”

Goeak turned to look at Gwangseom. Gwangseom merely drank in silence.

Goeak did not readily offer to help.

“If what you say is true, this is quite a dangerous matter. Why should we help you?”

Bi Sain did not say that it was an opportunity to render great service to the Sado Alliance, nor that they would be bestowing a favor upon the next Sado Alliance Leader. He did not say that a mere instructor was dreaming of becoming the Alliance Leader.

“I will not speak in circles. If I become the Alliance Leader, I will give you two what you desire.”

“Do you know what I desire?”

“On the day I ascend to the position of Alliance Leader, I will give you the manual for the Slashing Ghost Shadow Step (斬擊鬼影步) kept in the secret vault.”

Light gleamed in Goeak’s eyes.

The Slashing Ghost Shadow Step was a manual Goeak desperately wanted. When practiced together with his exclusive martial art, the Slashing Iron Blade, it produced far stronger effects, but regrettably, he had never learned the Slashing Ghost Shadow Step.

The reason he had done various tasks for the Sado Alliance Leader all this time was precisely to obtain the Slashing Ghost Shadow Step.

It was a double-edged promise. If he accepted this condition, he would help both materially and emotionally, hoping for Bi Sain to ascend as Sado Alliance Leader even a day sooner.

But once the manual was given, Bi Sain would no longer be able to use him.

“What guarantee is there that you will keep your promise?”

“If you wish, I will write it down as a document.”

Only then did Goeak nod.

“Good. I will accept on those terms.”

This time, Bi Sain looked at Gwangseom.

“Senior, I have not yet learned what it is you desire. Please tell me what you want.”

Only then did Gwangseom open his mouth.

“I want your life.”

In that instant, silence fell over the room.

Soon after, Goeak laughed heartily and said,

“This is why one must usually be sparing with words for a joke like this to work.”

Bi Sain laughed along.

But Gwangseom did not laugh. Words he normally never spoke poured out all at once.

“When you went out earlier to greet the Young Alliance Leader, I put energy-dispersing poison in the wine. They said it was specially made by improving Black Rain, and indeed, even you failed to notice it. Unless you possess a body immune to all poisons, you will not be able to use your internal energy for the next half hour. Of course, I took the antidote beforehand.”

At once, Goeak examined his internal energy. His expression hardened coldly. His internal energy truly had scattered.

“Why are you doing this? What kind of joke is this, so unlike you?”

Instead of answering, Gwangseom continued speaking.

“The one who killed the Young Alliance Leader here today will be you. It will be handled as if you killed him and then vanished from the martial world. A large sum will be deposited into your money house account, and circumstantial evidence that you accepted a commission to kill the Young Alliance Leader will be found as well.”

Gwangseom spoke words that were utterly horrifying from Goeak’s position without so much as blinking.

“You mad bastard! You must be dying to get killed. Do you think I will simply suffer it just because I have no internal energy?”

Unlike the confidence in his shout, he despaired. Fighting Gwangseom without internal energy? It would be a fight between a martial artist and a child.

“I apologize for using energy-dispersing poison. It means I did not underestimate your skill, so do not be too angry. I will bury your corpse somewhere that gets plenty of sunlight.”

Bi Sain was just as shocked and flustered. Gwangseom was an opponent he could not handle even with his internal energy, but the problem was that Geom Mugeuk had drunk the wine too.

Now that he thought about it, the one who had given Geom Mugeuk wine was Gwangseom. When that wordless man had gone out of his way to give wine to his subordinate, he should have been suspicious.

“Did you join hands with Hwang Seokgyeong?”

Gwangseom gave no answer, but it was a silence of affirmation.

“Why did you join hands with him?”

Gwangseom looked out the window for a moment, then said quietly,

“I fought him and lost. We had agreed that if I lost, I would become his subordinate.”

Respect could be felt in the word “him.”

Bi Sain could tell, just as Geom Mugeuk had said, that Hwang Seokgyeong was truly strong. It was surely not merely because of a promise. It seemed Gwangseom had been captivated by Hwang Seokgyeong’s strength and had sincerely become his subordinate.

Then Geom Mugeuk, who had been standing behind them and watching everything unfold, finally opened his mouth.

“The human heart truly is impossible to know, isn’t it? Of the two of you, I expected Goeak to be the traitor.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, everyone’s gazes turned toward him. When he suddenly stepped forward, not only Goeak but even Gwangseom wore puzzled expressions. The exchange between the two had not been a conversation between a master and his subordinate.

“Did you anticipate this situation?”

“Hwang Seokgyeong must certainly have anticipated that you would go to meet them. And yet he did not stop you. Two of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado might become his enemies, but he did not stop it? Does that not seem suspicious?”

Hearing it, Bi Sain thought it was true. Why did such thoughts never come to him so readily at the time?

“Then why did you not prepare for it?”

“Prepare how?”

“You should not have drunk the wine.”

“He was watching me like a hawk and trying to make me drink. How could I not? Should I have spat it out in front of him?”

Sssshhhk!

Gwangseom’s swift sword cut through the air.

Thud.

The mask sliced by the sword fell away. Knowing that the strike had not been intended to kill him, Geom Mugeuk did not dodge. In truth, accurately reading the opponent’s intent and not dodging required even greater courage and skill than evading his sword.

With his face revealed, Geom Mugeuk rubbed his chin and mouth as he said,

“I was having a hard time because the mask was uncomfortable anyway, so thank you for cutting it off. I keep saying we really need to change the cloth on the inside.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 355

Chapter 355: Didn't You Say You Could Use Internal Energy?
----------------------------------------------------------
“Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult!”

Gwangseom and Goeak recognized Geom Mugeuk at the same time.

At their first meeting, Geom Mugeuk had left such a powerful impression that he was impossible to forget.

“Why are you here?”

They had never imagined Geom Mugeuk would be here, and they were greatly shocked.

Gwangseom’s and Goeak’s expressions changed in opposite ways. A strand of hope bloomed in Goeak’s heart. A variable had appeared in a situation where death had seemed certain.

Gwangseom, on the other hand, looked utterly dismayed. He had been about to pin the crime on Goeak and kill the Young Alliance Leader.

But now, if things went this way, he would have to kill the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader as well. What he was doing now was already no ordinary matter, but if the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader died too, the murim would be turned upside down.

Burning with anxiety at the unexpected variable, Gwangseom strode over, poured himself a drink, and drank it.

‘How should I handle this?’

He was torn between reporting this to Hwang Seokgyeong at once and handling it on his own. Reporting and waiting for an answer would take time.

The greater problem was that his plan had been exposed to the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader.

Gwangseom looked at Geom Mugeuk. The ease in his eyes seemed to say he was certain Gwangseom could not kill him.

‘Young Cult Leader, this time you’ve chosen the wrong opponent.’

He made his decision on how to deal with this troublesome matter.

Gwangseom looked at Goeak and said,

“At least you won’t have to bear the crime of killing the Young Alliance Leader.”

Guessing Gwangseom’s meaning, Goeak responded coldly.

“You crazy bastard.”

Both he and Gwangseom were villains among villains. He had lived his entire life without trusting people, but he had still believed in Gwangseom, who had spent time at his side. He had liked his taciturn nature, too. He had been deceived by that taciturnity. More than the man who had deceived him, he was furious at himself for being fooled.

His martial art, the Iron Man Slash, was a body technique that fought with the entire body. Without internal energy, he could not even attempt a surprise attack.

What would happen if the Young Cult Leader, the Young Alliance Leader, and he himself all rushed him together? Even without internal energy, couldn’t they somehow manage?

But he knew better than anyone how fast Gwangseom’s sword was. His body technique without internal energy would be little more than a massage, and the two others’ attacks would become children’s swordplay.

The decision Gwangseom made was exactly as Goeak expected.

“Because it will be handled as the Young Cult Leader and the Young Alliance Leader having fought each other and both suffered fatal wounds.”

Geom Mugeuk said to Bi Sain,

“In the end, the thing you were worried about is happening. The two of us fighting and killing each other.”

“You can joke in this situation?”

“If friends die on the same day and at the same hour, that has its own value, does it not?”

Bi Sain was in no state of mind to indulge Geom Mugeuk’s banter.

‘To think I’m going to die like this?’

He had trusted Gwangseom and Goeak. That was why he had drunk the liquor without the slightest suspicion. Since they were people who followed the Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance, he had naturally thought he could trust them. Thinking about it now, they were people who had no real connection to him at all.

Suddenly, he remembered Geom Mugeuk’s words telling him to look at people properly and closely. Yes, this too was ultimately the result of failing to see people properly.

What would happen to the Sado Alliance after he died? What about the Alliance Leader? These were people who had even drawn Gwangseom in, so they would not be easy to deal with.

And the final worry of the man facing death was not himself.

“You must not kill the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader. Even if you kill us, let that man go.”

To think he would ask for someone else to be spared in a situation where he himself was going to die. Bi Sain felt unfamiliar to himself.

“The Young Cult Leader will promise. He will never reveal what happened here. He is a man of the standing of the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader, so he will not lightly break a promise.”

When Gwangseom showed no particular reaction, Bi Sain tried even more desperately to persuade him.

“Try putting yourself in the Young Cult Leader’s position. In any case, these are matters that happened within the Sado Alliance. They have nothing to do with that man, do they? There is no reason he would deliberately damage his own honor by revealing what happened today. If you kill the Young Cult Leader, the Demonic Cult will find you all and kill you.”

Bi Sain turned to Geom Mugeuk and said,

“Isn’t that right? You say it.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said,

“I will tell.”

“What?”

“He’s a man who killed you right before my eyes. I can’t just let that pass. Not only will I tell everything, I’ll take revenge.”

Bi Sain felt suffocated. Young Cult Leader, this is no time to joke. Please, just this once, play along with me.

Hiding those thoughts, Bi Sain calmly persuaded Gwangseom.

“The Young Cult Leader often says things he doesn’t mean. Don’t make this matter larger than it has to be. Let the Young Cult Leader go.”

As he said that, Bi Sain looked at Geom Mugeuk and spoke with his eyes.

—I am not joking. So you must not joke either.

Of course, if he obediently granted that wish, he would not be Geom Mugeuk.

“Of all the times I’ve seen you, you look the coolest right now.”

Please! Bi Sain almost burst into anger, then stopped himself. Yes, it had already come to this. It was not as if Gwangseom would spare him if he begged, or kill him if he did not.

“If a person trying to save me doesn’t look cool, wouldn’t that be a problem in itself?”

After saying that, Bi Sain gave a bitter smile. In the end, even in this moment, he was bantering. Fine. Better to die laughing and bantering with this man. Thinking that, his heart grew lighter.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Gwangseom.

“Look into that man’s eyes.”

Bi Sain also followed Geom Mugeuk’s gaze and looked into Gwangseom’s eyes. They were cold eyes that revealed no emotion.

“He has absolutely no intention of sparing me.”

How could Bi Sain not know that? He simply did not want Geom Mugeuk to die as well.

Bi Sain let out a long sigh.

“I’m sorry. I shouldn’t have dragged you into this.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said,

“That is your second sigh.”

He wanted to show him a tenth sigh, a hundredth sigh. But their relationship was ending with only two sighs shown.

If he was with Geom Mugeuk, he felt as though they could overcome any crisis. Even this time, Geom Mugeuk had pushed him to be the protagonist, but in a corner of his heart, Bi Sain had believed in him.

But he had forgotten. While marveling at Geom Mugeuk’s astonishing abilities and laughing at his banter, he had forgotten that Geom Mugeuk was human too.

“I truly am sorry.”

At the apology that came out like a sigh, Geom Mugeuk smiled.

“There is no need to be sorry.”

Watching this, Goeak said to Gwangseom,

“Even that half-baked friendship that hasn’t amounted to much is so heartfelt. What the hell are we?”

Gwangseom pretended not to hear and said nothing.

This time, Geom Mugeuk spoke to Gwangseom.

“You will become a protagonist who goes down in the history of the murim. After all, you’ll be killing the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader, the Sado Alliance’s Young Alliance Leader, and one of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado Alliance all in one place.”

Then Goeak added a word.

“Make sure the record also says you killed us with Dispersion Poison!”

As if he would drag this out no longer, Gwangseom tried to draw his sword.

Geom Mugeuk quickly asked,

“Let me ask one thing before I die. Where are the children who were kidnapped and taken away?”

“Why do you ask that?”

“That matter is why I got dragged into this, so shouldn’t I at least know before I die?”

But that matter was something even Gwangseom did not know.

“I don’t know.”

“Then who within the Sado Alliance did that person you respect join hands with?”

Geom Mugeuk tried to find out anything he could through him.

But this time as well, Gwangseom shook his head. It seemed he truly did not know.

“You are merely being used as Hwang Seokgyeong’s blade.”

Geom Mugeuk added to Bi Sain,

“Later, this man would have been used as the sword to kill the Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance.”

It seemed entirely possible. By continuing to build trust like this, he would have taken on the role of ambushing the Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance at the decisive moment. If poison was used alongside him, and hidden weapons too, and every possible method available, could they not have assassinated the Alliance Leader? And if even the Fighting King joined in?

What was even more worrying was this. Since the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader had died as well, the relationship between the Demonic Cult and the Sado Alliance would deteriorate rapidly. If misunderstandings piled up, war could even break out. In such a situation, would they be able to properly uncover the truth behind these deaths? Amid all that, those bastards would only seek to profit like fishermen.

Bi Sain conveyed his frustration to Geom Mugeuk.

“I wish you would say this. That in truth, you can use your internal energy. I wish you would say it with a smile, like one of your usual jokes. If only that could let us survive…”

“If we survive?”

“I’ll gather people and dance.”

He was a man who had never danced once in his life. He was the sort of man who thought he would rather die than dance in front of people.

Would he make such an absurd condition only if he truly wanted to live?

He wanted to live. Even if he had to die, he did not want to die like this.

He still had so much to do. He had not yet even sat in the grand preceptor’s chair of the Alliance Leader’s Hall. He had not yet established his own Sado. He had not met the woman he would love. He had not yet drunk with his friend at the tavern in front of his friend’s house… He had not yet even greeted his friend’s father.

Geom Mugeuk looked at Bi Sain, smiled brightly, and said,

“In truth, I can use my internal energy.”

Bi Sain smiled back. His rough, ugly face brightened. Yes, dying while smiling like this suited him and Geom Mugeuk.

‘I was glad to be your friend.’

Drawing his sword, Gwangseom said to Goeak,

“Still, since there was affection between us, I will kill you last.”

“We’ve known each other for twenty years, and we’ve traveled together for seven or eight. What an incredible affection.”

Pretending not to hear, Gwangseom walked toward Bi Sain.

“I bear no ill will toward the Young Alliance Leader.”

Though his heart beat faster, Bi Sain was dignified. Unafraid of death, he calmly gazed at Gwangseom.

‘Heavens, please protect the Sado Alliance!’

Gwangseom’s sword flew in.

Swaaaaaaak!

Clang!

Bi Sain’s eyes flew wide open.

What blocked his view was Geom Mugeuk’s back. In an instant, he had crossed the space and blocked the incoming sword with the Black Demon Sword.

“You!”

There was no leisure to exchange words.

Gwangseom, whose sword was crossed with his, immediately unleashed his unique martial art, the Churoe Three Swords.

From that one move that had stopped him, Gwangseom understood. If he tried any other shallow trick, he would simply die. He poured all of his internal energy into his unique martial art.

‘Even if you have internal energy, the result will not change!’

How could a human avoid falling lightning? At this distance, if the Churoe Three Swords were unleashed, he was confident he could certainly kill Geom Mugeuk.

Flash!

Shiiiiiiik!

It was an attack so fast it could not even be seen. But there were humans who could evade lightning.

‘Even if you dodge one!’

The Churoe Second Sword was unleashed in succession. Sword light flashed, and in an instant, more than ten lines of sword energy poured toward Geom Mugeuk.

Clangclangclangclangclangclangclangclangclang!

But Geom Mugeuk’s Black Demon Sword blocked every attack. If one side was lightning, so was the other.

Gwangseom was shocked and flustered. Until now, only one person had ever blocked the Churoe Second Sword when it was unleashed at this distance.

Yes, since he was the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader, perhaps he could block it. But he would never be able to stop the final Third Sword.

Just as the Churoe Third Sword, which would sweep away everyone here, was about to unfold.

Puk!

Together with the sound of flesh being torn, the sound of swords clashing stopped.

The sudden silence declared that this contest had ended in a single move.

Gwangseom’s gaze slowly lowered toward his own chest.

The Black Demon Sword had pierced through his chest. He could not believe he had missed this attack.

“…You’re faster than me?”

Gwangseom slowly raised his head and looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Those clear, deep eyes belonged to a different person from the one who had been bantering with Bi Sain moments earlier.

Looking in turn into Gwangseom’s eyes as their vitality drained away, Geom Mugeuk said coldly,

“The moment you put Dispersion Poison in the bottle of liquor, everything you had done as a martial artist vanished. Now you are nothing.”

Gwangseom moved his lips as if to say something.

Shiiik! Slash!

He was no exception. The Black Demon Sword, pulled from his chest, cut off his head in a single stroke.

Thud.

It was the moment Gwangseom, one of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado Alliance and a master of the swift sword, died without leaving a single word behind.

With a crisp flick, Geom Mugeuk shook the blood from the Black Demon Sword, turned toward Bi Sain, and grinned.
