“I told you I could use my internal energy, didn’t I?”

Along with the joy of having survived, a shudder ran through Bi Sain’s entire body. It was the moment the gates of hell had opened and closed again.

“How on earth?”

“It seems Dispersion Poison doesn’t agree with my constitution.”

Ah! Just how long did this man intend to keep surprising people like this?

“Just how far do you intend to drag me around?”

“I told you, didn’t I? Let yourself be dragged along comfortably, and look at the stars and the clouds. Someday, a day may come when you think, Those were good times. And when I get tired, you can drag me along then.”

Their gazes met in midair.

Bi Sain did not say thank you. They had agreed to settle all debts of gratitude at the very end. Now, a debt of his life had been added to that gratitude. Could all of this ever truly be settled?

Bi Sain was not the only one surprised. Goeak was staring blankly at Gwangseom’s corpse.

He had been shocked by Geom Mugeuk’s skill. Setting aside the fact that the Dispersion Poison had not worked, he had blocked all of Gwangseom’s attacks at such close range. And then he had killed him with even faster swift-sword technique.

Swift-sword technique was not Geom Mugeuk’s unique martial art, and yet he had defeated Gwangseom with what could only be called the fundamentals.

‘He is far stronger than us!’

He had known the Young Cult Leader was an extraordinary person, but he had never imagined he was this strong.

He cupped his fist respectfully and expressed his gratitude to Geom Mugeuk.

“Young Cult Leader, thanks to you, my life was saved. If you ever need help with anything in the future, contact me.”

“I will. Please take good care of my friend from now on.”

As he said that, he looked at Bi Sain. It looked like a polite request, but it was a warning to Goeak. Do not treat Bi Sain carelessly.

Goeak looked at Bi Sain. The two conversed with their eyes.

—How on earth did you become friends with someone like this?

—I don’t know either. By the time I came to my senses, I had become that man’s friend.

The three of them walked out of the building.

“By the way, when will you keep your promise?”

“What promise?”

“The promise that you would dance in front of people if we survived. I reserve a seat in the front row.”

“When did I ever make such a promise?”

Bi Sain flatly denied it.

“Sorry, but there is a witness.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Goeak.

“You heard the promise the Young Alliance Leader made, didn’t you?”

Goeak looked back and forth between Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sain. It had already been decided whose side he had to take.

“What promise? I never heard anything.”

“You expect to meet the Young Alliance Leader more often than me in life, do you?”

Goeak smiled awkwardly and changed the subject.

“A mere instructor dares aim for the position of Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance? Come, let’s go. We can’t stand to see someone else prosper, after all.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 356

Chapter 356: I Know Who He Is
-----------------------------
The three people inside the carriage were each lost in their own thoughts.

First, Geom Mugeuk had his eyes closed and was cultivating the Heavenly Demon Guardian Art. Cultivating, and cultivating again. He did not let even the smallest scrap of time go to waste.

When one trained in martial arts, there were times when a feeling came. A moment that tickled and stirred a person, as though a flower bud were about to burst open—the feeling just before reaching the next stage. The Heavenly Demon Guardian Art was exactly like that.

‘We’ve gotten quite close, haven’t we? Come just one step closer.’

Whether it knew Geom Mugeuk’s feelings or not, the Heavenly Demon Guardian Art, with Great Completion right before his eyes, remained one step away, tormenting him.

The one who had actually taken a step closer to him was staring out the carriage window, thinking absurd thoughts. Namely, he was imagining himself dancing in front of people.

He hated people who failed to keep their promises more than anything. If he brushed it off as a joke, Geom Mugeuk would probably pester him from time to time, but that did not mean he actually had to force himself to dance.

Even so, he wanted to show Geom Mugeuk that he kept his promises. That no matter how much he disliked something, a promise was a promise.

‘Of all the things I could have said, why did I have to say I’d dance?’

Bi Sain, who looked as though he were grappling with a grave dilemma, was dancing in his imagination. Beside him, Goeak was thinking of Gwangseom and making one vow.

‘I will never let my guard down again.’

Humans were a breed that should never be trusted in the first place. He had lived perfectly well that way, but perhaps because he had grown old, his heart had relaxed for a moment.

That was why he was also worried about Bi Sain. He could sense that Bi Sain trusted Geom Mugeuk.

‘Young Alliance Leader, if you trust people carelessly, you will regret it bitterly.’

He himself had been done in by a man who spoke too little, while Bi Sain was now being done in by a man who spoke too much.

But he did not convey those thoughts to Bi Sain. He was not the sort of person who said such things to anyone. That was simply how he had always been.

Still, he was inwardly paying close attention to the relationship between Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sain. His life debt to Geom Mugeuk was one thing. Even so, he still had ties with the Sado Alliance, and there was also martial art he was meant to receive.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk spoke with his eyes still closed.

“What do you intend to do now?”

Bi Sain’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk. Once again, Geom Mugeuk was asking for his opinion first. In the past, he had accepted it without much thought, but now he understood clearly that asking him first like this was consideration for him.

There had to be a better plan in his head, and yet he was still asking. Because Bi Sain knew well just how intelligent he was, he also knew that this sort of consideration must be all the more difficult for him.

“He tried to kill me, so I will kill him as well.”

All that remained now was a life-and-death battle.

The problem was Hwang Seokgyeong’s skill. Gwangseom, one of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado Alliance, had fought him, lost, and respected him for it.

That did not mean he could ask Geom Mugeuk to kill him. It was not a matter of pride. Since there was no guarantee of victory, he could be putting Geom Mugeuk in danger. That was not what he wanted.

And there was one other reason: whatever the case, this was a matter of the Sado Alliance.

‘How should I resolve this?’

He had met Gwangseom and Goeak to ask for help, but now only Goeak remained. Goeak alone would not be enough.

He thought of the masters within the Sado Alliance who might lend him aid.

But the problem was that among them, there might be people who had joined hands with Hwang Seokgyeong. Since Gwangseom had been recruited, it would no longer be strange if anyone else had been recruited as well.

“However, I want to deal with everyone who has joined hands with him too. I was thinking about how to find them.”

Since he did not want to ask Geom Mugeuk for a solution, he politely asked Goeak.

“Senior, I would like to ask for your wise opinion. Is there a way to ferret them out?”

At a time like this, it would have been nice to present a brilliant plan and show that old ginger was indeed the spiciest, but no particular method came to Goeak’s mind.

“Leave the thinking to the young ones. When it comes time to beat that bastard down, I’ll take the lead.”

Goeak clenched his fist. The power-dispersing poison had already completely disappeared, but that unpleasant feeling remained. He would not let the man who had recruited Gwangseom go. He had lived his life repaying those who harmed him with a hundredfold suffering.

“I will think on it further.”

Bi Sain turned his head back toward the window. His heart felt uneasy and stifled. If they had tried to kill him, they might also attempt to assassinate the Alliance Leader. Of course, the Sado Alliance Leader he knew was not someone who would easily fall to any ambush, but their opponents were no ordinary people either.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk asked Bi Sain as though taking him to task.

“Why do you not ask me for a method?”

Because I feel sorry. Because I just incurred a life debt a moment ago, and I have no face to ask again.

“It seems you do not want to see the stars, nor the clouds.”

It was a joke about not wanting to be dragged along by him.

“If you don’t want to, then never mind. I had a good idea, too.”

Geom Mugeuk closed his eyes again.

Even after waiting for quite some time, Bi Sain did not ask anything, so Geom Mugeuk opened his eyes again. Bi Sain was still silently looking out the window.

“No, I said I had a good idea!”

“I heard you earlier.”

“You’re not going to ask?”

“I’m not going to ask.”

“I want to show off how clever I am. Please give me the chance!”

A faint smile formed at the lips of Bi Sain as he looked outside. This was the kind of person Geom Mugeuk was. It was something he could have claimed credit for, yet instead, he lowered himself. Bi Sain felt it anew. True self-respect did not flaunt itself.

“I refuse! I don’t want to see you pretending to be clever.”

“Just listen this once.”

“Fine. Let us hear it, then.”

“Until now, Hwang Seokgyeong likely has not been meeting those who joined hands with him. He would have assumed you were watching him.”

“Most likely.”

Then Geom Mugeuk made an unexpected assumption.

“What if you had died?”

“!”

Bi Sain was not the only one startled. Goeak, who had been listening with them, was surprised as well.

Bi Sain could tell what Geom Mugeuk meant.

“You are saying we should deceive Hwang Seokgyeong into thinking I am dead?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“If he believes you are dead, he will undoubtedly make contact with his collaborator. They will need to discuss what comes next.”

It was certainly a plausible plan. It was to exploit the moment of carelessness that came when an obstacle disappeared.

“Then should we not inform the Alliance Leader?”

“That is for you to decide.”

Bi Sain fell into thought for a moment.

Informing the Alliance Leader meant that the Alliance Leader would be involved from now on.

He had wanted to resolve it himself until the very end.

But the enemy was so powerful that he also felt the time had come to receive the Alliance Leader’s help.

One concern was that if they acted according to a prearranged plan, a natural reaction might not emerge.

Conversely, what would happen if they proceeded without informing him?

He could not know what sort of reaction would come. In particular, he did not know how the Sado Alliance Leader would act. What if he did not grieve at all even though Bi Sain had died? The human heart was truly ridiculous. Even though this was a matter on which the fate of the Sado Alliance hung, he found himself first worrying that he might be hurt.

“He might appoint a new Young Alliance Leader.”

That would not happen, but the Sado Alliance Leader was the kind of person for whom it might.

“Take this chance to confirm who comes after you.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, half-joking and half-serious, Bi Sain smiled faintly.

After thinking for a while, Bi Sain reached a conclusion.

“I will inform the Alliance Leader and proceed from now on.”

Bi Sain had a separate reason for deciding so.

“If I go missing, Illang will be summoned to the Alliance Leader’s Hall.”

“He will speak about me?”

At that, Bi Sain shook his head.

“It is because the opposite will happen that I cannot allow it. Illang will never speak of you.”

He had faith in Illang’s loyalty. Faith that even if the Sado Alliance Leader asked, he would keep the secret.

“I cannot make that man lie to the Alliance Leader.”

To speak falsely to the Alliance Leader was a grave crime, regardless of the reason.

Geom Mugeuk smiled.

“Your care for your subordinate is admirable.”

“Spare me the needless words, and let me ask one favor. Watch Hwang Seokgyeong. It is my task, but as you can see, I am dead.”

Considering the opponent’s level of martial arts, he knew it was not something just anyone could do, so he asked Geom Mugeuk. He did not want to ask, but he had no choice. Right now, the person he could trust most was Geom Mugeuk.

“Very well. Let us do that.”

What did he think as he watched Geom Mugeuk answer so readily?

He thought that someday, he might have to seat him in the very front and dance once for him. He did not want to become someone who failed to keep a promise to a person like this.

* * *

—Rumors are circulating that the Young Alliance Leader is dead.

Even at Sahon’s astonishing report, the Fighting King showed no agitation.

—The current atmosphere in the Alliance Leader’s Hall is unusual. The ant swarm has been released, and the Captain of the Evil Squad has been summoned inside.

The Blood Ant Army, called the ant swarm, were elites of the Sado Alliance specialized in search and investigation.

The Evil Squad was an organization directly under the Alliance Leader’s Hall, elites who obeyed only the orders of the Sado Alliance Leader.

—Is he truly dead?

The Fighting King slowly nodded. They had said he would die if left alone, and the Young Alliance Leader really had died.

—How on earth did you dispose of him?

It was something Gwangseom had done using the power-dispersing poison. It was a matter that could not fail even if one wanted it to.

—They are investigating the circumstances while keeping the Young Alliance Leader’s death top secret, but since the matter is what it is, the Alliance Leader’s Hall is already in turmoil.

Gwangseom had decided to cut off contact and go into hiding for the time being after finishing this matter, so no matter how much they investigated, they would not be able to learn the truth behind it.

—He should have reported to the Sado Alliance Leader earlier. The Young Alliance Leader made a foolish choice.

The Fighting King did not agree with Sahon’s words. If it were him, he too would have handled it alone. Of course, he would not have died like this. He would not have lost in any fight.

—And he has requested an audience with you.

—In a situation like this?

The Fighting King tilted his head. Given the circumstances, he had expected that no contact to meet would come for the time being. Since he was a cautious person, there was surely a reason he had asked to meet.

—Tell him we will meet tonight.

—Yes, understood.

With the Young Alliance Leader’s death, the situation was entering a new phase.

* * *

At midnight that night, the Fighting King stood in a field far from the main headquarters of the Sado Alliance.

The terrain there was open on all sides, a place where no one could hide and watch.

With his hands clasped behind his back, he was looking up at the moon in the night sky.

A man appeared there. The man, wearing a bamboo hat pulled low, walked slowly over and approached the Fighting King’s side.

“It has been a long time, my lord.”

It was the voice of a middle-aged man. He was addressing the Fighting King as his lord, and from his steady voice alone, one could guess just how composed his nature was.

“There is no one around. You may take it off.”

“I know your martial prowess better than anyone, my lord, but it is still best to be cautious.”

It was an action that went against courtesy, as though he did not trust his lord’s ability, but the Fighting King was not displeased. That was how important this man was.

“For what reason did you ask to see me?”

“To inform you of one important fact.”

An astonishing fact flowed from the man in the bamboo hat.

“The Young Alliance Leader is alive.”

At those unexpected words, the Fighting King’s expression hardened.

“Are you certain?”

“Yes. The Young Alliance Leader personally contacted the Alliance Leader through Illang.”

Astonishingly, the man knew the secrets of the Alliance Leader’s Hall in detail.

Only then did the Fighting King understand why he had asked to see him at this crucial moment.

‘Then that means Gwangseom is dead?’

The Gwangseom he knew would not have been so easily defeated. Moreover, he had even been planning to use the power-dispersing poison.

“Then why is the Young Alliance Leader pretending to be dead?”

Astonishingly, the man in the bamboo hat had accurately grasped Bi Sain’s intent as well.

“He is likely trying to discover my existence behind you, my lord.”

All these results pointed to one fact.

“Whoever it is, an extremely intelligent and powerful master is helping the Young Alliance Leader.”

Then, after the bad news, good news followed.

“I know who that person is.”

Only then did the man remove the bamboo hat he had been wearing, revealing his identity. It was to emphasize that what he was about to say from now on was that important.

Beneath the moonlight, the gazes of the two men met. The man into whom the Fighting King had put the most effort to recruit.

The Fighting King was more intelligent than anyone. But the person standing before him now was far more intelligent than he was.

The answer to how he knew the top secrets of the Alliance Leader’s Hall lay in this man’s identity.

Deputy Strategist of the Sado Alliance, Hyeok Sagun.

Astonishingly, he was the Deputy Strategist of the Sado Alliance. As the Chief Strategist, Hwang Seoseong, had been suffering from senility since two years ago, he, who had been Deputy Strategist, was serving in the role of the Sado Alliance’s Chief Strategist in his stead. Since the illness was worsening by the day, all that remained was for him to soon become the official Chief Strategist.

“The one helping the Young Alliance Leader is……”

The Fighting King had heard many astonishing things today, but the words Hyeok Sagun spoke next were the most astonishing of all.

“Geom Mugeuk, the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.”

Hyeok Sagun knew everything.

But there was just one fact he did not know.

The fact that someone was watching him right now. The fact that, from a distance so far even the Fighting King’s martial realm could not detect it, someone was seeing his face clearly.

From a far-off place where they looked as small as ants, Geom Mugeuk was using the Divine Eye Art.

When the man removed his bamboo hat, he saw his face clearly.

Geom Mugeuk did not know all the facial records of the Sado Alliance’s masters. But he could tell who this person was.

At the very least, he had never thought this person would have joined hands with the Fighting King. He was someone more troublesome to deal with than anyone else within the Sado Alliance.

‘The Fighting King joined hands with Hyeok Sagun?’

It was truly the moment when both Geom Mugeuk’s and Hyeok Sagun’s identities were revealed to each other.

Absolute Regression, Chapter 357

Chapter 357: I'll Kill Those Things and Retire Today.
-----------------------------------------------------
The moment he heard the words "Little Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult," Tu Wang leaped into the air and surveyed his surroundings in every direction.

Only after confirming once more that there was no one around did he descend to his original spot. Just from this reaction, one could tell how much he cared about and feared the Demonic Cult.

"Why the Little Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult?"

It was the appearance of someone Tu Wang had never even imagined.

On the other hand, Hyeok Sagun knew to some extent about the relationship between Jian Wuji and Bisain.

"I understand that the two of them formed a bond while carrying out external activities earlier."

Tu Wang was surprised, but at the same time, all his previous questions were resolved.

The assassins being annihilated, his identity being exposed, Gwangseom failing—when he substituted the Little Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult into all those questions, the answers came easily.

"Yes, that could be so."

Tu Wang had also heard through rumors just how outstanding the current Little Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult was.

"Do you know where they are now?"

"They are hiding in the An Manor near the main alliance."

Since he was fulfilling the role of chief strategist, he naturally knew the location of the An Manor, as well as who had come to stay there.

"Is the Little Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult with them?"

"No, he is currently with Goeak."

The Young Alliance Leader was hiding in the An Manor pretending to be a dead man, while the Little Cult Leader was likely investigating his whereabouts.

"What do you think should be done?"

Hyeok Sagun was the only one whose opinion Tu Wang asked. Tu Wang held his intelligent and coldly rational abilities in high regard.

And more than anything, he knew exactly what Tu Wang wanted. He read his intentions and made plans accordingly.

"Since the Young Alliance Leader wishes to become a dead man, we must kill him, mustn't we."

Moreover, his ability to execute was extraordinary. When something seized his confidence, he handled it first and reported later. He was that sure of himself.

"I have already sent men."

"Goeak is with him as well, so it won't be easy. Whom did you send?"

Hyeok Sagun revealed the names of those who would resolve this situation.

"I sent the Yantian Shuangjian."

They were figures just as unexpected as the Little Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult.

"They were still alive?"

They were beings who had once spread fearsome notoriety throughout the unorthodox martial arts world.

They too had been peak experts belonging to the Seven Great Experts of the Demonic Path, but after committing all manner of evil deeds, they were declared public enemies of the martial arts world, trapped in the Martial Alliance's net of heaven and earth, and reportedly killed by a combined assault.

"They were secretly extracted by the main alliance right before death. Since then, the alliance has been using them in secret. I only learned of it recently, when I took up the position of chief strategist."

He felt confident that if it was the Yantian Shuangjian, they could kill Goeak and Bisain.

"You found out they were in the An Manor and sent the Yantian Shuangjian. Won't your involvement later become a problem?"

Hyeok Sagun turned that worry into idle paranoia.

"Would a busy man like myself have time to orchestrate such things?"

From his meaningful smile, one could tell. That someone else was prepared to take the fall for this matter. That they would eliminate a political rival who was a thorn in their eye together with the target.

Hyeok Sagun's handling of affairs was not perfect only on the Young Alliance Leader's side. The preparations against Jian Wuji were already complete as well.

"The problem is the Little Cult Leader. You must never kill him. If you do, everything we have planned will be ruined. We must send him back to the Divine Cult."

Tu Wang nodded with an expression showing he thought the same.

"Do you have a method in mind?"

"There are two methods. The first is to send him back by force. If we are to defeat him without killing him, my lord, you would have to step forward personally."

"What if he holds out to the end?"

He should be able to win against someone like the Little Cult Leader. The problem was if the Little Cult Leader refused to leave with the Demonic Cult at his back.

"Surely he is not sincerely helping the Young Alliance Leader. He must have some ulterior motive. If he senses his life is in danger, he will withdraw."

"What is the second method?"

"It is to use the Alliance Leader of the Demonic Path to send him back. Right now, the Alliance Leader of the Demonic Path does not know that the Young Alliance Leader maintains such a deep friendship with Jian Wuji. If he learns of it, he will certainly send the Little Cult Leader back. A stern order will fall upon the Young Alliance Leader."

He had deliberately blocked the information. To use it at the decisive moment.

"Which method would you choose?"

"The second method is safer and more certain."

"Then why did you mention the first method first?"

"Because I know it is the method my lord wishes for."

Tu Wang smiled with satisfaction.

"I look forward to our future together."

What he had given him was the position of chief strategist, and what he himself was now to receive was the position of Alliance Leader of the Demonic Path.

Tu Wang had recruited Hyeok Sagun long ago. Without Tu Wang's material and moral support, he would never have risen to the position of vice chief strategist.

There had been a few decisive moments in Hyeok Sagun's life that would determine his fate. He was clever, but how many among the military officers were not clever?

Tu Wang had eliminated people who would become strong competitors in advance. Some died of illness, others in accidents. Because these were carried out gradually over time, they were never discovered.

"Proceed with the second method."

"Yes. I will move the Alliance Leader of the Demonic Path and send the Little Cult Leader back first."

Hyeok Sagun donned his bamboo hat once more.

"Then until we meet again, take care of yourself."

Hyeok Sagun slowly moved his steps and left that place.

Tu Wang, left alone, looked up at the moon again.

"Young Alliance Leader, you will die today as you wished. No, perhaps you are already dead?"

When the moon sets, the sun will rise.

* * *

Bisain and Goeak remained in the An Manor.

Though the night had grown late, the two could not fall asleep. They sat in a small pavilion prepared in the courtyard and drank tea.

On the surface he was composed, but Bisain's mind was filled with thoughts of this matter.

Would Jian Wuji find out who was behind this? What would the Alliance Leader of the Demonic Path think of how he had handled this matter? Just how strong was Hwang Seokgyeong? All sorts of thoughts rose up, complicating his mind.

Perhaps moved by the faint moonlight, Goeak spoke in a carefree tone.

"If I retire, I'd like to live in seclusion in a place like this."

Goeak seemed to like this place. It was difficult to find from outside, while inside it was quiet and tasteful.

"You should buy a house in a place with better scenery."

At that, Goeak shook his head.

"Perhaps because I've lived a life accumulating many grudges, being in a closed-off place like this where no one knows of it feels rather comfortable."

Indeed, this was a place that gave one a sense of safety.

"Young Alliance Leader."

"Yes, Senior."

"Don't forget your promise."

"The day I become the Alliance Leader, I will give you the Samgyeok Gwiyeongbo."

That was the reason Goeak was risking his life to help Bisain. After Bisain answered readily, Goeak finally offered a piece of advice.

"Listen, Young Alliance Leader. Do not be deceived by another's kindness. Everyone harbors dark intentions in their hearts."

It was a warning not to trust Jian Wuji. A life debt is a life debt, but when one must suspect, one should suspect.

How could Bisain not know what he was worried about? Jian Wuji was someone even he himself was worried about.

"I will keep it in mind."

After answering politely, this time Bisain asked:

"There is something I wish to ask you, Senior."

"Speak."

"You have already risen to become one of the Seven Great Experts of the Demonic Path; is there a reason you wish to become stronger?"

He had thought perhaps he dreamed of being the best under heaven, or the best of the Demonic Path, but that was not the case. Anger filled Goeak's eyes as he looked down at his empty teacup.

"Because there are those I wish to kill."

"Who are they?"

It was precisely as Goeak hesitated to answer.

The door leading from the courtyard to the outer courtyard opened, and someone revealed themselves. It was a martial artist who managed the An Manor.

The moment they saw him, Bisain and Goeak sprang to their feet.

The martial artist, reeking of hot blood, collapsed on the spot. His back was drenched scarlet with blood.

Two men walked in over the corpse.

The moment Goeak recognized them, his expression turned cold and stiff.

"Yantian Shuangjian!"

At Goeak's words, Bisain was startled.

The Yantian Shuangjian were those who had once made the martial world tremble in terror.

The Yanluo Jian and the Qianshou Jian.

Together, the two were called the Yantian Shuangjian.

Though similar at a glance, the auras they exuded were completely different.

If the Yanluo Jian gave off a rough and fierce impression, the Qianshou Jian appeared coldly rational and without a single flaw.

What surprised Bisain was not that his opponents were the Yantian Shuangjian.

'How on earth did they find this place?'

That this secret place could be breached was impossible. In other words, it meant the Demonic Path Alliance's confidential intelligence network had been compromised.

The Yantian Shuangjian also recognized Goeak.

"Has it been ten years?"

Goeak answered the Yanluo Jian's question.

"It has been twelve years."

Goeak remembered their last meeting precisely.

"We have an old connection, so junior, you may leave."

Though they were younger in age, they called Goeak junior. Because when Goeak rose to the position of the Seven Great Experts of the Demonic Path, they had already been among the Seven Great Experts long before.

The two of them had taken one spot among the Seven Great Experts under the name Yantian Shuangjian, but each possessed the strength to claim a seat individually. They were not two combined into one.

That was why they were evaluated as the strongest among the Seven Great Experts. If their swords had not dulled, this fight would be hopeless.

Bisain could feel Goeak's conflict. He could tell the Yantian Shuangjian's strength from Goeak's conflict.

'Even Goeak has no confidence in handling them.'

Soon, a ruthless decision was handed down.

"Young Alliance Leader, please understand."

What those words meant was this: I will leave, so you die here.

'How could you be so cowardly?'

He could have lashed out coldly, or begged for his life.

But Bisain did neither. In the past, he surely would have, so why were such words coming out now?

"I am sorry that I cannot hand over the secret manual myself. I hope you safely kill those you wish to kill."

He had never been this kind of person.

Acting cool even in a life-threatening situation? That was something only Jian Wuji would say. Ah, perhaps he had truly been influenced by that man. Jian Wuji could afford such composure and survive magnificently, but he himself would simply die.

Bisain did not feel disappointed or betrayed. Such emotions were only felt toward someone one deeply trusted.

In the past, he would have regretted this moment. He would have thought he should have just reported everything to the Alliance Leader. But even that regret did not come.

'I truly have changed!'

That was why it was a shame. Crises of death kept coming, and each time, he felt regret. He wanted to live longer to feel himself changing. He wanted to show that change to him.

And so he wished again.

Like a lie, he wished that Jian Wuji would be sitting on that wall, smiling and saying this.

"If we survive this time, let us really dance."

But today of all days, such a thing would not happen. He was in a safe An Manor—a place he had no reason to come rushing to.

Goeak looked at Bisain for a moment, then turned his back and walked out.

The Yantian Shuangjian watched him pass by in silence.

The moment Goeak brushed past the two of them.

Shiiiek!

Huaaaang!

Goeak and the Yantian Shuangjian turned simultaneously, unleashing a single sword strike and twin palms at each other.

He had pretended to abandon Bisain and leave, but Goeak knew. He knew the Yantian Shuangjian had let him pass in order to strike him from behind and kill him easily. He knew better than anyone that they were such people.

Kwang!

With an explosive sound, they slid backward.

The Yantian Shuangjian retreated three steps, while Goeak, having faced the internal energy of two people, was pushed back seven steps.

"Your cowardly tricks are still the same."

At Goeak's scorn, the Yanluo Jian also sneered.

"Still as quick-witted as a little rat."

Goeak leaped and landed beside Bisain.

Bisain was surprised and moved that he had not fled but come toward him.

"Why have you come here?"

During the earlier palm exchange, he could have ridden the momentum of being pushed back and simply jumped over the wall to escape.

Then the Yantian Shuangjian would have scattered, one chasing Goeak and the remaining one trying to kill him.

Or they could have let Goeak go and simply finished him off. After all, the target they had to kill today was himself.

Whatever situation unfolded, his chances of survival would have been higher than now. There was no way Goeak didn't know that.

"Earlier, you asked whom I wanted to kill once I grew stronger, didn't you?"

Only then did Bisain understand. That the ones he wanted to kill were precisely the Yantian Shuangjian.

"Then shouldn't you have left all the more?"

Because he must have wanted the Samgyeok Gwiyeongbo since he believed he could not yet win.

Goeak let out a light sigh. It was a valid point. As could be seen from the clash just moments ago, he could not yet defeat them.

The gap in skill between Bisain and them would be even greater. In the end, it was an unwinnable fight.

So why did he stay? He was concerned about letting the Young Alliance Leader of the Demonic Path Alliance die, but there was a greater reason than that.

'This is all because of Gwangseom.'

He had been betrayed by Gwangseom and felt filthy because of it. He had been cursing about it all along, but if he ran away like this, in the end he would be no different from the same kind of person.

If Bisain had spoken cold words or cursed, perhaps he might have fled right after the palm strike exchange.

But seeing him send him off with kind words, he ultimately could not leave. And after advising him not to be deceived by kindness, no less.

"It's because I wanted to receive the secret manual from you personally."

Bisain could not know. Whether a person changed when death was near, or whether Goeak had changed because his own attitude had changed.

He thought that one truly never knows what will happen in life.

"If I had known this would happen, I would have stolen that secret manual and given it to you long ago."

Goeak clenched both fists tightly. An incredible aura, resigned to death, flowed out.

"I don't need it now. I'll kill those bastards and retire from today."

Bisain slowly drew his sword. A sharp, cold energy erupted from his blade.

"On the day of your Jinpen Xishou, I shall prepare the largest and most splendid golden basin in the martial world."

And if only I can survive today… on that day, I shall dance as well.

Absolute Regression, Episode 358.

Chapter 358: If I Get Stabbed Twice, You Get Stabbed Once Too.
--------------------------------------------------------------
Bi Sa-in steeled his resolve and calmed his heart.

‘I can do this. I can win.’

It was an overwhelmingly disadvantageous fight. Goeak had once stood among the Seven Great Masters of the Evil Dao, just like them, so even if he could fight them to a standstill, the problem was Bi Sa-in himself.

He lacked both internal energy and real combat experience. On top of that, those two had spent their entire lives roaming the murim together, while he was coordinating with Goeak for the first time today.

Even so, he was not afraid. He had been that way ever since he had tried to send Goeak off with a good heart. He had changed. And more than anything—

‘I am the heir of the Sado Alliance.’

A fight he would not regret, even in death!

Bi Sa-in’s expression turned solemn. His already fearsome face looked even more terrifying.

“Take the one on the left.”

Goeak had told Bi Sa-in to take the Yeomna Sword. It was not only because the calm Cheonsu Sword would be harder to deal with.

Judging by temperament and personality, the Yeomna Sword would try to toy with Bi Sa-in. If Bi Sa-in held out as long as possible, Goeak intended to kill the Cheonsu Sword somehow and then go help him.

“I won’t be able to last long.”

Bi Sa-in had inherited the Tyrant King Heaven-Shaking Sword Art, the unique martial art of the Sado Alliance Leader.

But the only form he could currently execute was the First Sword, White Heaven Form. Moreover, he had only reached the fourth level of mastery, so he had to land it with absolute precision.

‘There will be only one chance.’

To obtain that chance, Bi Sa-in planned to fight using the unique martial art he had learned before the Tyrant King Heaven-Shaking Sword Art—the Four Soul-Chasing Swords.

As Goeak walked toward the Cheonsu Sword, Bi Sa-in naturally found himself facing the Yeomna Sword.

The murderous aura radiating from the Yeomna Sword was extraordinary. Just looking at him filled Bi Sa-in with the tension of not knowing what kind of strike might come flying at any moment and bury itself in his heart. With every nerve drawn taut, Bi Sa-in asked him,

“I heard you were dead.”

“You brought us back to life.”

“You?”

“Yes. You.”

Bi Sa-in understood. The Sado Alliance had saved them.

“Who sent you here?”

“I told you. You did.”

He could feel hostility toward the Sado Alliance. In the end, these men, too, were nothing more than blades being used by the Sado Alliance. The only difference was that this was not a blade for slaughtering chickens, but one for slaughtering oxen.

The Yeomna Sword drew his sword and lightly launched himself forward.

Clang!

Their swords clashed once. Beyond the crossed blades, Bi Sa-in glared at the Yeomna Sword. They had exchanged only a single move, yet his arm hurt as if it were about to fall off.

‘How much pain are you in?’

But the Yeomna Sword’s expression was calm.

Clang, clang, clang, clang, clang, clang!

In an instant, more than ten exchanges passed between them, and once again their swords came to a standoff.

“As expected, the Young Alliance Leader is the Young Alliance Leader.”

But when the Yeomna Sword saw Bi Sa-in’s arm trembling, leisure flowed through his gaze.

“I never thought the day would come in my life when I would kill the Young Alliance Leader.”

“If you kill me, do you think the Alliance Leader will leave you be?”

“We’re only doing what you ordered us to do. What if he doesn’t leave us be?”

Just who did he mean by “you”? Bi Sa-in was terribly curious about who stood behind this.

“Young Alliance Leader, don’t blame me.”

“Why would I blame you? If I’m stabbed by a blade, I should blame the person holding it. I’ll blame those ‘you’ you spoke of.”

He had said it hoping to make him angry, but the Yeomna Sword was calmer than he looked.

“Fine. Then let us both blame the one holding the blade.”

“Will you yield the first move to me?”

“It is the Young Alliance Leader’s request. I can at least grant that much.”

Bi Sa-in, whose sword had been pressed against his, suddenly retreated and unleashed his technique first. He did not spare his internal energy.

Four Soul-Chasing Swords, First Sword: Soul-Cutting.

From Bi Sa-in’s sword, pure white sword energy rose like a wave, then surged forward in a violent sweep.

Kwaang!

With an explosive roar, the ground shook. Even though the attack was so powerful, the Yeomna Sword sent out his own sword energy and dissolved the incoming strike.

The impact drove them both back, but Bi Sa-in was pushed several steps farther.

“If I had not been lacking in internal energy, I could have faced you well enough.”

At that, the Yeomna Sword snorted and saw through Bi Sa-in’s intent.

“You’ve been making futile efforts for a while now. Do I look like some low-class fool who would fall for such petty provocation?”

“If you were, there would be no need for provocations like this.”

Bi Sa-in treated him courteously. Not because he respected him, but so that he himself would not become agitated.

‘If I let this turn into a chaotic melee, I’m at a disadvantage.’

He had to draw this into a contest of martial skill. Not to mention the Tyrant King Heaven-Shaking Sword Art he had learned, he was certain that the Four Soul-Chasing Swords was also a higher-grade martial art than the swordsmanship the Yeomna Sword had mastered. Somehow, he had to properly make use of the level of this martial art.

“Young Alliance Leader, no matter what trick you use, it will be useless.”

“Say that after you’ve seen all my tricks.”

Four Soul-Chasing Swords, Second Sword: Soul-Suppression.

No fewer than five streams of sword energy whirled left and right as they flew forth. They looked like five living dragons pouncing upon him.

Sword energy poured out from the Yeomna Sword’s blade as well. They were wolves that devoured dragons. The dragons, not yet fully grown, were all eaten by the monstrous, enormous wolves.

The Third Sword, Iron Soul, and the Fourth Sword, Mad Soul, did not work against the Yeomna Sword either.

The Yeomna Sword proved with his skill what kind of position it meant to be one of the Seven Great Masters of the Evil Dao. Conversely, as Bi Sa-in’s impatience grew, the power of his swordsmanship diminished.

“Wait! Let’s rest for just a moment before we continue!”

At Bi Sa-in’s words, the Yeomna Sword laughed at him openly.

“Do you think if you drag this out, Goeak will come help you?”

“I think he might.”

“On the contrary, you’ll have to face both of us, won’t you?”

The two naturally looked toward the battle between Goeak and the Cheonsu Sword.

Goeak’s Iron Man of Chopping and Striking was truly incredible. He dodged and received the Cheonsu Sword’s attacks with his bare body while launching counterattacks.

Swaaaak!

Dodging the flying sword, Goeak’s elbow shot toward the Cheonsu Sword’s chest.

The Cheonsu Sword narrowly avoided it and swung his blade. This time, his sword grazed past Goeak’s neck.

From the tip of Goeak’s fist as it flew in again, astral qi burst forth.

Ppeong!

The Cheonsu Sword twisted his body to evade, and this time the emitted sword energy swept through the place where Goeak had been standing. Goeak did not throw himself far away to dodge. He was doing everything he could not to allow distance to open between them.

It was truly a battle on a knife’s edge. A fight was unfolding in which a single moment’s mistake would cost one’s life.

Bi Sa-in and the Yeomna Sword stood briefly watching that suffocating exchange.

“If you truly trust your friend, then let’s wait until that fight ends. I’ll gladly face both of you.”

But their battle was far too close for that.

“If such petty tricks worked, do you think we would have survived until now?”

The Yeomna Sword thrust his blade and pressed the attack.

Clang, clang, clang, clang!

Internal energy was one thing, but the gap in combat experience was simply too great.

Because the Yeomna Sword had gone through all kinds of battles, it was not easy for Bi Sa-in to seize an opportunity.

Spurt!

Blood sprayed from Bi Sa-in’s arm.

Shiiik!

That instant in which he flinched was the crisis. The Yeomna Sword’s follow-up attack came fiercely.

‘It’s too late to dodge!’

But the next moment, the one who moved aside was the Yeomna Sword. Goeak had come to save Bi Sa-in.

Whoooong!

Charging in with lightning speed, Goeak tried to send the Yeomna Sword flying with his back and shoulder.

It was such a massive attack that the Yeomna Sword withdrew his own strike and dodged.

Ppeong!

But Goeak had to pay the price for leaving his fight with the Cheonsu Sword behind. The flying sword energy struck Goeak squarely in the back.

A groan escaped him, and Goeak coughed up blood. His protective astral qi prevented his body from being cut apart, but his insides were violently shaken by the impact.

Seizing that instant, the Yeomna Sword’s blade flew toward Goeak’s neck.

Claaang!

This time, Bi Sa-in threw himself in and saved Goeak.

Goeak wiped away the blood running from the corner of his mouth.

The Yeomna Sword said to Goeak,

“You weren’t this kind of person, were you? Just what did the Young Alliance Leader promise to give you?”

“When I was promised something by the Young Alliance Leader, I was not this desperate. But now that I’ve decided not to receive anything, I find myself feeling that I must save him.”

The Yeomna Sword and the Cheonsu Sword exchanged looks, their expressions saying they could not understand.

Only Bi Sa-in understood what Goeak had said. Because he was the same.

He had experienced his heart becoming at ease instead when faced with death. That was why he had been able to send Goeak off with good words.

“I thank you in advance. I don’t think I’ll have a chance to say it later.”

At Bi Sa-in’s gratitude, tinged with a sense of defeat, Goeak firmly shook his head.

“Say thank you after we win.”

In the gaze directed at him, Bi Sa-in felt it. Those were not empty words meant to sound pleasant. He truly meant that they should win.

“You’re fighting well right now. Far better than I thought you would. Trust yourself.”

At that moment, a certain flame kindled in Bi Sa-in’s heart. Until just a moment ago, the desire to simply give up had been growing inside him. He had been dominated by the thought that he would unleash White Heaven Form as his final move, and if it did not work, then he would lose.

Suddenly, it occurred to him that he might have set the direction of this fight wrong.

‘What if I don’t aim for a single chance, but truly fight him skill against skill?’

No matter what anyone said, he was the man who had proudly become the heir of the Sado Alliance. There must have been something in him for him to become the heir. Something that would not allow him to die so easily in a place like this.

‘Yes. Let’s not underestimate myself. This is when you’re supposed to bluff!’

Burning away the heart that had been about to give up with that heat, Bi Sa-in charged in.

Dozens of sword lines split the air, and sparks flew each time sword met sword. The shape of the fight was the same as before, but it was clearly a different battle.

Bi Sa-in no longer aimed for one decisive move. Because he had kept that decisive move in mind, he had not been able to pour everything he had into the fight. That difference was enormous.

Blood sprayed from Bi Sa-in’s side.

But in exchange for giving up his side, he cut the Yeomna Sword’s shoulder.

It was the Yeomna Sword’s first wound.

Having seen blood, the Yeomna Sword’s attacks grew even fiercer. But Bi Sa-in was instead pleased. He felt that his opponent’s composure had been shaken.

Bi Sa-in poured in everything he had. If you are one of the Seven Great Masters of the Evil Dao, then I am the heir of the Sado Alliance. If you have experienced every kind of battle, then I learned martial arts directly from the Sado Alliance Leader. If I get stabbed twice, then be prepared to be stabbed once yourself. I am younger than you, so I will endure.

Blood sprayed again from Bi Sa-in’s arm. He was cut badly enough that his leg wavered, and his waist was cut as well. But once out of every two times, it was the Yeomna Sword who spilled blood.

Moreover, what was fortunate was that thanks to the protective armor he wore, he could avoid fatal wounds.

The heat born from Bi Sa-in’s change in thinking was creating a miracle.

Puuuk!

The Yeomna Sword’s arm was deeply cut.

The next moment, Goeak shouted,

“Careful!”

Bi Sa-in threw himself aside and avoided the incoming attack. The one who had attacked was, astonishingly, the Cheonsu Sword.

Judging that the Yeomna Sword was in danger, he had intervened in this fight. He had to pay the price as well.

Ppeok!

Goeak’s shoulder, charging in after him, struck his torso precisely.

The Cheonsu Sword rolled across the ground, and the Yeomna Sword swung his blade as he retreated.

Bi Sa-in was the one who was surprised instead.

‘He came to save the Yeomna Sword!’

That meant he was fighting that well. Bi Sa-in understood. The other three, except for himself, were all paying attention to the battle on the other side. Because they could not afford for the other to die. If one side collapsed, it would become a two-on-one fight.

On the other hand, he alone had plunged into this fight like a madman. His opponent could only display eighty percent, while he was displaying one hundred and twenty.

Goeak and the Cheonsu Sword clashed again, and Bi Sa-in began fighting the Yeomna Sword once more.

They fought madly like that, but unfortunately, the heat began to cool. Little by little, he began to be pushed back. One hundred exchanges, two hundred, three hundred… In the end, the more time passed in a fight, the more advantageous it became for the side with greater internal energy and more experience.

Bi Sa-in felt regret. He had thought the gap in skill between them would be vast, but once he actually fought him, it was not that great. That was why he regretted it.

He was not dying to an enemy he could do nothing against. He regretted dying to an enemy he could have killed if only he had met him a little later.

There was a limit to enduring with youth and venom. He had lost too much blood, and his movements gradually slowed.

Thus Bi Sa-in was driven into the final corner. As he leaned against the wall, panting for breath, the Yeomna Sword slowly approached him.

‘So this is the end.’

He had only enough strength left to thrust his sword once.

Was it because he had shed too much blood?

As if the lantern of his life were passing before his eyes in the face of death, the movements around him appeared slow.

Even in those slowly moving figures, he could feel how fiercely Goeak and the Cheonsu Sword were fighting. Goeak’s own battle was too urgent for him to come help.

As the Yeomna Sword walked toward him, he said something, but Bi Sa-in could not hear what it was. It was merely the sight of his mouth opening and closing.

And then he began to see things.

At first, it was a dot embedded in the distant sky. Everything else was moving slowly, but only that dot moved quickly. The dot grew larger and larger as it approached.

He read the certainty of victory on the Yeomna Sword’s face.

Even amid that, the dot visible over his shoulder was growing larger and larger.

The Yeomna Sword thrust his blade.

Bi Sa-in could clearly see the sword flying toward his neck. The problem was that his own movements were moving slowly as well.

With the last of his strength, he thrust his sword too.

Two swords flying toward each other.

Even in the midst of that, the dot he had seen earlier was moving several times faster than he and the Yeomna Sword. Was it a bird flying in? Or a stone? Could it possibly be a person?

In the distance, he saw a wall collapse. Whatever had fallen from the sky kicked off the wall and launched itself upward again.

Dust from the wall, shattered by that fierce leap, slowly bloomed in all directions, but the thing that had broken the wall was already out of sight.

Meanwhile, the Yeomna Sword’s blade had reached his neck.

At the very moment the cold sword touched Bi Sa-in’s throat, someone dropped down and seized the Yeomna Sword’s arm tightly from behind.

The sword stopped, unable to go any farther.

At that very instant, time moved as it should again.

Shiiiiik!

Puaaaak!

Bi Sa-in’s sword did not stop, but extended straight forward, piercing through the Yeomna Sword’s flesh, bones, and heart.

It had not been an illusion. The sensation in his hand was real. He saw the Yeomna Sword slowly collapsing.

And standing behind him was one man.

It was Geom Mugeuk, drenched in sweat and breathing hard.

He must have moved so fast that the wall collapsing in the distance only now fell to the ground.

As his eyes slowly closed, Bi Sa-in no longer had the strength to stand. Leaning against Geom Mugeuk’s body as if collapsing into him, he spoke with the last of his strength.

“…Please save Senior Goe.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 359

Chapter 359: The Best Young Alliance Leader I’ve Ever Seen
----------------------------------------------------------
Before the fight, the Thousand-Hand Sword had one thing he wanted to know.

“From what I heard earlier, it sounded as if you had some grudge against us?”

No matter how far back he searched his memory, there was no debt of blood between them and Goeak. It was not as if it involved someone else—how could he fail to remember an affair with Goeak?

Goeak did not tell them why he meant to kill them. The Thousand-Hand Sword would not understand, and in fact would surely laugh at him.

There had been a woman Goeak had kept in his heart. She was a widow who raised a child while running a small restaurant, and he would sometimes stop by to eat there.

There was no special relationship between them. They were merely the owner of a restaurant and a customer.

Strangely, he was drawn to her. If he had set his mind to it, he could have taken her, but he did not. Simply watching the woman and her child put his heart at ease.

To him, who had lived wickedly all his life, she and that place were a kind of refuge.

Then that incident occurred.

While the Scorching Heaven Twin Swords were fleeing after being branded public enemies of the martial world, the guests in the restaurant were slaughtered. She and her child died with them.

Though he had no connection to her, Goeak resolved that he would kill the Scorching Heaven Twin Swords and avenge her.

Even now, he did not know what that feeling had been. Whether it had been for her sake, or because he could not stand the sight of the two of them swaggering about as if they were among the Seven Great Masters of the Evil Path.

Later, he heard that the Scorching Heaven Twin Swords had been trapped in a heaven-and-earth net and killed.

But Goeak did not believe it. They were not men who would die so easily.

And in the end, seeing how the heavens had brought them here and set them before him, perhaps she truly had been someone who held meaning in his life.

Goeak explained this revenge as follows.

“Have we ever killed people for some grand reason?”

The Thousand-Hand Sword laughed. It was a laugh in which his mouth and eyes seemed to move apart.

“True enough.”

The fight between Goeak and the Thousand-Hand Sword began.

Goeak did not fear swords, but the Thousand-Hand Sword’s blade was terrifying. Just as rock became tofu before his fists, lumps of iron became radishes before the Thousand-Hand Sword’s sword.

The Thousand-Hand Sword felt the same. To think this man dared rampage barehanded before his sword? He would hack him to pieces in an instant and kill him without leaving even a shape behind.

But if that opponent was Goeak, he himself might end up looking like a fool rampaging about with nothing but faith in a single sword.

They knew each other too well. They knew what level the Seven Great Masters of the Evil Path possessed.

The two men’s skills were evenly matched.

It was a clash between body arts that had reached the extreme and swordsmanship that had reached the extreme.

Amid dazzling techniques there were deceptions, and within light movements were hidden ingenious killing blows. They had roamed and fought across the Central Plains all their lives, and their skills were as outstanding as the infamy they had built.

In the end, this fight would come down to how well they controlled their internal energy, and who made the first mistake. Even the smallest mistake meant death.

All this splendor would merely be the process, and in the end, the final divide between life and death would be decided by a single simple technique.

Even as they fought, both men were paying attention to the battle between Bi Sain and the Yama Sword.

Goeak wanted somehow to kill the Thousand-Hand Sword quickly and save Bi Sain, but things did not go as he wished.

The one relief was that Bi Sain was fighting far better than expected.

‘Hold out just a little longer.’

Just as the fight was hurtling toward its climax.

When the two men briefly separated and put some distance between them, the Thousand-Hand Sword saw Goeak’s gaze turn toward the sky behind him.

At that moment, the Thousand-Hand Sword read the words formed by Goeak’s slightly moving lips.

He’s coming.

Yes, Goeak had clearly muttered, He’s coming.

It might have been a trick meant to divert his attention and launch a surprise attack, so he could not turn around to look at the sky. Who was coming?

What made him even more uneasy was that the joy blooming on Goeak’s face was not something that could be deliberately faked.

And then, faintly, he heard an alien sound of wind.

Shiiiiiiiiiiii.

It was a sound he had never heard before. Something really was coming. It was a sound from such a great distance that he never would have sensed it had Goeak not told him.

But he had no time to look back. Goeak had begun pouring out the last of his strength and driving him hard.

In a battle between masters like them, overexertion always demanded a price.

And yet Goeak was recklessly unleashing attacks. As if he meant to keep him from turning around. As if he were a man convinced that the fight was now over.

What on earth was coming? Was it the grim reaper himself?

In the midst of that fierce battle, he heard it.

The sound of the wall behind him collapsing.

‘Someone really has come.’

He saw someone who had flown in step on the wall and leap, then rush toward the Yama Sword. He wanted to go help, but he could not. By the time he recognized that fact, the other party had already seized the Yama Sword’s arm.

Puuuuk!

The moment he saw Bi Sain’s sword pierce the Yama Sword’s heart!

The Thousand-Hand Sword’s concentration shattered.

That fleeting instant was also the moment Goeak had been waiting for throughout the entire fight.

Bang!

Goeak’s elbow slammed into the Thousand-Hand Sword’s jaw.

Even through a shock that felt as if his soul would leave his body, the Thousand-Hand Sword forced himself to regain his senses.

As he swung his sword and defended against the second attack, Goeak’s figure vanished from sight.

‘No!’

The Thousand-Hand Sword despaired. For masters at their level, losing sight of an opponent was no different from letting go of the thread of life.

Craaaaaack.

With the sound of bones breaking, the Thousand-Hand Sword’s neck spun all the way around. He saw Goeak’s face behind him, and then the scenery before him returned to where it had been.

It had happened so quickly that he did not realize his neck bones had been smashed. He saw the Yama Sword fallen far away, and he was able to confirm what it was that Goeak had said was coming. The moment he realized it was a young man—

As if a gust of wind had blown out the flame of a lamp, the world went dark in an instant.

The light vanished from the eyes of the Thousand-Hand Sword, whose neck had been broken, and soon he collapsed limply like a bundle of straw.

“Haaaaaa.”

A long breath leaked from Goeak’s mouth.

Looking down at the Thousand-Hand Sword’s corpse, Goeak recalled the woman from long ago. The woman who had smiled brightly as she told him she had given him an extra-large portion.

Thinking on it in this moment, he realized that he had liked her.

‘The truth is, that was too much food for me. Rest in peace now.’

He now said the words he had not been able to say to her back then. In this world, there was such a thing as revenge for someone whose hand one had never even held.

Goeak raised his head and looked toward Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk was pressing Bi Sain’s acupoints to staunch the bleeding from his wounds after he had lost consciousness.

“Did you hear what the Young Alliance Leader said?”

“What did he say?”

“Beaten half to death, he said this: Please save Senior Goe.”

Goeak’s eyes trembled slightly.

“What nonsense is that from someone who was busy beating his opponent to a pulp?”

“It looked like you were in danger.”

“I told you, I wasn’t!”

Exhausted, Goeak also sank down where he stood. He had been cut in several places and had suffered internal injuries as well.

On top of that, his mental strength had been severely drained, and when the tension left him, sleep came surging in.

“What nonsense to say to someone who was winning just fine…”

Along with his head dropping, his bravado also fell asleep.

* * *

When Bi Sain opened his eyes, he was lying on a bed.

An unfamiliar ceiling, and the sharp scent of medicine stinging his nose.

‘I’m alive.’

At the fact that he was alive, an inexpressibly joyful relief surged through him. This time, he had truly thought he was going to die.

He recalled Geom Mugeuk looking at him at the very end. The sight of him, weary and gasping for breath. That relieved look in his eyes.

‘Anyone who saw you would think you were the one who fought.’

Bi Sain examined his body. Bandages were wrapped all over him, and the smell of Golden Wound Medicine was overpowering. Just how many places had he been cut? He remembered more than six.

Without thinking, he tried to raise himself, then cried out and lay back down. His entire body hurt as if someone had beaten him all night.

At that moment, Bi Sain’s eyes fell on two sets of protective armor propped against the wall.

Seeing them soaked in blood made him realize anew how fierce the battle had been. Toward the end, he could not even remember how he had fought.

‘Ah! What about Senior Goe?’

Worried about Goeak, he could not remain lying down.

Just as he was groaning and trying to raise himself again, Geom Mugeuk’s voice came from outside.

“Where do you keep trying to wander off to when your body hurts?”

Then came a welcome reply.

“I told you, I’m fine.”

“I told you, you’re not fine. If you don’t get proper treatment at times like this, your bones will ache when you’re old.”

“I’m already old enough, and they already ache enough!”

Bi Sain lay back down on the bed. Before he knew it, a smile had formed at the corners of his mouth. He was injured, and a formidable enemy still remained, but at least in this moment, his heart grew at ease.

When he fell asleep again and woke, Geom Mugeuk was in the room.

“You’re awake?”

“How long did I sleep?”

“Almost a full day. Aren’t you hungry?”

“I’m fine.”

When one felt too good, there were times one did not even notice hunger. This was exactly such a moment.

“But where is this?”

“A safe house on our side.”

Living long enough, he had even ended up visiting a safe house of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“Help me sit up.”

Geom Mugeuk laid Bi Sain back down as he tried to rise.

“Are people of the evil factions always this impatient? I think you’re even more hot-tempered than the demons of our cult.”

“That doesn’t sound like something someone who flew in so urgently yesterday should say.”

He postponed saying thank you yet again. He now felt that settling the debt of gratitude could not be done in a single day. He would have no choice but to live on and take out the gratitude in that ledger basket one by one.

Geom Mugeuk threw the window wide open.

“The weather is truly nice again today.”

Bi Sain looked at Geom Mugeuk’s back as he gazed out the window.

For some reason, when he was not speaking, he felt lonely. Especially when one looked at his back as he gazed somewhere.

“How did you know to come?”

Geom Mugeuk answered with his back still turned.

“I ran over because I missed you.”

“Stop saying useless things.”

The moment Geom Mugeuk confirmed that the person who had joined hands with the Fighting King was Hyeok Sagun, the one serving as chief strategist, he had rushed toward the safe house where Bi Sain was staying with swift footwork.

Hyeok Sagun would know that Bi Sain was alive, and he would also know the location of the safe house.

Had it not been for that instantaneous decision, Bi Sain and Goeak would have died.

“The person who joined hands with Hwang Seokgyeong was…”

Bi Sain tensed. There were not many people who knew the location of the safe house and could send the Scorching Heaven Twin Swords.

Geom Mugeuk turned toward Bi Sain and said,

“Hyeok Sagun.”

“!”

Bi Sain was shocked. He had thought that it would have to be someone of that caliber for it to make sense, but he had not imagined it would truly be Hyeok Sagun.

“Are you certain?”

“I confirmed it myself.”

Bi Sain’s chest tightened, and he could no longer remain lying down. Enduring the pain, he forced himself upright.

This time, Geom Mugeuk did not stop him. When he extended his hand, an energy as soft as cotton flowed out and helped Bi Sain sit up.

Bi Sain leaned against the wall and sank into thought for a moment.

He recalled the way Hyeok Sagun had smiled at him. Whenever he saw that young man, he had felt strangely good. It was because he had felt that once Hyeok Sagun officially became chief strategist, the Evil Path Alliance would be transformed into something new.

But all of that had been pretense and lies?

Anxiety came before anger. Hyeok Sagun was a more troublesome enemy than any other foe of the Evil Path Alliance. After all, he was closer to the Alliance Leader of the Evil Path than anyone else.

“Are you worried?”

Bi Sain nodded. They would have to face Hwang Seokgyeong’s martial arts and Hyeok Sagun’s brain.

“I survived because I received your help again.”

“No. This time, you survived on your own.”

“What do you mean?”

“You met an enemy stronger than you and endured to the very end. All I did was grab that man’s arm once. You were the one who killed him.”

Geom Mugeuk gave all the credit to Bi Sain. Another piece of gratitude went back into the ledger basket.

“You fought truly well. Among all the Young Alliance Leaders I’ve seen, you are the best.”

A smile formed on Bi Sain’s rough face.

“You’ve only ever seen one Young Alliance Leader.”

This time, Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly. Seeing that smile of Geom Mugeuk’s made it truly feel as if he had survived.

Just then, Goeak entered the room.

Bi Sain tried to offer a fist-and-palm salute as best he could while sitting, only to groan. Now that he thought about it, both his arms had been cut.

“It’s fine. Stay as you are.”

“Senior, are you all right?”

Goeak, too, reeked of medicine and had bandages wrapped here and there.

“I’m fine.”

He was grateful that the man was alive. If he alone had survived and the man had died, he would have spent the rest of his life thinking of him and feeling guilty.

“I am alive like this because you saved me, Senior.”

“How could that be because of me?”

As he said that, Goeak looked at Geom Mugeuk. Because Geom Mugeuk had arrived in time, both he and Bi Sain had been able to live. He had already owed him his life twice.

When he tried to thank him, he felt pointlessly contrary.

“When can I leave? Demon Cult food really doesn’t suit my palate.”

He was uncomfortable staying in one of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s safe houses. Before meeting Geom Mugeuk, he had always attached the word “damned” in front of “Demon Cult.”

“As for me, my business is finished now, so wouldn’t it be all right for me to go?”

He had killed those who needed killing, so there was no need to covet the secret manual.

“I’m thinking of living in seclusion somewhere with good mountains and clear water.”

At that, Bi Sain said to him.

“Please postpone that retirement.”

“Why? Have you decided it’s a waste to buy me the biggest golden basin in the world?”

“How could that be? Before you put those hands into the basin, I would like to borrow them for a while. You have already saved me once, so please take responsibility for this life of mine from now on as well.”

Having lived as a martial artist, Geom Mugeuk knew better than anyone how significant it was to overcome a crisis of death together. And so he knew just how important this moment was for these two.

Bi Sain bowed his head politely and asked him.

“Please help me become a good Alliance Leader.”

Bi Sain did not raise his head. There was a determination in him to remain bowed like this unless permission was granted.

“I have many enemies and many flaws. If you keep me at your side, there will be a great deal of opposition.”

“I will tell them all to get lost.”

A faint smile formed at the corners of Goeak’s mouth.

“That may be how you feel now.”

Goeak did not accept easily. After looking for a moment at Bi Sain, who had his head bowed, Goeak glanced at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk said to him.

“I’ll pay you twice what the Young Alliance Leader offers. Come to our side.”

Bi Sain, who had been keeping his head bowed, let out a hollow laugh. Geom Mugeuk was the sort of person who could not resist joking even in a situation like this.

“Your food doesn’t suit my palate.”

After refusing in a single sentence, Goeak went over to Bi Sain and helped him straighten up from his bow.

“There’s no need to keep me at your side out of consideration for me. On the contrary, I’ll be able to help you properly only by taking a step back.”

It meant he would help quietly without stepping to the front. It was also his resolve not to swagger about under the authority of the Alliance Leader.

“Thank you. Truly, thank you.”

“The biggest and best golden basin. Don’t forget.”

It was the moment Bi Sain, who had depended only on the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance, gained an ally more reliable than anyone.

Geom Mugeuk congratulated the two of them with his gaze. Naturally, a few people he missed came to mind.

Just then, a demon practitioner managing the safe house came and delivered a letter.

“It is an urgent letter sent from the Sado Alliance to the Young Cult Leader through Tongcheongak.”

Geom Mugeuk checked the contents of the letter.

“What does it say?”

At Bi Sain’s tense question, Geom Mugeuk answered with an expression that said what was finally meant to come had come.

“Your Alliance Leader wants to see me.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 360

Chapter 360: The Young Alliance Leader Was Bewitched
----------------------------------------------------
“It’s a trap.”

Bisain’s expression grew cold. The opponent he resented even more than the Flame Heaven Twin Swords, who had tried to kill him, was none other than Strategist Hyeok.

“This is a trap devised by Strategist Hyeok. To lure you in and kill you.”

Geom Mugeuk thought that Bisain had seen correctly, but had gotten one thing wrong.

“It is indeed a trap, but it is not meant to kill me. They wouldn’t try to turn the main cult into an enemy at a time like this. Right now, they are hell-bent on killing you.”

Tu Wang was a clever man, and Strategist Hyeok moved beneath him. Precisely because he was clever, there were parts that were easy to predict. At the very least, it wasn’t a ploy to kill him.

“They are likely trying to send me back to the main cult.”

Having separated him from Bisain, their intention was to eventually kill Bisain.

“Will you still meet the Alliance Lord, knowing that?”

“I must meet him.”

“The Alliance Lord will not be fond of you.”

Bisain’s expression darkened. He had already been worried about how the Alliance Lord would react upon learning of this matter. But now, to think that Strategist Hyeok, who attended the Alliance Lord’s side, was the traitor.

“He will have sowed discord between the Alliance Lord and me to the point of no return.”

What exactly had Strategist Hyeok said about him? He didn’t even want to think about it.

But Geom Mugeuk was guessing differently.

“He wouldn’t have done that.”

Geom Mugeuk had anticipated something Bisain had not considered.

“On the contrary, he will have shown an attitude of caring for you the entire time. That you were getting close to me to dig something up—Strategist Hyeok will have kept taking your side.”

The words that snapped Bisain, who was blinking in confusion, awake.

“Because only then would your calling him a traitor produce the proper backlash.”

The moment he heard those words, Bisain felt something strike him deeply.

“Instead, he will not believe me.”

“He will think I have been bewitched by the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult and am framing the Chief Strategist.”

A chill ran down Bisain’s spine. The more he heard, the more it seemed that was exactly how it would go. The thoughts of clever people always surpassed his expectations like this.

“Praise can sometimes be a more powerful form of discord.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, not only Bisain but also Goeak, who had been listening, let out a sigh of admiration.

‘What a terrifying person has become the Young Cult Leader.’

It was said that the current Heavenly Demon possessed talent that ranked among the greatest in history. Could even that Heavenly Demon have shown such bearing at a young age? He was certain the answer was no.

“What do you intend to do?”

“I must meet the Alliance Lord. Right now, I am the only one who knows that Strategist Hyeok has joined hands with hidden forces. My testimony is the sole evidence. If I cannot convince the Alliance Lord, not only your life but even your position as Young Alliance Lord will be in danger.”

Having barely survived, another crisis was now upon them.

Geom Mugeuk asked Bisain.

“Can you deliver a letter to the Alliance Lord without Strategist Hyeok’s knowledge?”

After a moment of thought, Bisain mentioned a person.

“I can deliver it through Illang.”

He was the person Bisain trusted most.

“You must not be discovered.”

“You can trust him.”

Geom Mugeuk immediately wrote two letters.

One was a reply to the letter that had arrived moments ago from the Four Paths Alliance. It was a response stating that he would personally visit the main bastion of the Four Paths Alliance to meet the Alliance Lord.

He gave that letter to a rider of the An Estate and told him to send it to the Four Paths Alliance.

The other letter was one to be delivered secretly to the Alliance Lord.

“You must deliver this letter to the Alliance Lord without Strategist Hyeok’s knowledge.”

“What does this one say?”

“I told him to meet with me alone outside instead of at the Four Paths Alliance. I wrote that if he refuses, I’ll kill you.”

“You… really!”

“A jest, a jest.”

“Even in jest, do not say such things. If you play around with the Alliance Lord as you do with me, you could end up dead.”

“I shall keep that in mind.”

Not that such a thing would happen, but if he failed to handle it properly, the Alliance Lord wouldn’t believe him. The Alliance Lord would try to send him back to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult to separate him from Bisain. Should that come to pass, Bisain’s situation would grow difficult in many ways.

“Do you have a plan?”

At Bisain’s worried question, Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“I shall give it thought from now on.”

About to ask what that meant, Bisain suddenly realized.

Were it him, he would still be worrying over whether to meet or not.

But Geom Mugeuk had already decided to meet and was now worrying over how to do it. It was precisely this that made him keep trusting him. It felt as if every plan was already in place.

Geom Mugeuk approached the bedside.

“Are you alright?”

He could tell it was not a question about his injured body. He must be asking if his heart was alright amidst this chaos.

What would have become of him if not for Geom Mugeuk? Could he have endured? As a martial artist, as the successor to the Four Paths Alliance, he didn’t want to say this but—

‘It’s alright because you are here.’

These were Bisain’s innermost thoughts, words he couldn’t bring himself to speak.

‘The Alliance Lord may not believe me. He will likely think he is being deceived by you. He may scold me for being a fool.’

Anyone in the Four Paths Alliance would think so. They wouldn’t think the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult approached with good intentions. That was why this battle was difficult.

‘Even so, I will not doubt you.’

Because he couldn’t move forward while harboring doubt. This doubt wouldn’t be of Geom Mugeuk, but ultimately of himself. It would lead to self-reproach for failing to see a person for who they truly were.

It was because of this that Bisain could answer thus.

“I am fine.”

Of course, there was someone in this room who was not fine.

Goeak, who had been listening to the two’s conversation, cut in.

“I am grateful that you saved me, and that gratitude stands. But don’t forget that I am watching you.”

Those were words he should have been saying to him. To take good care of Bisain, and that he was watching.

“Both of you, tend to your wounds first. The time to fight again is approaching.”

* * *

Strategist Hyeok’s expression was stiff as he walked toward the Alliance Lord’s manor.

The Flame Heaven Twin Swords’ surprise attack on the An Estate and their failure to kill Bisain had been an unexpected turn of events.

‘So it has come to this.’

Had Bisain died at the An Estate, the Alliance Lord would certainly have suspected Geom Mugeuk. They had been together right until his death—how could he not be suspicious? The crisis with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would have escalated and relations grown strained, but he and Hwang Seokgyeong would have naturally slipped away from behind this incident.

But now, he had to make the Alliance Lord send Geom Mugeuk back to the Demonic Cult. In that process, his own loyalty would be tested as well.

Of course, he was confident he could handle it. This matter would be handled as Bisain having fallen into Geom Mugeuk’s scheme.

‘Young Alliance Lord, you are finished anyway.’

Before entering the Alliance Lord’s manor, Strategist Hyeok stopped briefly in the corridor and looked at the steel wolves.

That these wolves stopped intruders was naught but a legend. He had been coming and going as he pleased, yet the steel wolves merely watched.

Strategist Hyeok thought that when Tu Wang became the new Alliance Lord, he would get rid of these chunks of scrap metal first.

Strategist Hyeok entered the Alliance Lord’s manor.

The Alliance Lord of the Four Paths Alliance, Baek Jagang, stood with his hands behind his back before a window overlooking the main compound behind the Taesa Hall.

“The Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult has sent a reply saying he will come to meet the Alliance Lord.”

Despite Strategist Hyeok’s report, Baek Jagang gave no answer.

“The Young Alliance Lord appears to be with the Young Cult Leader.”

A cold aura emanated from Baek Jagang.

“He’s doing pointless things.”

He had disliked it from the moment the message came that he was consorting with the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult and would feign death. Had Strategist Hyeok not told him to trust the Young Alliance Lord, he would never have permitted it.

“What if?”

Strategist Hyeok guessed what the Alliance Lord was worried about.

“Had the Young Alliance Lord died, the Young Cult Leader would not have dared reply that he would seek an audience with the Alliance Lord.”

Baek Jagang slowly nodded, and Strategist Hyeok’s report continued.

“The surprise attack on the An Estate is still under investigation. Everyone who knew the location of the An Estate is being thoroughly investigated without exception. It will soon be revealed who leaked the information.”

He intended to eliminate his political enemies by entangling them in this affair. Falsified evidence had already been prepared for the occasion.

Though he showed no emotion outwardly, Baek Jagang was furious.

A secret had been breached, and someone had tried to kill the Young Alliance Lord of the Four Paths Alliance. Someone had dared draw their sword against the Four Paths Alliance.

The worse situation was this.

“Is there a possibility this is the Young Cult Leader’s doing?”

If so, it meant Bisain was being used by Geom Mugeuk.

“The Young Alliance Lord is a wise man. He would never be easily done in by the Young Cult Leader.”

Just as Geom Mugeuk had predicted, he was defending himself by showing complete faith in Bisain.
