Baek Jagang interpreted Strategist Hyeok’s attitude politically—as a young Chief Strategist planning for his future. When Bisain became Alliance Lord, he would no doubt wish to remain Chief Strategist.

“The Young Alliance Lord is likely obtaining something by using the Young Cult Leader.”

“And not the other way around?”

After a brief pause, Strategist Hyeok spoke.

“Please trust the Young Alliance Lord.”

Bisain would swing an axe to kill him, but in the end, he would shoot himself in the foot. Because he would try to push someone who supported him this loyally into the position of a traitor.

“See to it carefully.”

“Do not worry, Alliance Lord. I shall take my leave now.”

From the place where Strategist Hyeok had withdrawn, a report came from a concealed person.

“Illang requests an audience.”

He was Ingung, the captain of the Alliance Lord’s guard.

“Illang? Let him in.”

“He desires a secret, private audience with no one informed.”

The situation was taking an unusual turn. Anyone else would have suspected this sudden request. But Baek Jagang knew. He knew how deeply loyal Illang was to the Alliance.

“Bring him to the training ground.”

* * *

Illang entered the Alliance Lord’s private training ground.

Outsiders could absolutely not enter this place without permission. Apart from Baek Jagang, only Ingung, the captain of the Alliance Lord’s guard, could come and go.

Baek Jagang was wiping an iron dummy in the training ground with a cloth.

It was not ordinary iron. Mixed with ten-thousand-year cold iron, this was a special thing that could not be cut by sword qi or sword gang, made solely for the Alliance Lord.

Countless sword marks were etched upon it. From faint, shallow traces to deep, heavy scars.

Baek Jagang knew well when and how each of the countless sword marks on the iron dummy had been made. What technique had carved it, what mindset had been behind it.

The very fact that he remembered each and every one allowed one to guess at the depth of his training.

It was now an iron dummy he could slash apart with a single stroke, but he did not. Because he wished to pass it down to Bisain just as it was.

“This humble one greets the Alliance Lord.”

“It has been a while. Have you been well?”

Baek Jagang welcomed him gladly. It was clear whom he disliked and whom he liked. Illang belonged in the domain of those he liked.

“You asked to see me separately?”

“Yes, the Young Alliance Lord sent a letter asking that it be delivered only to the Alliance Lord.”

Illang was tense. He could sense it. If he made the slightest misstep today, he would not only fall out of the Alliance Lord’s favor but might lose his life.

“Does that mean you are not to deliver it to Strategist Hyeok either?”

Because all letters entering the Alliance passed through the Chief Strategist.

“He said to deliver it especially without Strategist Hyeok knowing.”

Baek Jagang’s expression grew subtle. When asked for a secret meeting, he had thought it unlikely, but it truly was because of Strategist Hyeok.

“Did he give any reason?”

“No, he only said to make sure it reached the Alliance Lord.”

Illang took the letter from his breast and offered it to the Alliance Lord.

Having checked the letter’s contents, Baek Jagang was startled. It was the first time Illang had seen the Alliance Lord so surprised.

Baek Jagang raised his head and looked at Illang.

“Do you know what is written here? It says that Strategist Hyeok is involved in this affair, and that the attack on the An Estate was his doing as well. The Young Cult Leader says he will meet me personally to explain.”

“!”

At the unexpected words, Illang was also startled. He had never imagined that the letter he delivered would contain such content.

“Do you think so as well?”

Baek Jagang fixed his gaze on Illang. Illang knew how well those small eyes could read a person’s heart. The Alliance Lord was someone who could tell whether his counterpart spoke truth or lies uncannily well.

Should he deny the words of the leader he served, or deny the chief strategist who served the leader of the Sado Alliance?

“I believe that once a subordinate begins to judge the one he serves, he can no longer protect him properly.”

It was the best answer Illang could come up with.

At that, Baek Jagang asked again.

“What sort of person was the Demonic Cult’s young cult leader, as you saw him?”

He had avoided answering once, so there was no escaping now.

Illang answered honestly.

“He was an extraordinary man.”

“Do you think the young alliance leader was bewitched by him?”

Illang hesitated for a moment before answering. He wanted to say no, but—

“Yes. The young alliance leader was bewitched.”

He wanted to add something on Bisa-in’s behalf, but no words came to mind. That the young cult leader had not seemed so bad? That because the young cult leader had saved Bisa-in, he could be trusted?

No. To the Sado Alliance Leader he knew, such an answer would only do more harm than good.

“And I understand why the young alliance leader was bewitched.”

He had answered the question honestly, and the Sado Alliance Leader would make the judgment himself.

“You may withdraw.”

After offering a courteous bow, Illang left the place. He was not certain. Had he truly answered properly? Had his words harmed Bisa-in?

After Illang withdrew, the Sado Alliance Leader asked the empty air.

“What do you think?”

Then Ingung’s answer came.

“There are many things I cannot understand. I believe it would be best if you did not meet him.”

“Then should we inform Strategist Hyeok of this matter?”

Ingung could not answer easily. It was a question of whom he trusted more.

“I cannot believe that Strategist Hyeok is a traitor.”

And that he had even tried to kill the young alliance leader? No matter how he thought about it, he could not believe it. On the other hand, the young alliance leader had fallen for the Demonic Cult’s young cult leader? That, too, was hard to believe, but still—perhaps?

Baek Jagang, meanwhile, had yet to reach any conclusion. A fierce glint flowed from his small eyes.

“So aren’t you curious? He asks to meet me, knowing full well we would never believe him? Just what is that fellow thinking?”

Having served the Sado Alliance Leader for a long time, Ingung could tell. Baek Jagang had already become intrigued by Geom Mugeuk, as if thinking, “Look at this little rascal.” Illang’s words—that he understood why he had been bewitched—had been decisive.

If Baek Jagang had made up his mind to meet him, then what he had to do was this.

“I will choose a new meeting place and send word.”

Because they, not the other side, had to control the situation.

Baek Jagang silently nodded, then looked at the iron man dummy standing in the training ground.

Over the iron man dummy, the figure of a man overlapped. The person best suited to this expressionless iron man dummy was none other than the Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin.

He was the person Baek Jagang acknowledged most, and at the same time, the person he hated most. More than anything, he hated that arrogant gaze that seemed to look down on people.

‘Let’s see just what sort of crop you’ve raised.’

Absolute Regression, Chapter 361

Chapter 361: If You Had a Son Like Me.
--------------------------------------
Bi Sa-in was doing everything he could to recover his body as quickly as possible.

Circulating his qi, then circulating it again.

After completing several rounds of breathing and qi circulation, he rose from his seat. His entire body still ached and throbbed, but the frustration made it impossible to remain still.

When Bi Sa-in walked out into the yard, Geom Mugeuk was standing there, looking up at the sky.

Bi Sa-in stared for a moment at that back, which looked lonelier than anything, then approached him.

“What are you staring at so blankly?”

Geom Mugeuk turned to Bi Sa-in and smiled. Now, when he looked at him, he smiled so easily like that. He was always one step ahead. In making plans, in putting them into practice, and even in relationships between people.

Could he really smile as brightly as that smile? Could he completely shake off the suspicion that Geom Mugeuk had approached him only to use him? It was truly an endless repetition of believing and doubting.

“I’m trying to look at the sky, even if I have to force myself. When was the last time you looked at the sky?”

“It’s been a while.”

“Then come here and let’s look at it together.”

Bi Sa-in sat down beside Geom Mugeuk and looked up at the sky.

“Your aura feels like the sea, so why do you like the sky so much?”

“I like the sea, too. Next time, let’s go to the sea together. We can seduce the wandering heroines who came to play there.”

Just imagining Geom Mugeuk throwing flirtatious glances at women on the beach was enough to make him laugh. And how awkwardly would he himself be standing beside him?

“Is your body all right?”

“I’m much better now.”

“That’s a relief.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze, which had been on Bi Sa-in, turned back to the sky. Bi Sa-in also gazed at the clouds drifting quietly with Geom Mugeuk.

“What kind of person is the leader of the Sado Alliance?”

Geom Mugeuk did not know much about the Sado Alliance Leader. Before he could properly learn, everyone had died. The only information he had about the Murim Alliance Leader or the Sado Alliance Leader came from what had been recorded in history.

Bi Sa-in hesitated for a moment before letting out a light sigh.

He had been wondering what he should say, but he realized that he himself did not know his master very well either.

“It hasn’t been long since I started trying to look at Master properly, in detail, so I don’t know well either.”

At the honest answer, Geom Mugeuk nodded and said,

“To be honest, I’m the same. Even though I’m trying so hard to look at my father properly, if you asked me, I wouldn’t know how to answer either.”

“Is it difficult because they’re fathers and masters? Or is it simply always this difficult?”

“Wouldn’t it be both?”

The two looked at each other and smiled faintly. A smile now suited Bi Sa-in’s rough face quite well.

“Are you nervous about meeting the Alliance Leader?”

“I am.”

“I think this is the first time I’ve seen you nervous.”

“Before, I was pretending to be strong. Now we’re friends, so I’m saying it honestly.”

Bi Sa-in also conveyed his honest feelings.

“I’m sorry for leaving it to you.”

No matter how much pain he was in, it was something he should have gone to do himself. Even if he had to be loaded onto a cart, he should have gone himself. Think about it. If he sent Geom Mugeuk in his place when he was alive and well like this, what would the Sado Alliance Leader think of him?

That was why he had considered insisting that he would go himself. He had also thought about asking to go together.

But his instincts stopped him. Just leave it to Geom Mugeuk. At decisive moments, do not put forth useless pride. Step in properly when it is time to step in, and withdraw decisively when it is time to withdraw.

Instead, he wanted to say this much.

“I’m receiving your help now, but someday there will be a time when I help you.”

“That’s why I asked you in advance, didn’t I? When that time comes, pull me along a little. The more you get used to me, the more you’ll realize I’m not such an extraordinary person. When that time comes, think of it like this. Let’s not allow familiarity to devour all sense of value. There is still old affection between us, so let’s not disregard it too much.”

Would that really happen? Geom Mugeuk seemed like someone who would keep surprising him until his hair turned white. So even when they were old, he felt he would still be saying this: Isn’t it about time you got tired? Please stop being so clever now. Stop surprising people so much.

Even if that were not the case.

‘The more familiar you become, the more I will respect you.’

Bi Sa-in was still not able to reveal all of his inner thoughts.

At that moment, a demonic practitioner from the safe house came and delivered a letter. It was a letter that had secretly arrived from the Sado Alliance.

“The Alliance Leader has sent word that he wants to meet separately.”

Illang had done it.

Bi Sa-in looked at Geom Mugeuk. Now his fate hung on this meeting.

“I’ll be back.”

When Geom Mugeuk rose, Bi Sa-in rose with him. He had been about to make a joke telling him not to get punished for acting impudent before the Alliance Leader, but he did not.

“Please explain things well to the Alliance Leader.”

And prove once again that my instincts were right, my friend.

* * *

Geom Mugeuk arrived at a windswept field.

In that place, open on all sides, Baek Jagang had arrived first and was waiting.

The moment he saw Geom Mugeuk walking toward him, Baek Jagang could tell.

‘He is definitely that man’s son.’

He had not revealed demonic energy, nor had he shown any particular aura, but Baek Jagang could tell. And most decisively.

‘They look alike.’

Geom Ujin’s figure overlapped with Geom Mugeuk’s, then separated again. He imagined the two of them walking side by side.

‘At least in looks, it’s a bumper crop.’

Compared to Geom Ujin’s cold and intense appearance, he was certainly handsome and pleasant to look at. Instead of that arrogant gaze that looked down on others, his clear and deep eyes also drew attention.

“I greet the Alliance Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk bowed politely in greeting.

Though he knew the other party was the Young Cult Leader, Baek Jagang pretended not to know.

“How am I supposed to believe that you are the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult?”

He wanted to see how Geom Mugeuk would react.

Geom Mugeuk put on a deliberately startled expression.

“Would there be anyone in this world who would dare attempt fraud before the Sado Alliance Leader?”

“The Murim is a place where experts as numerous as grains of sand live, so there may well be someone like that. Is there any guarantee that you are not an assassin who came to kill me?”

“If I had the skill to succeed in assassination while under the Sado Alliance Leader’s suspicion, I wouldn’t be working as an assassin.”

Even at Geom Mugeuk’s answer, Baek Jagang’s expression did not change at all. In the end, Geom Mugeuk rubbed his own face once and shouted,

“Have you ever seen an assassin this handsome in your life?”

At that banter, Baek Jagang could not help but let out an incredulous laugh.

Since no one usually joked with him, he was rather weak to this kind of jest. If Geom Mugeuk had taken even that into account while speaking with him, then for his young age, he could be called quite clever.

“To be honest, I have never seen an assassin.”

“Of course not. What kind of fool would come to kill the Alliance Leader?”

Within his casual words, he included words that acknowledged and elevated the Sado Alliance Leader. As he did with the Demon Supremes, even if he did not openly flatter him, he tried his utmost to match his mood.

Baek Jagang’s first impression was strange. He was different from his father, and different from the Murim Alliance Leader Jin Paecheon as well. Was it because his eyes were small? It was hard to sense what he was thinking.

“If you speak with me for just a little while, you will think, Ah, this fellow really is the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult. By the time we part, you will be reluctant to let me go and ask, Won’t you come with me to the alliance? After a few days, you will even tell me to try sitting in the Taishi chair.”

“You’re very confident.”

“If I didn’t at least have this reckless confidence, how could I dare face the Alliance Leader?”

From the start, Baek Jagang had been wary that Geom Mugeuk was someone who bewitched people. So he had been confident that he would not be shaken no matter what Geom Mugeuk said.

But at the very least, this kind of conversation was fresh. It had truly been a long time since someone had spoken so freely in front of him.

All the more so for someone who even said this.

“What do you think it would be like to have a son like me?”

“I would certainly dislike it.”

Baek Jagang answered firmly.

To be honest, I would too.

Geom Mugeuk agreed as well. Rather than disliking it, it would be closer to being burdensome. Looking at it that way, he thought that perhaps this Baek Jagang might have something in common with him.

Staring into the clear eyes looking at him, Baek Jagang asked,

“I hear you enjoy bewitching people.”

“I simply like people. That’s why I’m often suspected of being a flatterer.”

“Because I have never seen a demonic practitioner who likes people.”

“Then today, at last, you are seeing one.”

Baek Jagang stared intently at Geom Mugeuk. The rumors that he was good at detecting lies were not exaggerated. When someone lied, Baek Jagang felt it. Ah, this fellow is lying. Goosebumps would rise along his ears.

“Please watch me today. Judge whether I am bewitching people, or simply liking them.”

So far, he did not sense any lies from Geom Mugeuk. He was merely curious where this confidence came from. It was not easy to be this bold in front of him.

“Why did you ask to see me?”

“The reason I asked to see you today is to tell you the truth.”

“What truth? That the Deputy Strategist of our alliance, who is to sit in the position of Chief Strategist, is a traitor?”

“That’s right.”

“And not an evil, dark truth imbued with the schemes of the Demonic Cult?”

“The truth shines all the more when it is in an evil and dark place.”

Geom Mugeuk knew. For Baek Jagang, this would not be easy to acknowledge. The moment he acknowledged it, it would mean that he and the Sado Alliance had been played by those bastards.

“Where is Sa-in now?”

“He is treating his injuries at a safe house of our cult.”

The moment he said those words, Geom Mugeuk saw it. Irritation and worry flickered across Baek Jagang’s expression at the same time.

“How badly is he hurt?”

“He suffered serious injuries, but fortunately, they are wounds he can recover from.”

“Whose doing was it?”

“Have you not yet received a report on who was responsible? Well, if they are going to fabricate evidence, reporting as late as possible would be advantageous. The ones who ambushed the Young Alliance Leader were the Yeomcheon Twin Swords. The person who sent them was Hyeok Sagun.”

Geom Mugeuk observed Baek Jagang’s reaction.

Baek Jagang showed no emotional disturbance. It seemed he had not yet decided who the traitor was, or perhaps he was treating Geom Mugeuk’s words as lies altogether.

“And not someone you sent?”

“That was my first time meeting those people. Though it was also the last time I saw them.”

With those small eyes, Baek Jagang stared at Geom Mugeuk as if trying to pierce through him.

If he had to endure that gaze, he would have done so calmly, but Geom Mugeuk deliberately shifted his eyes slightly as if afraid. Taking one step back was courtesy toward the Sado Alliance Leader.

“Right now, he is being protected at our cult’s safe house by Goeak, one of the Seven Great Masters of the Sado. You need not worry. Ah, perhaps you may not trust Goeak, Alliance Leader.”

“At the very least, he is not someone who would harm Sa-in. Rather, it is you I cannot trust.”

“Do not think of me as a demonic practitioner. Think of me as your disciple’s friend. Please trust your disciple’s eye more than you trust me.”

“A friend, is it? I have never had one in my life, so I would not know.”

As expected, he was not easy to deal with. He seemed frank, yet it was not easy to know what was inside him.

“You will know by watching Hyeok Sagun’s next move.”

“What do you mean?”

“I am saying you should watch whom he frames for the crime. Hyeok Sagun will use this incident as an opportunity to eliminate a political enemy. He has probably already planted all the evidence as well. Please watch carefully whom he points to.”

Geom Mugeuk did not intend to turn his heart around immediately with this single meeting.

What always ruins matters is haste. The greater and more important the matter, the more people want to see results quickly, and that is what always ruins things.

They could part now and meet again after Hyeok Sagun dealt with his political enemy. He would meet him ten more times, and if necessary, a hundred more. This matter had to be handled with that kind of mindset.

“I will see you again then and speak further.”

“There is no need.”

“Pardon?”

“I understand well enough, so withdraw from the affairs of our alliance now.”

This was precisely the response Geom Mugeuk had feared and worried about most. Whether Baek Jagang believed him or not, he would try to make him withdraw.

“Return to the Demonic Cult.”

When Geom Mugeuk gave no answer, Baek Jagang released his aura. The baleful energy he emitted pressed down on Geom Mugeuk and surged toward him.

As if demanding an answer at once, his energy was so powerful that it made it impossible to breathe. The Heavenly Demon Protective Art activated on its own and protected Geom Mugeuk’s body.

Ordinarily, he would have said, I’ll go, I’ll go right away, while joking around and retreated for the time being. There was no need to reveal his skill unnecessarily.

But Geom Mugeuk endured. It was what his instincts told him to do.

‘I have to withstand this.’

Instead, he wore a relaxed expression, as if provoking Baek Jagang.

Baek Jagang’s baleful energy grew even stronger. The energy he emitted whirled around Geom Mugeuk. Rocks shattered and flew away from the force alone.

Baek Jagang’s momentum was truly powerful. If not for the cry of his instincts, it was a dangerous energy he should never have tried to endure.

Even so, Geom Mugeuk endured and endured again.

At the very moment Baek Jagang’s baleful energy reached its peak.

Geom Mugeuk’s expression brightened radiantly, and he shouted,

“I did it!”

At the same time, unable to hold out any longer, Geom Mugeuk was sent flying and rolled across the ground.

Only then did Baek Jagang withdraw his baleful energy.

As he approached, wondering just why this fellow had endured, Geom Mugeuk sprang to his feet and said,

“Thanks to you, Alliance Leader, I have achieved a breakthrough in my martial art.”

In the process of withstanding Baek Jagang’s baleful energy, the Heavenly Demon Protective Art had reached great completion. With his recent continuous training, it had been tickling and stimulating Geom Mugeuk, and today it had borne an unexpected result.

Geom Mugeuk had truly never dreamed that he would achieve great completion of the Heavenly Demon Protective Art through this meeting. The movement of great martial arts was truly impossible to predict.

Geom Mugeuk dropped to the ground and gave a deep bow.

At that sight, Baek Jagang flinched in surprise. He had never imagined that the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult would bow to him.

“Alliance Leader, you guessed why I was enduring and continued sending your energy until the end. I am truly grateful.”

Of course, that was not why he had pushed him to the end. He had pushed him because he wanted to know the extent of Geom Mugeuk’s martial skill. There had also been a bit of the thought, You dare endure against me?

“Why are you telling me that? You did not need to say it.”

“If I am to thank you, I must tell you. It is an achievement I gained thanks to you, Alliance Leader.”

From the start, he had met Geom Mugeuk with such deep wariness that he had not felt especially impressed. He spoke well, joked well, and was clever. That was all. How could one be bewitched by a brat like this?

And yet, he bowed without hesitation and expressed his gratitude like this?

Come to think of it, not once today had goosebumps risen along his ears.

“Your father would not be pleased if he learned of this.”

Baek Jagang imagined the displeased expression of that arrogant Heavenly Demon.

“If my father learned that you helped his son achieve progress in martial arts, he would have expressed his gratitude to you, Alliance Leader. He would have told me to bow ten times if I had to. My father is not someone who lets pride ruin a greater cause.”

A comparison with the Heavenly Demon. It was a provocation Baek Jagang absolutely could not avoid.

‘Look at this little brat.’

Absolute Regression, Chapter 362

Chapter 362: You Raised Your Child This Well?
---------------------------------------------
“Then I will take my leave.”

Geom Mugeuk bowed politely and turned around.

The crux of this provocation lay in turning his back and leaving. My father would not let me go for the sake of the greater cause, but you are letting me go.

Geom Mugeuk strode away with long steps. Please call me back. Hurry up and call me! The more desperately he wished it, the less lingering attachment his steps showed.

“Wait.”

At Baek Jagang’s call, a triumphant smile formed at the corners of Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

Geom Mugeuk turned back toward him.

“What is it?”

“Where do you think you’re going?”

“Did you not tell me to return to the cult?”

Baek Jagang’s small eyes narrowed until they looked like lines drawn sideways.

If he sent him away like this, he would become a man who ruined the greater cause for the sake of his pride.

Of course, he knew this was Geom Mugeuk’s provocation.

The problem was that the object of comparison was the Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin.

He might not care about losing to anyone else, but he did not want to lose to Geom Ujin. Geom Ujin was his lifelong archenemy. He was someone Baek Jagang acknowledged, yet hated—a target of love and resentment.

“Did your father really tell you to bow to me even ten times if you had to?”

How could Baek Jagang not know he had been provoking him? It was time to be honest. Geom Mugeuk let out a light sigh and said,

“I lied. Father would have been furious.”

Geom Mugeuk lowered his head to Baek Jagang.

“I apologize for using such a petty method.”

Geom Mugeuk frankly admitted that he had provoked him on purpose.

“So you know it was petty?”

“Yes.”

Once he admitted that much, Baek Jagang’s expression softened a little.

“That is how desperate I am.”

“If anyone should be desperate, it should be me. Why are you the one who’s desperate?”

Though he appeared calm, Baek Jagang felt a sense of crisis.

A safe house had been breached, and his successor had been attacked. And the one pointed to as the mastermind behind it was the Alliance’s deputy strategist.

He had never once taken this matter lightly. If he had, he would not even have come here. He simply did not want to show impatience in front of the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult.

Geom Mugeuk’s desperation, on the other hand, was extremely personal.

“I truly regard the Young Alliance Leader as my friend. I hope that friend safely inherits the position of Alliance Leader.”

Baek Jagang realized that there was still no chill rising near his ears.

It was a sense that worked on many people, but there were also people like this, on whom it did not work. After all, he was not a fortune-teller.

This Geom Mugeuk would have to be considered an exception too.

The young cult leader of the Demonic Cult was telling the truth? There was no way that could be.

“Why do you want to become friends with Sain?”

Geom Mugeuk answered without hesitation.

“Because I believe that friend can open a new path for the unorthodox way.”

“A new path for the unorthodox way? What is this new path you speak of?”

“I do not know that either. It must be a path the Young Alliance Leader has to find. But whether it is my Demonic Path or the Young Alliance Leader’s Unorthodox Path, I believe there will surely be a new ideal in our era.”

Geom Mugeuk knew what Baek Jagang was thinking right now. Before his regression, he had been the same. If some green youngster had spoken of ideals, he would have thought exactly what Baek Jagang was thinking now.

It is because you do not know reality.

He had lived through that reality to the very end. He had lived that life—at times insignificant, at times astonishing and great—while overcoming all manner of pain and storms.

Of course, he knew that did not mean he could give the right answer, nor did living through it once mean he would live this life well. Even if he lived a hundred times again, he knew he could not live well unless he made an effort. Rather, the very fact that he had already lived once might become a shackle around his ankles and hurl his life into the abyss.

Baek Jagang, that is why I am trying. If I leave things alone, both you and I will die, and the bitter reality waiting for us is one where the Twelve Zodiac Kings rule the martial world.

“How do you feel when you think of my father?”

It was true that whenever his father was mentioned, Baek Jagang’s eyes gleamed.

“You first get angry and want to fight him and win, don’t you? When I think of the Young Alliance Leader, I feel happy. The first thing that comes to mind is meeting him, laughing, chatting, and having fun. Our martial world will not fight one another or hate one another. We will be playing on the beach, flirting with young ladies.”

“That is nothing more than a childish ideal.”

“Why is that?”

“Because a human who possesses power will never be satisfied with that. You, and Sain as well, will all change.”

Geom Mugeuk looked up at the sky for a moment.

“Before coming here, I sat together with the Young Alliance Leader and looked up at the sky. We imagined the day when our hair would turn white like those white clouds. Will we really change?”

Baek Jagang gave no answer. His expression said, Do I really have to say it for you to understand? Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“Then I will strive to change in a good direction. I will strive to change as little for the worse as possible.”

Baek Jagang quietly stared at Geom Mugeuk. The mere fact that this fellow could make him listen to such absurd talk was enough to admit that he was no ordinary lad.

“A dream, is it.”

Come to think of it, he had never once thought about it. How could a dreamer rule the Unorthodox Path? The Unorthodox Path was a place where one could die just by closing one’s eyes.

Suddenly, he found himself wanting to hear Geom Mugeuk say, “Alliance Leader, you were right. My thoughts were nothing more than childish ideals.” He wanted to impart a lesson. To Geom Ujin’s son.

Geom Mugeuk knew that what would seize his interest was neither ideals nor friendship.

The strongest thing that could draw his interest was this.

“Father once spoke of you, Alliance Leader.”

As expected, Baek Jagang’s eyes grew intense.

“What did he say?”

“It may be rude, but may I repeat it exactly as he said it?”

“Do so.”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk said,

“For an unorthodox bastard, he is a man with grand dreams.”

Once again, Baek Jagang gave an incredulous laugh. It was something that should have put him in a bad mood, but it somehow felt as if he had been acknowledged, so it did not feel that bad.

Yes, if it was Geom Ujin. If it was him, he was someone qualified to say such a thing.

Then Geom Mugeuk suddenly asked,

“Is the dream you dream, Alliance Leader, the same as my father’s dream?”

Baek Jagang flinched for an instant.

He knew. He knew Geom Ujin dreamed of unifying the martial world. He had even warned Bi Sain about it.

Now the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult was asking him.

Do you dream of unifying the martial world too?

It was a question no one had ever dared ask, and naturally, it was something he had never answered.

I do not know what dream your father dreams. He omitted such obvious words.

He still did not know for certain what kind of person this young cult leader was, but at the very least, he could tell that the other was someone with whom words could be exchanged even if what should be omitted was omitted.

“And if it is?”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk said calmly,

“I ask that you dream a different dream.”

Baek Jagang burst out in anger and shouted,

“So that only your father can dream that dream?”

A powerful energy swept over Geom Mugeuk, but the Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art, having reached great accomplishment, protected him far more comfortably than before.

“No. I will wake my father as well.”

As the energy pressing down on Geom Mugeuk vanished, Baek Jagang burst into laughter. It was the first time since the two had met that he laughed out loud.

“You know your father even less than I do. The moment you shake his body to wake him, you will no longer be able to become the next Heavenly Demon. He may smash your head in his sleep.”

That was how the Sado Alliance Leader saw his father.

Geom Mugeuk sighed and said,

“Perhaps.”

In at least one regard, Baek Jagang was certainly being drawn to Geom Mugeuk. The way he admitted things when they had to be admitted. He did not possess the pointless stubbornness of those who were half-baked and clever. That was why he did not frustrate people.

And there was one more point that drew him in: with unexpected words, he left no room for boredom even for a moment.

“Alliance Leader, I am hungry.”

This too was something he had heard for the first time since becoming the Sado Alliance Leader. Who had ever dared look at him and say they were hungry?

“Please buy me a meal.”

The fellow knew very well that it was difficult to coldly send away a hungry person.

Baek Jagang thought of Geom Ujin again. Of that man, colder and more taciturn than anyone in the world.

“With that personality of yours, you somehow managed to raise a rascal like this.”

Yes. Even if he was going to send him away, he should at least feed him first.

Baek Jagang spoke into the air.

“Prepare a carriage.”

Then the answer of Ingung, the captain of the guards, came.

“Yes!”

While waiting for the carriage, the two silently looked at the river flowing far below the mountain in the distance.

“I like this place. It is quiet, and the scenery is excellent.”

“When there is someone I wish to kill in secret, I summon them here.”

Geom Mugeuk looked down at his feet, then looked back at Baek Jagang and said,

“You are not joking.”

“No.”

“Was I perhaps close to dying too?”

“It was close.”

Rather than liking Geom Mugeuk himself, Baek Jagang enjoyed this kind of conversation. Since becoming the Sado Alliance Leader, he had lived his entire life rigidly.

“Do you still have people you want to kill these days? If one reaches your level, Alliance Leader, I would think there would be no one left to kill.”

“There are still many. Resentment piles up along with age.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled faintly.

Seeing that smile, as if saying he knew all about it, Baek Jagang thought inwardly,

“What would a young whelp like you know?”

That was why he kept wanting to dig into him. No matter how bright he was, he was still a child, was he not? He wanted to show him that.

“Why? Do you think your father does not kill? You may only see his noble and dignified appearance, but in places unseen, corpses must be piling up every day. Do you think the orthodox bastards are any different? They must be killing people by accusing them of being demons and branding them as unorthodox.”

This was likely one of the reasons this man did not believe in ideals. It was likely the reason he tried to hold only cold reality in those small eyes.

“In your ideals, no one would die, I suppose.”

“No. I will kill even more.”

“!”

Baek Jagang looked at Geom Mugeuk with puzzled eyes.

“You may wonder what the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult is talking about, but I hate those who have given up being human. I will kill them all.”

It was not something the young cult leader of the Demonic Cult should say, and even less something he should say to the Sado Alliance Leader, so Geom Mugeuk finished his words with a jest.

“The saying that if good exists, evil must also exist—I think it was probably made up by the evil side. To try and hold out somehow. Did you perhaps make that saying, Alliance Leader?”

Baek Jagang realized that he was not a man without laughter. At the question of whether he had made it, he had almost laughed again.

“In your Demonic Path, we would die the most, it seems.”

“That is why I am supporting and cheering for the Young Alliance Leader, so that does not happen.”

Baek Jagang realized that this young cult leader was not an easy opponent.

Suddenly, he wanted to see Bi Sain.

Geom Ujin had raised his son this well, and he felt that he himself had been complacent.

He could not allow himself to fall behind Geom Ujin, and then have his child fall behind as well.

His child? Yes. A disciple was no different from a child.

He did not know whether it was because of the sense of defeat that he could not surpass Geom Ujin, or because he usually regarded Bi Sain like his own child, but at least right now, he was engaged in a battle over the cultivation of their children.

Meanwhile, the carriage driven by Ingung, who had pulled a bamboo hat low over his head, arrived. To avoid attracting attention, he had not brought a carriage of the Sado Alliance, but one commonly seen in the marketplace.

The two climbed into the carriage. For this secret outing, bamboo hats with veils attached had been prepared inside the carriage as well.

“Where are we going?”

“You said you were hungry, did you not?”

The place where the carriage carrying the two arrived was a shabby restaurant somewhat removed from the marketplace.

“It is a place I come to from time to time.”

And so the two ate there.

Geom Mugeuk once again marveled at the surprising experiences life occasionally threw his way. To think he would be eating with the Sado Alliance Leader in front of the Sado Alliance’s main headquarters. When he had first set out for this place, could he ever have imagined such a thing?

“I also have a regular place in front of the cult. If the opportunity arises next time, I would very much like to invite you there, Alliance Leader.”

Baek Jagang nodded slightly, but he believed such a thing would never happen. Unless war broke out and he conquered the Demonic Cult’s main headquarters. And if that happened, would a meal even go down his throat? Young cult leader of the Demonic Cult.

As they ate, Geom Mugeuk brought up Hyeok Sagun.

“Hyeok Sagun must have played the role of an excellent strategist better than anyone until now. Looking back now, he was too perfect, and he must also have been extremely lucky. From now on, view him under the assumption that he is a traitor. You will certainly see a different side of him.”

He was not worried that saying this for no reason would expose him. Baek Jagang was not someone who easily revealed what was on his mind. Moreover, he had been examining other aspects as well.

“If I assume your words are true, then the Chief Strategist who withdrew because of illness must have been done in by that bastard too.”

“Yes. I believe he was poisoned.”

“Then why did you not say so?”

“Because you would realize it anyway, Alliance Leader.”

Baek Jagang looked at Geom Mugeuk. He did not know what the true face beyond that veil was, but one thing was certain. Though he spoke that much, he was thoroughly controlling what he should and should not say.

At the center of Baek Jagang’s deepening interest in Geom Mugeuk was Geom Ujin.

“He raised his child this well? That cold-blooded man?”

That thought kept coming to him. At least in this battle, he did not want to lose.

“When did you say you would come to the Alliance?”

“In two days.”

“I will see you then.”

Baek Jagang withdrew his intention to send Geom Mugeuk away.

As he rose from his seat, Geom Mugeuk said to him,

“Before you leave, please scold the Young Alliance Leader.”

“Scold him? Why?”

“He must feel very apologetic that he could not come in person. If he hears that you scolded him, Alliance Leader, he will feel more at ease instead.”

Baek Jagang was genuinely surprised.

He was considering Bi Sain’s feelings in this situation? That too, such an easily overlooked emotion? This young fellow?

Now he understood a little why the word “bewitchment” had come up.

“Do not grow accustomed to it.”

At Baek Jagang’s words, Geom Mugeuk wore a puzzled expression.

“What do you mean?”

“If you tell Sain that, he will understand.”

It seemed to be a conversation that Baek Jagang and Bi Sain had shared in the past.

“I will tell him so.”

After parting from Geom Mugeuk, Baek Jagang climbed into the carriage. The light flowing from his small eyes was intense enough to swallow the entire world.

“Look at these rascals!”

Astonishingly, the person added to “rascals” was Geom Ujin.

Though it was a battle taking place only in his own heart, now even the farmers had joined this fight, making it a two-on-two battle.

Absolute Regression, Chapter 363

Chapter 363: I Was the Most Impatient in Murim.
-----------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk returned to the safe house.

Bi Sain had been waiting the entire time for him to come back.

“How did it go?”

Bi Sain was desperately curious about how the meeting between Geom Mugeuk and the Sado Alliance Leader had gone. So much so that he wanted to grab him by the collar and shake him, telling him to hurry up and speak.

“Let me catch my breath first. I’m coming back from meeting none other than that fearsome Sado Alliance Leader. Let me drink some water too.”

“As if you were ever afraid. How did it go?”

Burning with impatience, Bi Sain limped after Geom Mugeuk.

“At this rate, you’ll be using lightness arts soon.”

“I’m in so much pain I could die. So stop teasing me and tell me what happened.”

Geom Mugeuk drank the water refreshingly, then turned back to Bi Sain.

“As you can see, I didn’t come back beaten to a pulp.”

“When you told him, what did the Alliance Leader say? Did he believe us?”

“He half-believed, half-doubted.”

If it had been up to him, it would have been better if he had believed him completely, but the current situation was far more unfavorable to him.

He thought it fortunate that Baek Jagang had believed even half of it. Yes, if Geom Mugeuk had gone, how could he not have handled it well? He trusted him. If he could not trust this man, then whom could he trust? He believed that Geom Mugeuk had explained it better than he himself could have.

“How was the Alliance Leader? Was he hurt anywhere?”

Bi Sain’s voice trembled slightly.

Concern for his master could be felt beneath his rugged face.

“The Alliance Leader is safe, so don’t worry.”

Only then did Bi Sain feel relieved. Now that it had been confirmed that Hyeok Sagun was clearly an enemy, there was no way he could not have been worried. In his heart, he wanted to run over at once and stand guard beside the Alliance Leader, but he could only lament the reality that he could not.

“You’ve worked hard. Rest.”

He wanted to ask if the Alliance Leader had said anything about him, but he could not bring himself to open his mouth.

If he heard, “He said nothing,” he would feel pointlessly disappointed. It would only be natural, since his innocence had yet to be proven. Yet the human heart was just like that.

Just as Bi Sain was about to turn away, Geom Mugeuk spoke the words he had been waiting for so desperately.

“The Alliance Leader had a message for you.”

Bi Sain turned around in surprise. Expectation and fear mingled in his heart.

“What did he say?”

Geom Mugeuk gazed at Bi Sain and relayed the Sado Alliance Leader’s words exactly as they were.

“Do not grow accustomed to it.”

At first, Bi Sain did not understand and tilted his head slightly.

“That was the message the Alliance Leader told me to give you. He said you would understand once you heard it.”

“Do not grow accustomed to it? Do not grow accustomed to it.”

The moment he repeated those words, Bi Sain flinched. The instant he remembered when he had heard them before, emotion surged across his face.

“The Alliance Leader was hoping you would recover quickly.”

Strictly speaking, he should have said, “He must have been,” but Baek Jagang had clearly been worried about Bi Sain. Geom Mugeuk could tell that much just from the feeling.

“Thank you.”

Bi Sain could feel it. Geom Mugeuk going alone had brought about the best possible result. The words his master had sent proved it. If his mood had been foul, he would never have said those words.

Bi Sain returned to his room.

Once inside, he first drank the medicine that had been left to cool.

He recalled the day the Alliance Leader had spoken those words to him. That day too, he had been in a room filled with the smell of medicine, just like this.

It had been the day he returned injured after going out on an Alliance mission. It was a failure from a time when, as the Alliance Leader’s disciple, he had enjoyed great trust, so the blow to his heart had been far greater than the wound to his body.

The Alliance Leader had come to the infirmary.

Standing beside him as he lay on the bed, the Alliance Leader had said,

—Do not grow accustomed to defeat.

That moment came vividly back to him. The moment his master had looked at him and spoken.

—If you grow accustomed to defeat, your life becomes third-rate.

The moment he heard those words, tears had burst forth.

Rather than gratitude that the Alliance Leader had come to see him, the shame of having failed his mission swallowed his heart whole. Shame had paralyzed his reason; he had been that age then.

—Are you angry?

—Yes.

Wiping his tears, he tried to sit up.

—I will go again and accomplish it. This time, I will definitely succeed.

At that time, his master had forced him back down and said this.

—Who wouldn’t be angry when they lose? But listen. If you make a scene just because you are angry, that too is third-rate.

—How can I become first-rate? How can I keep winning?

Back then, instead of answering, his master had fed him the medicine placed beside him and changed his bandages.

He had not understood it then, but now he felt he did.

Do not be swept away by emotion; first, gather yourself. Do not draw your sword; change your bandages. When you can calmly govern your feelings and examine yourself, only then can you become first-rate. His master had answered him by wrapping his bandages.

“Ah! Master.”

Back then, he had not understood that profound meaning. He had only felt anger and shame, and wanted only to somehow go back and accomplish the mission.

Just like right now. Even now, his feelings were the same. Quickly, quickly, quickly. He had to go quickly and cut those bastards down. He had to go quickly and prove his innocence to the Alliance Leader.

At the realization that the emotions he was feeling now were no different from the rash urgency of his youth, goosebumps rose all over his body.

“Master!”

Tears welled up in Bi Sain’s eyes. That day, he had cried out of anger, but now he cried because he missed the Alliance Leader. He cried because he was so grateful that his master remembered that day, a day he himself had completely forgotten. He cried because he was so sorry.

Bi Sain slowly undid his bandages and applied fresh medicine. As he calmly looked at his wounds, the battle from that time came back to him, and new thoughts arose about how he should have dealt with his opponent.

Even without being unable to restrain his anger and charging in like a madman, these wounds were telling him how he could become first-rate.

“……Master.”

Bi Sain slowly wrapped the bandages around his wounds.

* * *

Baek Jagang was seated in the grand preceptor’s chair of the Alliance Leader’s Hall.

Ever since returning from meeting Geom Mugeuk, he had kept thinking of one person.

Geom Woojin.

Meeting Geom Mugeuk had felt like this.

To be honest, it had not felt like meeting Geom Mugeuk, but like meeting Geom Woojin.

It was as if Geom Woojin had not held out his sword and asked, “How is my swordsmanship?” but rather, “How is my child?”

Naturally, Geom Mugeuk’s face came to mind.

—Please buy me a meal.

He wondered whether Bi Sain could truly overcome such an absurd fellow. He needed to flatten that nose of his.

Of course, that would only be if the one behind this matter truly was Hyeok Sagun, as Geom Mugeuk had said.

At that moment, the voice of Guard Captain In Gung came from the air.

“Strategist Hyeok has arrived.”

“Let him in.”

A moment later, Hyeok Sagun entered.

With small but haughty eyes, Baek Jagang silently watched him walk toward him.

“How long has it been since you entered the Alliance?”

“This year marks my seventeenth.”

“And since you began acting as Chief Strategist?”

“It has been nearly two years.”

“Has it already been that long?”

Hyeok Sagun sensed that Baek Jagang was different from usual. But he did not find it strange.

Given the current situation, would it not be stranger if he were exactly the same as usual?

“Where did you go? When I came to see you earlier, I heard you had left the Alliance.”

“I went out and ate a meal outside for the first time in a while.”

Hyeok Sagun knew well that there was a restaurant Baek Jagang often frequented.

“The situation is not ordinary, so please be careful for the time being.”

He had been keeping watch on the Alliance Leader’s movements. But if he moved secretly with only the Guard Captain, as he had today, there was no choice but to lose track of him.

Hyeok Sagun was not in a good mood. It was bad enough that everything needed to proceed smoothly according to his plan; unexpected situations could not keep occurring.

“I have discovered who gave the order to raid the safe house.”

It was exactly the matter Geom Mugeuk had told him to watch closely: whom Hyeok Sagun would name.

“Who is it?”

“Commander Beon of the Extreme Blade Corps.”

The moment he heard those words, Baek Jagang’s aura burst forth as if exploding from his body. A man who did not reveal emotion over most matters had openly shown his anger.

The Extreme Blade Corps.

The elite organization directly under the Alliance Leader.

After Yaryul Han, the original commander of the Extreme Blade Corps, stepped down, the newly appointed commander, Beon Cheon, was a man of remarkable loyalty to Baek Jagang.

‘He is naming Beon Cheon?’

If Beon Cheon became the mastermind, it would be no different from having his right arm cut off. At the very least, what Geom Mugeuk had said was true. He had said Hyeok Sagun would use this as an opportunity to deal with his political enemies.

“The evidence?”

“Here it is. The Demonic Cult was behind it.”

Hyeok Sagun walked up the steps and handed the documents he had brought to Baek Jagang.

For a traitor, he appeared far too calm. No chill rose at Baek Jagang’s ears either. That meant either he was not lying, or he was someone on whom that sense did not work.

After handing over the evidence, Hyeok Sagun returned to his original place and lowered his head with an expression of shame.

“It seems the Young Alliance Leader has been won over by the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader.”

If Baek Jagang had not met Geom Mugeuk beforehand, he would have been listening to this report with a different heart.

With the evidence this clear, would he truly not have suspected Bi Sain?

He did not think so. He would have thought that Bi Sain had been won over by the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader.

Thinking that made him feel a chill.

Whether he believed this Hyeok Sagun had betrayed him, or believed Geom Mugeuk had framed Hyeok Sagun as a traitor.

Of course, Baek Jagang was still standing in the middle. This entire matter could still be part of some grand scheme by the Demonic Cult.

“How do you think we should handle it?”

“We should strip Commander Beon of his position and have him remove his hands from the Extreme Blade Corps.”

“Let us do that. However, we will handle it after meeting the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader.”

“As you wish. Then I shall take my leave.”

As Hyeok Sagun bowed politely and turned to leave, Baek Jagang spoke to him.

“It is hard to believe. That Sain has joined hands with the Demonic Cult.”

“I feel the same.”

“Organize your opinion on whom we should choose as the next successor and report it to me.”

“Understood.”

As Hyeok Sagun turned and walked out, a faint smile formed on his face.

Of course, he did not know.

That the gaze Baek Jagang directed at his back was not that of an Alliance Leader, but the gaze of King Yama holding a scale and watching which way it would tilt.

* * *

Geom Mugeuk stood atop the Thousand-Fathom Cliff.

Looking down into the bottomless depths below, he fearlessly threw himself off the cliff.

Shiiiiiiik.

Geom Mugeuk fell rapidly. As if he had lost consciousness, he simply plummeted defenselessly. No matter that it was him, if he fell defenselessly from this height, he would certainly die.

The reason he was doing something so perilous was to test how the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art would respond in such a case.

‘Protective art, if I fall like this, I’ll die. Are you just going to watch?’

Geom Mugeuk believed in the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art.

But the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art did not activate. Do you trust me? Don’t tell me you know this isn’t a real crisis? Or is your role to keep me alive only after I crash into the ground and end up half-dead?

It was now time to raise his internal energy and use his lightness art.

Geom Mugeuk held out until the very end.

‘I’m really going to die at this rate!’

And at the truly final moment.

The internal energy within Geom Mugeuk’s body moved on its own.

Whiriririk.

It twisted his body in midair and set him upright, moving his hands and feet by itself.

Using the Heavenly Demon Flying Art he had already learned, it instantly reduced his falling speed, and soon he landed lightly. It was movement carried out on its own by the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art, which had reached Great Completion.

“Whew.”

Geom Mugeuk let out a sigh of relief.

“You rascal, you nearly scared me to death.”

But soon Geom Mugeuk cheered. He had been happy even in front of Baek Jagang, but that happiness had not even been a tenth of his true joy.

Geom Mugeuk jumped up and down beneath the cliff in delight.

“This is the martial art of the Heavenly Demon!”

His voice rang out thunderously. No matter what kind of life he had lived before his regression, Geom Mugeuk too was a martial artist. What delighted him most was still achievement in martial arts. And since this was none other than the Heavenly Demon’s exclusive martial art, his joy was beyond words.

Now that it had reached Great Completion, even if he fell into a deeper sleep, it would wake him on its own if it sensed killing intent, and in moments of danger, it would react even more surely to protect him.

Not only that, there were surely more effects of Great Completion that he had yet to experience.

“I’ll be counting on you from now on, protective art.”

Father had said it.

That if he achieved Great Completion in the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art, he would never die.

It was as if I had gained another life.

He also believed that Great Completion of the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art would have a positive effect on the Nine Flames Demonic Art as well. Fundamentally, the two martial arts were very close in nature.

Now only Great Completion of the twelfth level of the Nine Flames Demonic Art remained.

If only I can set the Heavenly Demon Soul before my eyes. And not a Heavenly Demon Soul at the Great Completion of the tenth level, but one that has achieved Great Completion of the twelfth level.

For that moment, I must raise the realm of the Sicheon Secret Art even higher. As I am now, there is not enough time.

I am moving forward like this, one step at a time.

Was it because he had achieved Great Completion of the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art? Today, he missed his father more than usual.

If I told him I had achieved Great Completion, he would say, “Shall we test it once?” and clench his fist first. Even if I were struck by that tremendous fist, I would not mind.

Father, I miss you!

* * *

The next day, Geom Mugeuk left the safe house to meet the Sado Alliance Leader.

“Be careful.”

“Your eyes are very swollen.”

“More useless nonsense! We don’t know what Hyeok Sagun may have plotted!”

“It’s not that we don’t know what he may have plotted. He has already plotted it. He must have given the Sado Alliance Leader evidence that you and I joined hands to devise this conspiracy. That is the only way he can drive me away from you.”

Bi Sain let out a light sigh.

“If I could go, I would go with you.”

He missed the Sado Alliance Leader. Since meeting Baek Jagang, this was the first time he had ever wanted to see him this badly.

“Do not rush. Our opponent has spent long years setting this conspiracy in motion. Do not try to catch up to all of it at once.”

Bi Sain could tell that Geom Mugeuk and Baek Jagang had something in common.

The more crucial the moment, the more calmly they tried to act. They did not seek answers from the outside. They trusted themselves and found the answer within themselves.

“If by any chance you get the opportunity, please tell the Alliance Leader this. That I will live as he told me to.”

“I will.”

With that, Geom Mugeuk left the safe house.

Bi Sain did not return to his room, but watched him depart.

At that moment, Goeak came over and stood beside Bi Sain.

“Do not be too anxious. Soon, an opportunity will come for you as well, Young Alliance Leader.”

“It is all right even if no opportunity comes. Until two days ago, my heart was the most impatient in all the martial world, but now I am no longer in a hurry.”

Goeak looked at him in puzzlement, not understanding what he meant. Bi Sain gave him a peaceful smile.

“Because now is not the time to draw my sword, but the time to wrap my bandages. And the sword drawn by one who has wrapped his own bandages will be far more frightening.”

Absolute Regression Chapter 364

Chapter 364: This Is the First Lie.
-----------------------------------
Two people were walking down the corridor.

The one in front was the captain of the guard, Ingung, and following behind him was Geom Mugeuk.

When they had nearly reached the Alliance Leader’s Hall, Geom Mugeuk stopped for a moment and looked up at the steel wolf.

“So this is the famous wolf of the Sado Alliance. I heard that when a crisis comes, this wolf comes to life and stops intruders. Is that really true?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Ingung gave no answer. Even while guiding Geom Mugeuk, he had not uttered a single word. He was someone who rarely showed himself to begin with, and today was a special case.

“Wolf, if you do come to life later, don’t bite me.”

As he said that, Geom Mugeuk suddenly thrust his hand into the wolf’s mouth.

Then, all of a sudden.

“Uaaaaaagh!”

Geom Mugeuk let out a scream filled with pain.

Despite the realistic acting, Ingung merely stared at Geom Mugeuk in silence.

Smacking his lips, Geom Mugeuk pulled his hand out of the wolf’s mouth.

“You were so quiet that I tried playing a little prank.”

With a blunt expression, Ingung began walking again, and Geom Mugeuk followed after him. What he had sensed inside the wolf’s mouth was a strange energy that only an expert of his level could perceive.

The great doors opened, and the two entered the Alliance Leader’s Hall.

Ingung naturally stepped back behind Geom Mugeuk, and then at some point vanished. It was a high-level concealment art, the sort one might see only from Hwi, his father’s guard.

Far in the distance, the Sado Alliance Leader, Baek Jagang, was seated in a taishi chair.

As Geom Mugeuk walked toward him, he said,

“In our cult’s Cult Leader’s Hall, a red carpet is laid along this path. Everyone calls that carpet the Path of Blood. I don’t know the exact reason—whether it’s because the path to becoming the Heavenly Demon is too arduous, or because countless lives and deaths have been decided by the hands of those who come and go along that carpet.”

From far away, Baek Jagang replied,

“Have you ever lifted that carpet and looked underneath?”

“No.”

“Beneath it, there are likely mechanisms hidden that even experts cannot avoid.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk stopped walking, crouched down, and examined the floor closely.

“There are some here too!”

The floor was densely dotted with tiny holes from which poisoned needles could be fired. If poisoned needles were launched from these holes all at once, it seemed unlikely that even the greatest expert would survive. It was poison guarding the Alliance Leader’s Hall; how could it be the sort of poison solved with a single mouthful of antidote?

“It is more trustworthy than people.”

True. If the Sado Alliance Leader trusted people, that would be the stranger thing.

“When I return, I’ll be sure to lift the carpet.”

Geom Mugeuk stood up again and continued walking.

After reaching the foot of the taishi chair, he gave a courteous bow.

“I never imagined there would come a day when I entered the Sado Alliance Leader’s Hall.”

“The same goes for me. I never imagined I would receive you here.”

“As expected, Alliance Leaders look the most impressive when seated in a taishi chair.”

He acted as comfortably as though he had stepped into the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Cult Leader’s Hall.

“Are you not afraid of me?”

“I am. After my father, you are the person I fear most.”

“Then why do you behave as though you are not afraid?”

“In my experience, those who felt frightening to me never actually harmed me. The people who tried to harm me were all smiling with pleasant faces.”

Baek Jagang deliberately smiled. His eyes curved like crescent moons. Short crescent moons.

“Like this?”

“That puts me even more at ease.”

“Why?”

“Because when you smile, Alliance Leader, you become even scarier.”

How could one possibly defeat Geom Mugeuk with words?

‘How did a son like this come from under that taciturn man, Geom Ujin?’

Once the greetings were over, Geom Mugeuk asked with a serious expression,

“Whom did Hyeok Sagun name?”

“He named the person who follows me best.”

The betrayal of Beon Cheon, commander of the Extreme Blade Corps, had been an even greater shock than hearing that Bi Sain had been bewitched by Geom Mugeuk.

“There is even evidence that you people schemed this from behind the scenes. Despite that, why should I trust you?”

“Do not trust me. Trust your disciple.”

Geom Mugeuk conveyed Bi Sain’s words to the Alliance Leader.

“He said he would live as you told him. He must have cried a great deal over what you said to him, because his eyes were swollen. With that face, and then his eyes swollen on top of it… I had to force myself not to tease him for being ugly.”

Baek Jagang stared straight into Geom Mugeuk’s eyes and said,

“I trust Sain. It is you I cannot trust.”

“If I had plotted in order to seize the Sado Alliance, then among all the many schemes available, I would not have planned something that split apart a master and disciple to gain something from it.”

“Why not?”

There were many reasons he could have given, but Geom Mugeuk chose the most emotional one.

“Because it has no style.”

He had expected an eccentric answer might come, but because it had no style? One corner of Baek Jagang’s mouth curled upward.

Geom Mugeuk continued.

“If it were me, I would have sent you to the dueling ground. In that field where you called me, I would have said, Let us fight one bout like martial artists. Whoever wins takes this murim! That is my way. That is why I do not dream of things like the unification of murim. It is a dream that can never be achieved with such a naive heart.”

After a brief pause, Baek Jagang asked,

“Is that not why it can be achieved?”

Geom Mugeuk saw it. In that fleeting instant, a certain heat flared in Baek Jagang’s small pupils.

He too thought similarly. Let us fight one bout, and the side that wins takes everything. He too was that kind of man.

“I thought you viewed murim with tremendous coldness and realism, Alliance Leader, but you possessed an emotional side like this as well.”

Baek Jagang gave no answer. In truth, it was a point he had never once considered. There had been no one who dared speak of the duality within him.

“If that is the case, Alliance Leader, you too should wake up from your dream of unifying murim!”

A shout burst from Baek Jagang’s mouth.

“You wretch!”

Baek Jagang’s cry rang out thunderously.

“How dare you say such a thing in my Alliance Leader’s Hall!”

Outwardly, he was angry, but Baek Jagang was not angry at all. When would he ever hear someone tell him to wake up from his dream? It had never happened until now, and it never would again.

“I misspoke! Please forgive me.”

What was more interesting was the look in Geom Mugeuk’s eyes, which remained relaxed even as he apologized. Just as Baek Jagang was not truly angry, Geom Mugeuk was not truly apologizing.

He was reading Baek Jagang’s mind precisely. Though this was only their second meeting, it was as if they had known each other for a long time.

“The reason it is difficult to believe my words is probably this. Did someone really try to devour the Sado Alliance? Even pushing aside the chief strategist? Is that not it?”

Now came the move Geom Mugeuk had been saving.

“Do you know this? Our cult, the Murim Alliance, and even the Pungcheon Cult beyond the borders suffered similar incidents.”

Baek Jagang, who had been maintaining his composure, sprang up from his taishi chair. This time, he could not hide his astonishment. What is this now? That emotion was plainly revealed on Baek Jagang’s face.

“There was such a hidden force. An organization plotting against the entirety of murim at the same time.”

To Baek Jagang, who could not possibly believe it, Geom Mugeuk told him what the greater problem was.

“Your problem, Alliance Leader, is not the fact that such a hidden force exists.”

“Then what is the problem?”

“The greater problem is that you have not received any reports whatsoever related to it. Why did Hyeok Sagun not report this to you?”

“!”

“There must be spies who reported it, so you should certainly be able to confirm the contents. If they were so thorough as to eliminate not only the spies who reported it but even the subordinate strategists as well, then secretly seek cooperation from our cult and the Murim Alliance. They will surely inform you of the facts officially.”

If that matter turned out to be true, it would be undeniable evidence that Hyeok Sagun was behind this incident. The mere fact that a strategist had not conveyed such an important matter was, in itself, a grave crime.

Baek Jagang immediately summoned the captain of the guard, Ingung, and gave him instructions through sound transmission. After receiving the order, Ingung vanished in an instant. When it came to handling matters, the Sado Alliance Leader did not hesitate in the slightest.

He had heard Geom Mugeuk’s words and ordered an investigation, but that did not mean he completely trusted Geom Mugeuk yet. This matter would decide not only his fate, but the fate of the Sado Alliance as well.

“Supposing your words are correct, and Hyeok Sagun is the head, then who is the body?”

“Hwang Seokgyeong, an instructor at the Sado Alliance Academy.”

Baek Jagang knew who Hwang Seokgyeong was. The fact that he knew the name of a mere instructor likely meant that the man was performing his duties that well.

“Then why did you not tell me that when we first met?”

Because it was a problem that had to be unraveled one piece at a time.

“He is an extremely dangerous man. I judged that it was right not to touch him rashly and to inform you when the time came.”

“And now the time has come?”

“At the very least, you are half-believing and half-doubting me now.”

Their gazes tangled in midair.

“This is why I cannot help suspecting you.”

“This is why you should trust me. Would someone like me have schemed in such an obvious way?”

It was only their second meeting, but Baek Jagang was agreeing with Geom Mugeuk’s words. He thought that if it were Geom Mugeuk, he would have been more meticulous.

“Strategist Hyeok will arrive soon.”

At last, the time for the three-way confrontation had come. The fact that the two of them had not been waiting here from the beginning was enough to guess that Baek Jagang’s trust in Bi Sain was not small.

“Ah! I would like to call my strategist too! I may speak well and look clever, but I am more on this side.”

As he said that, he tapped the Black Demon Sword at his waist.

Baek Jagang thought it was nothing more than pointless banter, but Geom Mugeuk was truly thinking of Gowol. From now on, he would have to win a war of words against the deputy strategist of the Sado Alliance.

A short while later, Hyeok Sagun entered.

Hyeok Sagun, who walked in calmly, paid his respects to Baek Jagang and then greeted Geom Mugeuk first.

“I am Hyeok Sagun, serving as deputy strategist.”

“I am Geom Mugeuk of the Divine Cult.”

Hyeok Sagun studied Geom Mugeuk closely, but it was impossible to discern anything from the outward appearance of Geom Mugeuk, who had reached Return to Simplicity.

“I have heard many rumors that the Young Cult Leader’s talent is exceptional.”

“Rumors tend to be exaggerated.”

“I do not think that is necessarily so. After committing such a deed, you still have the nerve to come to our alliance like this.”

“What deed are you saying I committed?”

“Did you not bewitch the Young Alliance Leader?”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and nodded.

“I admit it. He was a person I liked very much.”

“Then do you admit that the Divine Cult was involved behind this incident?”

“What does it matter to me whether that is so or not? However, if that were the case, would not the Young Alliance Leader, who barely escaped death, be far too wronged?”

“I know that the attack on the safe house was a self-directed play.”

“If, as you say, the Young Alliance Leader and I joined hands, why would we do such a thing? We would have quietly aimed for that person over there.”

“You tell me. Why did you do it?”

Baek Jagang sat in the center like a judge, watching the verbal battle between the two men.

“There is clear evidence that you people were involved.”

“I have evidence too.”

“What evidence?”

“The clear evidence you spoke of is my evidence.”

“What on earth does that mean?”

“If we had schemed this, there is no way such clear evidence would have emerged.”

Hyeok Sagun snorted.

“Do you think such absurd sophistry will work? You should produce evidence as well.”

“Very well. I will produce evidence.”

At those words, Hyeok Sagun grew inwardly tense. Could there really be evidence?

After heightening the tension as much as possible, Geom Mugeuk abruptly said,

“I saw it.”

“What?”

“I saw you scheming this.”

Because he had not expected Geom Mugeuk to point to him, Hyeok Sagun was inwardly greatly startled.

“Are you trying to frame me now?”

“I am not framing you. I am saying I saw you scheming this incident.”

With a smile, Hyeok Sagun looked at Baek Jagang and said,

“As you can see, the Young Cult Leader is forcing an unreasonable argument.”

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk struck at his opening.

“Why do you not ask? Under ordinary circumstances, should you not ask when and what I saw you scheming? Would an innocent person not usually ask that first? Or did you omit it because you already know what matter I am referring to?”

For an instant, Hyeok Sagun thought, Ah, damn it. He should have done exactly as Geom Mugeuk said, but he had been impatient.

“That is because it is not worth speaking of.”

Baek Jagang, who had been watching, said in a low voice,

“Your testimony cannot be evidence.”

When Baek Jagang took his side, Hyeok Sagun smiled faintly in triumph. As long as there was clear evidence, Baek Jagang would ultimately have no choice but to trust him. The more dazzling the demonic cult’s Young Cult Leader’s eloquence became, the greater the suspicion would grow.

“You should leave now. The only reason I am letting you live is because you are the Young Cult Leader. If you go any further here, then even your position as the Divine Cult’s Young Cult Leader will no longer protect you.”

“Very well. I will go.”

It seemed strange that he was giving up so easily.

“But even if I go, I will meet the people who are on their way before I leave.”

“Who is supposed to be coming?”

Then astonishing words flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“Our cult’s Demon Physician and the Poison King are on their way.”

Hyeok Sagun flinched. The Demon Physician was known to possess medical arts superior even to the divine physicians of the Sado Alliance or the Murim Alliance. And the Poison King as well?

“Although your chief strategist is said to have stepped down due to old age, I believe he fell ill because of poison. The Poison King and the Demon Physician will confirm it.”

“!”

In that instant, even Hyeok Sagun was shaken. Geom Mugeuk had struck at a point he had never imagined.

“Those two are on your side, so how can they be trusted?”

“Of course they are people of our cult. However, they are people who value their own honor more than anyone else.”

“If they received the Cult Leader’s order, they could lie.”

“That is for the Alliance Leader to judge.”

The gazes of both men turned toward Baek Jagang.

The person most astonished in that place was not Hyeok Sagun, but Baek Jagang.

When Geom Mugeuk said that the Poison King and the Demon Physician were coming, goosebumps rose around his ears. He had realized that Geom Mugeuk was lying.

As it turned out, Geom Mugeuk was someone his senses worked on. He could guess why Geom Mugeuk had lied just now. It must have been to drive Hyeok Sagun into a corner with the Demon Physician and the Poison King.

What shocked Baek Jagang was the fact that this was his first lie. If that was so, then?

—I truly consider the Young Alliance Leader my friend. I hope that friend safely inherits the position of Alliance Leader.

—I will wake my father as well. I will ask him to dream a different dream.

—Our murim will not fight one another or hate one another. We will play around on the beach, flirting with young ladies.

—There is a regular place I go to in front of our cult. If there is an opportunity next time, I would very much like to bring you there, Alliance Leader.

The words Geom Mugeuk had spoken flashed through his mind.

‘Everything he has said until now was sincere?’

Absolute Regression, Chapter 365

Chapter 365: Just Thinking About It Makes Me Angry.
---------------------------------------------------
Baek Jagang couldn't believe it.

‘Does the current Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult truly harbor such thoughts?’

That he genuinely considered Bisain a friend, genuinely wished not to fight one another, and genuinely wanted to buy him a drink?

That Geom Muguk, rumored to be the greatest successor among all past Heavenly Demons? That Young Cult Leader who had even charmed the Young Alliance Lord and his maid?

Hyeok Sagun was the first to read the disbelief in Baek Jagang's eyes.

‘Perfect!’

He misinterpreted that disbelief as follows:

How could one believe the words of the Demon Physician or the Poison King when they came?

‘Refuse him! Hurry and say it cannot be done!’

Hyeok Sagun was inwardly anxious. It wasn't just one of them—the Poison King or the Demon Physician—but if both came and examined the patient, the use of poison would definitely be discovered.

‘Then even I will be unable to help. For your own sake, refuse him.’

Meanwhile, Geom Muguk also read the disbelief in Baek Jagang's eyes. Of course, Geom Muguk didn't know of Baek Jagang's ability to detect lies. That was why he desperately tried to persuade him.

—It was a lie to corner them. Please believe me, just this once. Please pretend to accept the Demon Physician and the Poison King.

Even without such a confessional voice transmission, Baek Jagang now believed Geom Muguk. Because he clearly knew who was speaking the truth.

Soon, Baek Jagang spoke. Of course, since Hyeok Sagun was present, he did not readily agree.

"If, after those two come and verify, it is revealed that he is not poisoned, what will you do?"

Geom Muguk could tell that Baek Jagang intended to grant his request. They were people who wouldn't come anyway; what was needed now was to offer conditions that even Hyeok Sagun could accept.

"I will cut my cult's forces in the Gangseo region by half."

The Gangseo region was a strategic point where the Cheonma Divine Cult confronted the Sa Do Maeng.

"Can you put that in an official document?"

"I can."

Baek Jagang felt goosebumps rise along his ears again. His senses were functioning all too well.

As if to prove it, Geom Muguk's voice transmission flew in right after.

—I can't do that. If I did that without Father's permission, I'd be expelled that very day! Perhaps if you were to adopt me as your son, then I might.

Baek Jagang forced down the corners of his mouth that threatened to rise. What an incorrigible lad. Cracking such jokes at a critical moment like this.

"Very well. Then let's have them verify upon arrival."

Hyeok Sagun revealed his dissatisfaction at this unexpected decision.

"Surely you are not believing the Young Cult Leader's words right now?"

Baek Jagang fixed his narrow eyes on Hyeok Sagun. In that moment, Hyeok Sagun realized he had spoken rashly. After all, if his own words were true, there was no reason to get agitated.

If Geom Muguk had proposed such conditions, as a military strategist, he should have welcomed this decision instead. Having the Cheonma Divine Cult's forces in the Gangseo region halved would bring immense benefit to the Sa Do Maeng.

"I will follow the Alliance Lord's will."

Baek Jagang asked Geom Muguk,

"When will they arrive?"

"It will take about ten days or so."

He deliberately claimed as much time as possible. It was born from the hope that Bisain would recover as much as he could from his injuries to fight together. This wasn't his fight, but Bisain's fight, and also Baek Jagang's and the Sa Do Maeng's fight.

"Very well, you shall stay in the Alliance until then."

Geom Muguk looked at Hyeok Sagun with a suspicious gaze.

"In the meantime, the Deputy Strategist might kill the Chief Strategist to destroy the evidence."

Geom Muguk had struck the nail on the head. Hyeok Sagun had truly been pondering how to get rid of the Chief Strategist without arousing suspicion.

"What nonsense are you spouting? The Chief Strategist is like a teacher to me."

"Exactly. Then why did you do such a thing to your teacher?"

Hyeok Sagun glared at Geom Muguk with cold eyes.

He had handled even those clever strategists with ease, but Geom Muguk, far younger than them, was strangely suffocating and difficult to deal with.

Was it because of the status of the Demonic Cult's Young Cult Leader?

No. This brat had something about him that made people's hearts feel suffocated. The kind of person who, even if he were an innkeeper, would somehow make it difficult to place a simple order.

Baek Jagang put an end to the taut battle of wills as they stared at each other.

"Stay near the Alliance until the Poison King and the Demon Physician arrive."

"Thank you, Alliance Lord."

Hyeok Sagun felt the tide shifting. A new phase had begun.

"Let us stop here for today. Strategist Hyeok, remain for a moment."

Sending Geom Muguk out and keeping Hyeok Sagun behind? It appeared as though he trusted Hyeok Sagun, but the opposite was true. Now was the time to soothe and placate the enemy.

"Because that young brat is putting on a show of bravado, we've come to reap great benefits."

The term used for Geom Muguk was no longer Young Cult Leader, but brat.

In other words, it revealed that he trusted himself, not Geom Muguk.

‘If that's the case, he shouldn't have accepted the Poison King and the Demon Physician.’

Hyeok Sagun revealed his frustrated heart under the guise of concern for the Sa Do Maeng.
