—At the very least, you won’t leave behind words like “This feels disgusting,” as Sinwoldo did. You’ll leave behind different words. I’ll listen carefully until the very end.

—I’ll live longer than you. I’ll show you the tenacity of a man from the Unorthodox Path.

Goeak closed his eyes and began circulating his qi and regulating his breathing.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Baek Jagang.

Feeling the gaze directed at him, Baek Jagang looked over.

After drawing his attention that way, Geom Mugeuk immediately looked toward the Battle King. It was as though he were saying:

I’ll take care of the Battle King now, so please watch over your disciple for a moment.

The Battle King was someone whom they could not afford to take their eyes off for even a moment.

At the same time, Geom Mugeuk sent a voice transmission.

—What you see may not be everything. This is a conspiracy plotted against none other than the Alliance Lord himself.

Geom Mugeuk then gave an extreme example.

—Our father might even walk out of those bushes behind us.

How could he speak so cheerfully even while reminding him of the danger?

Baek Jagang felt an intense jealousy toward Geom Ujin once again.

‘You’ve struck it rich in the child-rearing business. I’ll give you that.’

Not merely a good harvest—he acknowledged it as an abundant harvest.

‘But isn’t farming something you do every year?’

There was hope. Goeak had stepped forward for Bisa-in’s sake. The fact that he had gained such a difficult man also meant that he had finally become worthy of obtaining a true ally.

Geom Mugeuk sent another voice transmission.

—You must not be injured by even a single hair. I’ll help as well.

Since he was repeating what he had said earlier, Geom Mugeuk truly seemed intent on bringing this situation to an end in that manner.

‘Very well. I’ll enjoy your abundant harvest this year.’

Yes, if he wanted to compete properly with Geom Ujin in the child-rearing business—

—Very well. I won’t be injured by even a single hair.

The atmosphere on the Unorthodox Alliance’s side was good, but things were different on the Battle King’s side.

With the Poison Tyrant dead and Sinwoldo dead as well, the morale of the Battle Wolves plummeted to the floor.

No matter how much they enjoyed fighting, they did not enjoy fighting battles they were bound to lose.

They were men who could make their opponents bow their heads with nothing but a look, but that gaze no longer worked. Their opponents were the warriors of the Thirteen Wolves of the Unorthodox Path and the Extreme Blade Corps—men who might or might not win even with morale soaring to the heavens.

Naturally, everyone’s gaze turned toward the Battle King.

He did not waver in the slightest. Nor did he try to encourage the Battle Wolves or forcibly raise their morale. Instead, that very composure eased their anxiety.

The Battle King spoke respectfully to the Azure Killing Ghost.

“Senior, please step forward and take care of this.”

The Battle King trusted his abilities. He was worried about the man’s hot temper and foul disposition, but the Azure Killing Ghost had weathered many storms, so he was not overly concerned.

The Azure Killing Ghost stepped forward and spoke to the warriors of the Unorthodox Alliance.

“You picked one of ours, so surely we can pick one of yours now, can’t we? Surely you’re not going to let this old man frighten you into swarming me like a pack of dogs? You bastards, your Alliance Lord is watching!”

He grinned, baring the few teeth he had left.

A faint smile appeared at the corner of the Battle King’s lips. Yes, just like that.

Baek Jagang’s expression hardened instead. He knew there was no one here who could face the Azure Killing Ghost one-on-one. Moreover, the old man would not pick Geom Mugeuk.

The Azure Killing Ghost spoke to Goeak.

“How about you step forward first? You, the one running around after killing all seven of the Seven Great Masters of the Unorthodox Path, trying to become the greatest master of the Unorthodox Path.”

Perhaps he had shared a personal relationship with Sinwoldo, for he was picking a fight with Goeak.

But Goeak was not someone who could be trifled with. His eyes, which had been closed, opened.

“And what are you standing there to gain?”

The Azure Killing Ghost’s expression twitched.

“Did someone who has been named a public enemy of our Alliance really expect to be treated like a senior? How filthy must one’s life have been to become a public enemy of both the orthodox and unorthodox paths?”

The Azure Killing Ghost advanced one step, murderous intent spilling from him.

Bisa-in stepped in front of Goeak as though to shield him.

“Stand back.”

The Azure Killing Ghost gave him a contemptuous look.

“Little Alliance Lord, unless that hideous face of yours has somehow transformed into martial prowess, don’t you dare stand in my way.”

The Azure Killing Ghost deliberately provoked Bisa-in. His target had never been Goeak in the first place—it had been Bisa-in. Knowing that the two shared a close relationship, he had picked a fight with Goeak in order to draw Bisa-in out.

If the enraged Bisa-in charged at him, he intended to take him hostage. Even if Bisa-in rushed forward only ten steps, the Azure Killing Ghost planned to snatch him up like a hawk.

With the Poison Tyrant and Sinwoldo dead, it was the best way to turn the tide. If they held Bisa-in hostage, the Unorthodox Alliance would be unable to fight a melee. He had devised a plan to eliminate them one by one, then finally kill the Alliance Lord together with the Battle King.

“No one has probably ever been honest with you, Little Alliance Lord, so I’ll tell you. Your face is disgusting to look at.”

When had Bisa-in ever heard someone say his face was disgusting? He felt terrible. In the past, he might have fallen for such provocation. He truly was angry.

But Bisa-in was different now. How would Geom Mugeuk react? It was natural to feel angry, but surely he would not let that emotion influence his actions.

“Someone once told me that this face of mine was handsome.”

“Who in the world said that?”

Bisa-in looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk spoke with an awkward expression.

“…I was joking.”

Bisa-in laughed. He was pleased with himself for having become someone capable of laughing at a moment like this. He was certain this change would make him stronger in the future.

He was also grateful to Geom Mugeuk, who had formed a relationship with him in which he did not feel offended even upon hearing that it had been a joke. That considerate man always examined his feelings with care. After joking around, he never failed to add words like these:

“You do look somewhat handsome when you smile like that.”

Because all the warriors of the Unorthodox Alliance were watching, Geom Mugeuk praised Bisa-in once more.

“Someone who doesn’t know how to look at a person’s face will see only your scars, but someone who does know how to look will recognize how handsome the face beneath those scars is.”

He was not talking about seeing Bisa-in’s inner self. Geom Mugeuk hoped these words would work like magic. He hoped someone would say, “Now that I look again, it’s not so bad.” He hoped the number of such people would gradually increase.

“And your smile resembles Lord Fist Demon, the Demon Venerable of our sect, quite a lot. After my father’s, it’s my favorite smile.”

How could Bisa-in not be grateful when Geom Mugeuk said such things in front of everyone? After meeting him and hearing words like these again and again, his long-standing inferiority complex had begun to fade. At this moment, he felt so grateful and happy that he thought he might even be able to cast it all off after today.

“Then I suppose I’ll have to create more reasons to smile.”

“Isn’t that up to you?”

The Azure Killing Ghost, who had been listening to their conversation, felt as though he had somehow been drawn into it. Rather than provoking his opponent, he seemed to have helped improve the relationship between the two men, which left a foul taste in his mouth. In any case, he had failed to take Bisa-in hostage.

The Azure Killing Ghost’s gaze turned toward his original target.

“You come out.”

The opponent he selected was Beoncheon, commander of the Extreme Blade Corps.

“I’ll face him.”

Beoncheon was not frightened by the Azure Killing Ghost in the slightest. That was the spirit of the man named Beoncheon. It was why he led the Extreme Blade Corps, the elite force directly under the Alliance Lord.

The Unorthodox Alliance had countless divisions and corps. But whenever a situation like today’s arose—when it was impossible to know whom to trust—the first organization mobilized was the Extreme Blade Corps.

And Beoncheon was the head of that organization. Thus, even if the Battle King had stepped forward alongside the Azure Killing Ghost instead, he would not have been frightened in the least.

For that reason, Baek Jagang had to stop this fight. No matter how formidable Beoncheon was, he could not withstand the internal power of the Azure Killing Ghost, a master of the previous generation.

But if Baek Jagang stepped forward to stop them, the Azure Killing Ghost would mock Beoncheon’s honor and pride with every kind of insulting remark. Since his subordinates were watching, he had to respond carefully.

Without realizing it, Baek Jagang looked toward Geom Mugeuk. He hated to admit it, but at this moment, he felt that he was relying on him.

And Geom Mugeuk once again began to live up to the Alliance Lord’s expectations.

Geom Mugeuk spoke respectfully to the Azure Killing Ghost.

“Before the two of you fight, may I say one thing to our old master?”

“What is it?”

“To be honest, if we’re comparing who is uglier, Senior, you are uglier than the Little Alliance Lord.”

“What?”

The Azure Killing Ghost had not expected anything like that. His bewildered expression crumpled into a frown.

“I’m saying this because if you die in the fight, you might return without ever learning the truth.”

The idea of dying to Beoncheon? That too was sure to irritate him. But the words that bothered him even more were these:

“Are you saying I’m uglier than the Little Alliance Lord?”

“That’s right.”

Geom Mugeuk knew that although the Azure Killing Ghost was now unattractive, with missing teeth and countless wrinkles, he had been called a beautiful young man in his youth.

He was immensely proud of his looks. That was why he had mocked Bisa-in’s appearance as well.

“Even if I imagine you with a young face and compare the two of you, you’re definitely uglier. I’m sorry.”

When Geom Mugeuk even bowed his head and apologized, the Azure Killing Ghost grew angrier still. If he had simply cursed him and shouted that he was uglier, it would have been nothing more than meaningless abuse. But the polite apology made him feel truly wretched.

‘This brat is quite something!’

He knew Geom Mugeuk was deliberately getting under his skin, but he could not avoid it. He simply could not accept being uglier than that Bisa-in—especially after calling that very face disgusting.

“You don’t seem to believe me. Shall we check?”

Geom Mugeuk raised his voice so everyone present could hear.

“Come, then. Let’s see who’s uglier. Those who think the Little Alliance Lord is ugly, raise your hands!”

No one had expected him to ask such a question, and everyone was bewildered.

Baek Jagang was surprised, and even the Battle King was surprised. Not a single person there remained unfazed. An appearance contest, in the middle of this situation?

Naturally, no one on the Unorthodox Alliance’s side raised a hand. Even if they thought so, who would dare raise one? Conversely, some of the Battle Wolves were too caught off guard by the sudden question to raise their hands.

“Then those who think our old master is ugly, raise your hands!”

As soon as Geom Mugeuk finished speaking, he shot his own hand into the air. The Thirteen Wolves of the Unorthodox Path followed suit, and then all the warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps raised their hands as well.

At that moment, Bisa-in’s eyes widened.

Baek Jagang had raised his hand. He had never dreamed that his master would raise his hand for him in such an absurd situation.

‘Master!’

It was only the act of raising a hand, but Bisa-in was deeply moved.

He did not know that Baek Jagang’s action also contained an apology for having doubted him this time.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to the Azure Killing Ghost.

“Look. There are far more people who think our senior is ugly, aren’t there?”

“You bastard, isn’t that only because there are more of you?”

“Isn’t it the same for you?”

“What is?”

“Doesn’t our senior also act however he pleases just because he’s old? He mocks a junior by calling him ugly—isn’t that only because he has more internal power?”

“!”

It was an entirely unexpected remark, and Cheongsal-gwi was unable to respond for a moment. This had been Geom Mugeuk’s true intention all along.

“Earlier, Senior Goe chose someone of a similar age and comparable skill to fight. But you, Old Senior, are different, aren’t you? If you want things to be fair, then we should step forward in numbers equal to your age.”

“What is that supposed to mean?”

“I mean that we’ll field as many people as the sum of your age, so let’s have a match. Or you could boldly admit that you’re uglier than Senior Goe.”

“What kind of madman says such things?”

He had brought up the insult about his appearance to provoke Bisa-in, but instead, he had fallen right into Geom Mugeuk’s provocation.

“Do you think you can defeat me by joining forces? Is that why you’re pulling this ridiculous stunt?”

“Just admit that you’re ugly and fight Danju Beoncheon!”

“What kind of unreasonable nonsense is this?”

“No, I shouldn’t have brought it up. Just continue your duel.”

“You little—!”

“What’s so shameful about a man admitting that he’s ugly?”

In the end, Cheongsal-gwi could no longer endure it and shouted,

“Fine! I’ll kill you all at once.”

The moment his words fell, Bisa-in stepped forward first.

“Let’s see who’s uglier.”

As if he had been waiting for it, Geom Mugeuk followed him out.

“If my friend is going, then I’m going too! We’re still short on age on our side, so I’ll join forces with one more person.”

Geom Mugeuk looked back and forth between Ilang and Beoncheon before speaking to Ilang.

“Danju Beoncheon has already been singled out, and he was even falsely accused this time. Won’t you yield?”

Ilang readily nodded. With Geom Mugeuk and Beoncheon stepping forward, he would not need to. In any case, if Bisa-in found himself in danger, Ilang intended to intervene regardless of any promise. If that was the case, it was better for him to stay out of it from the beginning.

Cheongsal-gwi came to his senses belatedly.

‘Ah, this wasn’t how I planned things.’

He had lost his temper and shouted, and now three people stood in a line before him.

Because of that hot temper of his, he had become a public enemy of the three factions—the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic paths. No matter how old he grew, it never changed.

It was Tuwang who brought the situation under control.

“The ages don’t match.”

Tuwang spoke to Geom Mugeuk in a calm tone.

“You should step out, and Ilang should take your place.”

Geom Mugeuk had expected Tuwang to intervene, so he readily accepted. Tuwang would never permit him to fight alongside them.

“Then I’ll leave it to you, Lord Ilang.”

Ilang took his place instead.

An image arose in Tuwang’s mind: Geom Mugeuk, who had thrown out countless words, jokes, and absurd arguments, returning once more to the battlefield where blood flowed. Standing there alone, staring back at them with eyes of unfathomable depth. Since Cheongsal-gwi could not see that sight, he was being overwhelmed helplessly.

In the place where only Beoncheon was originally meant to stand, Bisa-in and Ilang now stood together.

Geom Mugeuk, who had stepped back, looked toward Baek Jagang.

‘This is as far as I can go to help.’

Absolute Regression, Chapter 371

Chapter 371: Fight with Style.
------------------------------
Baek Jagang was sincerely moved.

Where Beoncheon had been meant to fight alone, three people now stood. They could now attack together without damaging Beoncheon’s honor and pride in front of his subordinates.

Who else could have achieved such a result against that Cheongsalgwi?

He had inwardly scoffed when he heard that someone had been bewitched by the Demon Cult’s young cult leader. Now he felt he had to admit it. How could anyone not be bewitched by such a man?

And more than anything, he was grateful that he had been given the chance to raise his hand for his disciple. When he had raised it, Bi Sain’s rough face had shown plainly just how moved he was. So much so that Baek Jagang felt terribly sorry for ever having doubted such a disciple, even for a time.

That was why he would not trust Geom Mugeuk. Because he now knew that this was a man capable of bewitching even him, he would trust him even less. After ninety-nine truths, a single lie could bring the Sado Alliance to ruin. Even so, he had to convey his gratitude.

Baek Jagang gave Geom Mugeuk a slight nod.

It was not a greeting to the Demon Cult’s young cult leader. Beyond age, beyond status, it was the martial artist named Baek Jagang greeting the martial artist named Geom Mugeuk. Thank you for protecting our honor, and my faith in my disciple.

Geom Mugeuk, too, bowed his head respectfully to him, without the slightest trace of playfulness on his face.

Seeing that, Cheongsalgwi could clearly sense it. From the very beginning, Geom Mugeuk had deliberately created this situation. He had known that Geom Mugeuk had intentionally provoked him, but to think he had been carrying things out at the alliance leader’s request from the start.

If he could, he wanted to unleash a string of curses at Geom Mugeuk beginning with “that filthy tongue of yours,” but it was obvious that it would only be spitting in his own face.

The needless sparks flew toward the three standing before him.

“Did you think the outcome would change just because three of you gathered together? You could have died with dignity, but now you’ll simply die ugly, tangled up with one another.”

Cheongsalgwi thought that, in any case, the result would not change.

Today’s battle would only end when one side was entirely dead. It was merely a matter of order, a difference in the process.

“Who shall I kill first?”

Meanwhile, the three were secretly exchanging voice transmissions. With Bi Sain at the center, he gave instructions to the other two.

—I will take the lead in the middle, so please support me from both sides. We must not be more than two steps apart from one another. In the early exchange, he will undoubtedly try to toy with us. We must not miss that opportunity.

Bi Sain burned with the resolve to kill Cheongsalgwi no matter what.

He did not show it to the other two, but in truth, he was afraid and trembling. Their opponent was a master stronger than they were. On top of that, the leader of the Sado Alliance and the warriors of the Sado Alliance were watching.

He had been through real combat many times, but never once had he trembled as he did today. The decisive reason for his tension was this.

‘If I make a mistake, Illang and Commander Beon will die.’

Just as that worry was making him stiffen, Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission reached him.

—Do not concern yourself with the two of them.

Bi Sain truly almost shouted, “Are you inside my head?”

—If you fight to protect them, you will lose. Whether they die or not, fight as though you are fighting one-on-one. Only then can you win.

—!

Instinctively, he felt that Geom Mugeuk was right. He had to trust the two of them. If he clumsily tried to protect them, all three of them would die.

—Fight splendidly.

Geom Mugeuk’s final piece of advice was truly unexpected.

It was not advice to fight desperately, nor was it advice to fight cautiously.

Splendidly.

He knew very well that it did not simply mean putting on a show. Rather, that ill-fitting word felt like a guidepost showing him how he ought to fight.

‘Yes, splendidly.’

When Bi Sain drew his sword, Beoncheon and Illang drew theirs as well.

The warriors of the Sado Thirteen Wolves and the Extreme Blade Corps, who were cheering them on, all shouted at once.

Not to be outdone, the Turang also roared in unison.

Together with the roar, the one who took the first move was Bi Sain.

He did not use the martial art of the Sado Alliance Leader, of which he had only learned the first form, but instead unleashed his own martial art that he had brought to completion: the Soul-Chasing Four Swords.

When the first sword of the Soul-Chasing Four Swords, Severing Soul, was unleashed, pure white sword qi surged from Bi Sain’s blade like waves.

Cheongsalgwi did not dodge. He released palm force and met the sword qi head-on.

Boom!

With an explosion, the ground shook. As if boasting of his inner force, Cheongsalgwi blocked the attack without taking even a single step back.

At the same time, Beoncheon and Illang launched sword qi from the left and right.

This time as well, Cheongsalgwi remained in place, thrusting both palms to either side and neutralizing their attacks.

A succession of explosions rang out, and clouds of dust rose.

It seemed he would merely mock them and show off his strength like that, but along with the dust, Cheongsalgwi’s figure vanished in an instant.

Swaaaak!

Before anyone knew it, Cheongsalgwi was behind Bi Sain, striking down with a palm.

Bi Sain twisted his body and barely avoided it, but he could not dodge the following palm. It was fast. Far too fast. Just as Bi Sain raised his protective gangqi and braced for the impact—

Illang, who had rushed in before anyone knew it, thrust his sword toward him.

Illang’s sword art was the Heavenly Yang Seven Hands.

Of the seven techniques, he unleashed the fastest: the Fourth Hand, Quick Yang Form.

Because he was even prepared to sacrifice his own body if Bi Sain fell into danger, Illang’s attack was desperate.

Cheongsalgwi abandoned Bi Sain and avoided Illang’s attack. The battle was advantageous to him anyway, so there was no need to force an attack at the cost of an injury.

As he evaded, Beoncheon’s attack flew at him this time.

Beoncheon’s sword, pouring down like a deluge, was fast. His martial art was the Startling Soul Sword Art, a sword technique based on speed and extreme yin.

Swish, swish, swish, swish, swish!

Cheongsalgwi was always at the tip of the sword, but regrettably, the blade could not pierce his body.

Cheongsalgwi was so fast that it was hard to understand how such an old body could move so quickly. His movements were also the answer to how he had survived until now.

The second sword of the Soul-Chasing Four Swords, Quelling Soul, unfolded, and five streams of sword qi bent as if alive, flying toward Cheongsalgwi and enveloping him.

Cheongsalgwi leaped into the air to avoid them, and the sword qi changed direction to pursue him. They were like dragons chasing a dragon pearl, but regrettably, they could not obtain that pearl in this ascension.

Cheongsalgwi changed direction in midair, avoided the sword qi, and shot downward as fast as a beam of light.

The one he targeted this time was Illang.

As he flew downward, Cheongsalgwi released palm force in succession.

Boom! Boooom!

The space where Illang had been standing exploded, and the surface of the ground flipped over.

Illang threw himself aside and barely avoided the attack. In front of him, the rising cloud of dust split apart, and Cheongsalgwi rushed in.

A palm flying in with a pure white glow!

It was the White Bone Heart-Piercing Palm, filled with extreme yin energy.

If it struck, it would cause a grave injury.

‘Too late.’

If he was injured, he would only become a burden in the fight. With the resolve to perish together with his foe, Illang thrust out his sword. He intended to give up his life but take at least one of Cheongsalgwi’s arms in return.

‘I’m sorry, Young Alliance Leader! This is as far as I go.’

At that moment, the White Bone Heart-Piercing Palm changed direction.

The instant he disappeared—

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwak!

Sword qi swept through the place where Cheongsalgwi had been.

It was the third sword of the Soul-Chasing Four Swords, Iron Soul.

Cheongsalgwi could have killed Illang, but if he had done so, he too would have been badly injured, so he turned his body and avoided the attack. This time, Bi Sain had saved Illang.

Illang did not even have the leisure to express his thanks.

Cheongsalgwi was attacking Beoncheon now.

Boooom!

Overpowered in inner force, Beoncheon was shoved backward. At that, Illang flew over, caught him, and poured his internal energy into him. Only then did the two, who had been sliding back, finally come to a stop.

Bi Sain was pouring attacks toward Cheongsalgwi to prevent any follow-up attack.

The other two also joined the offensive. They did not think of what would come afterward. To fight Cheongsalgwi while conserving inner force was arrogance. They had to pour everything out and settle the matter before their inner force was exhausted.

Cheongsalgwi was certainly stronger than they were. Every gesture he extended was a series of killing moves that inevitably led to death.

In the midst of that perilous situation, Bi Sain instead felt hope.

‘This is doable!’

They were coordinating better than he had feared.

Because they were not trying to coordinate, they coordinated all the better.

If they had tried to match their movements from the beginning, it would have been a far more difficult battle. They would have found it hard to interfere recklessly, fearing they might obstruct an ally’s ultimate technique. In the end, instead of a combined attack, it might have become a form where they took turns fighting one by one.

But now it was different.

Bi Sain fought with the mindset of a one-on-one duel, with the mindset that it did not matter even if his sword qi injured Illang or Beoncheon.

The attack that had saved Illang moments ago had also followed Geom Mugeuk’s advice exactly.

It had been an attack to save Illang, but he had not thought of it that way. It was not an attack to save him, but an attack to kill Cheongsalgwi. The result was the same, but Bi Sain was unmistakably fighting a different battle.

And all of this was possible because Illang and Beoncheon were fighting far better than he had expected. In the end, Geom Mugeuk had been right. Trust the two of them and focus only on this fight. That was the best course.

Before anyone realized it, the composure had vanished from Cheongsalgwi’s face.

The battle was not unfolding as easily as he had thought. There was one fact he had overlooked. All three of them were fighting with their lives on the line.

Beoncheon for the honor of the commander of the Extreme Blade Corps. Illang to protect Bi Sain. Bi Sain for the alliance leader and the Sado Alliance.

When the battle did not go his way, Cheongsalgwi vented his anger in an absurd direction.

The palm force that seemed as though it would be fired at Bi Sain instead flew toward the warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps who were watching the fight.

It was an attack he had deliberately launched, far too fast and powerful for them to avoid. Their deaths would disturb Bi Sain’s concentration, and more than anything, he felt that only then would he be satisfied.

Those standing at the front thrust out their palm force and shouted.

“Dodge!”

If they could not avoid it anyway, they had resolved to release as much palm force as possible and at least save the comrades behind them.

Boooom!

An explosion erupted, and dust rose.

The Extreme Blade Corps warriors standing at the front looked around at one another. They were unharmed, though they should have been swept away by the palm force.

The one who had neutralized the attack was Baek Jagang.

With his hand extended toward the air, his gaze was still fixed on the Fighting King.

Baek Jagang slowly withdrew his hand.

All the warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps bowed their heads respectfully. If not for the alliance leader, they would all have died.

The Fighting King could have used that opportunity to attack, but at that moment, Geom Mugeuk raised his energy in warning.

If you go, I go.

The Fighting King did not move.

He was convinced that if he failed to eliminate Geom Mugeuk today, then even if he ascended to the position of Sado Alliance Leader, Geom Mugeuk would become a great source of trouble. If Geom Mugeuk returned after properly preparing, would he truly be able to stop him?

Cheongsalgwi’s attack just now became the signal flare. The warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps charged at the Turang.

“Kill them!”

The warriors on both sides rushed toward one another. They had already reached the peak of fighting spirit while watching the battle. The Sado Thirteen Wolves also joined the fight.

Baek Jagang and the Fighting King did not stop their battle. In any case, they would have had to fight once.

With even the Sado Thirteen Wolves intervening, the Sado Alliance had the advantage both in numbers and in skill.

Even so, the Fighting King only watched. Of course, the moment he intervened, the Sado Alliance Leader and Geom Mugeuk would leap in, and a chaotic melee would unfold. It was not an easy situation to enter, even if he wanted to.

Meanwhile, Bi Sain’s battle was reaching its climax.

Swaaaaaak!

Cheongsalgwi’s fingers, driving in toward Bi Sain, curled.

He was about to use his signature martial art, the Blood-Yin Iron Claw Hand, together with the White Bone Heart-Piercing Palm. Earlier, Bi Sain had nearly died twice to that attack.

The Blood-Yin Iron Claw Hand, so powerful that even a graze would shatter flesh and bone, flew in to tear open Bi Sain’s chest.

At that moment, Bi Sain made one dangerous choice.

Cheongsalgwi’s fingers raked across Bi Sain’s chest, which he had failed to dodge in time.

Kwa-ddeu-ddeu-deu-deuk!

At the very instant his chest was being torn apart—

‘Got him!’

The balance of the fight collapsed. And at that moment, as Cheongsalgwi turned toward Beoncheon with a triumphant smile—

Bi Sain’s sword, which should have been dead with his chest ripped and shredded, flashed.

Shiiiiing!

At an extremely close distance, the fourth sword of the Soul-Chasing Four Swords, Mad Soul, was unleashed.

Puaak!

At that moment, Cheongsalgwi, unable to avoid Gwanghon in time and having taken the blow, staggered as blood spurted from his shoulder.

From the left came Genesis Soul-Shock, the final ultimate technique of the Soul-Shocking Sword Art; from the right came the Great Yang Form, the final ultimate technique of the Heavenly Yang Seven Hands.

Shunk!

He avoided Genesis Soul-Shock, but he could not avoid the Great Yang Form.

The instant he staggered again.

Shuuuunk!

Bi Sain’s sword pierced through his chest.

Impaled on the blade, Cheongsalgwi vomited a gush of blood and stared at Bi Sain in disbelief.

“……You were definitely struck by the Blood-Yin Iron Claw Hand?”

Only then did Cheongsalgwi see Bi Sain’s chest. Two protective armors had been torn away. The layered armor had been ripped apart, but the attack had not reached Bi Sain’s body. His gamble with his life had paid off.

Bi Sain stared straight at him and said quietly,

“Even up close, I’m better-looking.”

“……Damn it!”

Thuk! Shunk!

Immediately after, Illang’s and Beoncheon’s swords flew in and buried themselves in his neck and heart.

Cheongsalgwi died on the spot and toppled over.

The three of them let out long sighs of relief at the same time.

Though it was their first time coordinating, they had succeeded in killing Cheongsalgwi through a joint assault. They looked at one another and exchanged burning gazes. What words were needed? They were comrades who had shared life and death.

Bi Sain turned his head and first looked toward the leader of the Sado Alliance.

Baek Jagang showed him a radiant smile. It was the first time his master had ever smiled so brightly at him. He had a feeling that his relationship with his master would begin anew from this moment on.

Bi Sain’s gaze then turned to Geom Mugeuk.

The reason they had been able to win this battle was because of the voice transmission Geom Mugeuk had sent before the fight. They had won because they had fought not to defend, but to pour everything out. They had won because they had fought as if it were one-on-one, not a coordinated assault.

‘My debt of life keeps piling up, and yet he’s looking at me with such a bright smile.’

Then came Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission.

—Your fight was magnificent.

As if responding to that transmission, all of the Sado Thirteen Wolves and the martial artists of the Extreme Blade Corps burst into cheers at once.

Their battle was already over as well. Since even the Sado Thirteen Wolves had joined the fight, there had been no surprise in the result. All of the Fighting Wolves were dead, while the Sado Alliance’s losses had not been great.

Now only the Fighting King and the deputy strategist, Hyeok Sagun, remained there. Hyeok Sagun stood frozen in silence, while the Fighting King remained calm.

Everyone’s gaze focused on the Fighting King.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to him calmly.

“From the beginning, you didn’t care if all of them died, did you?”

He had been far too calm in the face of his allies’ deaths.

And Geom Mugeuk was certain he had some other move left. It was hard to believe he had simply trusted in his own skill, considering his opponent was the leader of the Sado Alliance.

“What card are you hiding?”

When the Fighting King did not answer, Baek Jagang finally stepped forward.

He walked forward slowly. His presence overwhelmed everyone there.

Baek Jagang slowly drew his sword. From his small eyes began to flow an aura colder and sharper than the blade itself.

“I’ll hear that answer through my sword.”

Absolute Regression Chapter 372

Chapter 372: The Fist Asks and the Sword Answers.
-------------------------------------------------
At last, the decisive moment that would change the fate of the Sado Alliance had arrived.

Baek Jagang standing there, and Baek Jagang with his sword drawn, were two entirely different people.

The air around him began to flow differently.

Baek Jagang had begun to make even the flow of the air his own.

Beoncheon and Illang had their subordinates withdraw far behind them. In any case, no one was allowed to interfere in a fight between the Alliance Leader and the Fighting King, and there was no one with the skill to do so either.

Bisa-in, too, stood beside Geom Mugeuk, silently watching the Alliance Leader and the Fighting King.

It was the first time he had ever watched his master engage in a life-and-death battle with someone. Of course, he believed his master would win. Even so, the reason he trembled like this was not because he doubted the Alliance Leader’s skill.

It was because this was the path he himself had to walk. Someday, when he became the Sado Alliance Leader, a day like this would come for him as well.

What kind of person would he be when he stood there?

Geom Mugeuk felt the Heavenly Demon Protective Art activate on its own. The Heavenly Demon Protective Art, having reached great completion, was warning him that he stood within a space dominated by someone else. He had now reached the realm where mind and martial arts were one.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Fighting King. If he had sensed the change in the air, then the Fighting King must have sensed it too.

Now, what will you do?

The Fighting King slowly raised his hand.

Paang!

With his right fist, he struck the palm of his left hand with force. When he had first confronted the Sado Alliance martial artists here, he had struck his palm with his fist in just that way.

Dooong!

A wave extending from the Fighting King’s hand shook the flow of air that Baek Jagang had seized.

But the space did not break.

Paaang!

With an even louder sound, the surroundings trembled. He tried to force his way into any gap and refresh the air around him, but the space Baek Jagang had created did not break. That space seemed to be saying:

From now on, breathe only with my permission.

Even so, the Fighting King was not frightened in the least. He had his own secret technique to oppose his opponent’s momentum. He focused his mind. Then, in his eyes, everyone other than Baek Jagang began to appear in black and white.

Only Baek Jagang, the one he was to fight, appeared in his original colors, while all others turned monochrome like an ink painting.

That was not all. Within that black and white, if someone drew a sword or used inner power, only that part would be marked in red. Because of that, even when he stepped into a space filled with hundreds of people, he could immediately tell who was the most dangerous opponent.

It was a secret technique of his own, created through training in extreme concentration.

But today, he experienced something unusual.

There was one person from whom he could not remove the color. The one whose color did not fade despite not being his opponent was none other than Geom Mugeuk.

No matter how hard he tried to suppress Geom Mugeuk’s presence, his color would not disappear. The other man’s presence was overcoming his concentration.

‘How in the world?’

When the Fighting King looked at Geom Mugeuk, the gazes of the Sado Alliance martial artists naturally turned toward him as well. Favor flowed in their eyes. After all, he was the one who had saved their lives today.

After looking over the corpses of his subordinates, the Fighting King spoke calmly.

“The variable that is you created this result.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk answered.

“How could their deaths be because of me? They are because of you, who plotted to swallow the Sado Alliance whole. Because of the greed of those who joined hands with you. And because of the will of these people here, who risked their lives to fight you for the Sado Alliance and their comrades.”

Geom Mugeuk was seeing straight through the Fighting King’s heart.

“In any case, weren’t you planning to silence all those subordinates who died earlier once you became the Sado Alliance Leader?”

He had been planning the heroic tale of an instructor from the Samaeng Hall ascending to the position of Sado Alliance Leader. There was no way he would have left alive those who had been used in the process.

“That’s why you were able to be so calm.”

The Fighting King could not deny it. Because it was true. The moment he became Sado Alliance Leader, he had intended to discard everything and fill it all with something new.

“So don’t blame others for no reason, and go properly by the Alliance Leader’s hand.”

Geom Mugeuk bowed his head to Baek Jagang and said,

“Then I leave it to you.”

He asked with utmost courtesy, as though only the Alliance Leader could do this.

“This man can be eliminated only by you, Alliance Leader.”

Before Baek Jagang’s subordinates, Geom Mugeuk showed him the highest respect and courtesy. Suddenly, Baek Jagang recalled something Geom Mugeuk had once said to him.

—My honor does not lie in looking impressive. I want to return safely and see my people. I want to return safely, drink with them, and talk with them.

Baek Jagang looked at Bisa-in. Yes, he too had a reason to return.

The moment Geom Mugeuk finished speaking, the Fighting King saw it. He saw the color drain from Geom Mugeuk’s body. He saw him entrust this fight completely to the Sado Alliance Leader and suppress his own presence.

‘Truly outstanding!’

The Fighting King stretched his body out as if limbering up. He felt good. As long as opponents like these existed, he would be able to fight like a madman. Thinking of the immense pleasure he would savor when killing men like them, he felt a desire to win even if he had to commit any deed to do it.

Pang! Paang! Paaang!

The sound of palm and fist colliding rang out like drumbeats raising the Fighting King’s momentum.

In this battle, Baek Jagang did not yield the first move.

He did not view his opponent as a martial artist, but as an assassin. Since he was someone who would do anything, Baek Jagang intended never to reveal the slightest opening.

The moment Baek Jagang’s form flashed, he was thrusting his sword in front of the Fighting King.

Shiiiiik!

It was a killing attack aimed at the Fighting King’s throat.

Ttaang!

The Fighting King flicked the blade away with his fingers and sent a fist toward Baek Jagang’s chest.

Baek Jagang’s sword, which had been knocked aside, returned like a spring and struck upward in a semicircle.

Thus, the exchange between the two crossed as straight lines and curves.

When the first exchange ended, the Fighting King landed lightly a dozen steps away.

Tuk.

The front of the Fighting King’s robe split open.

Baek Jagang’s sword had been faster, and the Fighting King had abandoned his attack and pulled his body backward.

As he looked down at the severed hem of his clothes, exhilaration flashed across the Fighting King’s face.

“You truly are str—”

Before he could finish speaking, a sword line was passing through where his mouth had been.

Shiiiiik!

As the Fighting King dodged that swift attack, his fist flew toward Baek Jagang’s face. His attack, too, was so fast that his arm could not be seen.

Paang!

The space where Baek Jagang’s face had been exploded. If air had possessed form, whatever it was would have been smashed to pieces by that powerful attack.

Baek Jagang, having avoided the Fighting King’s fist, cut diagonally with his sword. Though it was an attack that minimized the angles from which one could evade, the Fighting King avoided it and even added a counterattack.

Even without using exclusive martial arts that required vast inner power, a terrifying exchange where lives hung in the balance continued.

The confidence that each could kill the other with a single sword stroke or a single fist shot toward one another like light.

To those watching, only dozens of lines could be seen. Formed and vanishing, formed and vanishing. The moment one of those lines touched a body, victory and defeat would be decided in an instant—lines of death.

It was also an exchange that revealed the fundamentals of a martial artist. It was a conversation with sword and fist: thrusting, cutting, dodging, throwing punches—things that could never be understood through flashy techniques alone. The fist asked, and the sword answered. That sound was like the wind, and also like rain.

Peoeok!

Then, with a sound like a drum bursting, the two separated.

“Hoo, hoo.”

The Fighting King’s breathing immediately grew rough. It showed just how exhausting the exchange moments ago had been. By contrast, Baek Jagang’s breathing was steady. That breathing alone revealed whose fundamentals were superior.

Baek Jagang gave the Fighting King no time to rest.

In the instant he flashed forward, Baek Jagang’s sword rushed in, fast and precise.

The Fighting King blocked that perilous attack by striking it aside with the back of his hand.

Shukak! Shukak!

Baek Jagang’s sword began carving away the space. It was a chilling attack that seemed as though life itself would be carved away if one remained there.

The Fighting King took his first step backward.

Just when it seemed he would be pushed back that way, the Fighting King’s fist flew in with unusual lightness.

Ordinarily, it would have been something easily knocked aside with a sword. It was that light of an attack.

But Baek Jagang instinctively sensed danger and desperately twisted his body away from the fist.

Whoooooong!

Along with a tremendous sound of wind.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

The ground and grass behind him were swept away, and a vast trench appeared in a straight line. It was an astonishing attack that erased even the distant trees and rocks.

Had he not realized that the essence of fist arts was contained in that one move that seemed so light, he would have suffered a fairly serious injury.

Bisa-in, who had been watching, clenched his fist tightly. If it had been him, would he have recognized the power contained in that light fist? No. He would not have. It was a level of skill entirely different from the Cheongsalgwi he had fought earlier.

The Fighting King was a heartless man who treated the lives of his subordinates like worn-out shoes, but at least his martial arts were profound.

Baek Jagang recognized his opponent’s martial art.

“Linked Wind and Cloud Fist!”

The martial art of Magyeo, who had once been called the god of the battlefield.

“As expected! You recognize it.”

“If it is the Linked Wind and Cloud Fist, then I can acknowledge it.”

An intense radiance poured from Baek Jagang’s eyes.

To oppose that momentum, the Fighting King stamped his foot down where he stood.

Kwang!

Jjeoooooook!

The ground split in all directions like a spiderweb.

A line cracked open and passed even between Geom Mugeuk and Bisa-in.

Because it happened to pass between them, Bisa-in felt uneasy. Was it foreshadowing some ominous future?

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk lightly leapt over the split line and stood beside him. Then, without a word, he stared at the fight.

‘Ah! This is it.’

He could simply cross over like this, so what had he been agonizing over? What in the world had he been agonizing over?

If someone were to ask what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was, he could now answer. He was someone who crossed the line first.

It had always been this side that felt lacking, yet he was the one who lightly hopped over first. He hopped across and said, let’s be friends. He did not let pride get in the way, wondering whether he should go over or tell the other to come.

Bisa-in watched Geom Mugeuk’s profile.

The way he was absorbed in the battle was the most serious he had ever seen him. This was the face of the martial artist Geom Mugeuk.

‘…Even if he approached me in order to kill me.’

Bisa-in’s gaze also turned to the fight. In that time, he had missed dozens of moves, but that was fine. Learning this one thing—the way he casually leapt over a line—would make him grow far more.

Because to him, that small hop a moment ago was far more difficult than the soaring leap now taking place.

Unable to withstand Baek Jagang’s attack, the Fighting King shot up into the sky.

Baek Jagang soared after him.

The two flew upward at terrifying speed and vanished from sight in an instant. Only Geom Mugeuk, who had mastered the Divine Eye Art, could perceive what was happening up in the sky.

A short while later, someone fell at terrifying speed.

Kwang!

He crashed into the ground.

The one who had fallen with enough impact to cave in the surroundings was the Fighting King.

Lying on his back and facing the sky, he immediately rolled to the side.

Chwaaaak.

A streak of sword energy cut through the place where he had been lying. Had he stayed still, his body would have been split in two; the line created by the sword energy was that straight and deep.

Baek Jagang, who dropped down after him, pressed the attack like a storm.

The Fighting King tried to say something, but Baek Jagang gave him no opportunity.

If it had been the Heavenly Demon he was fighting, they might have talked leisurely, drunk wine in the midst of battle, then fought again. In that way, they might have battled for seven days and nights.

But Baek Jagang did not grant the Fighting King that worth.

Sword and fist crossed the air. The movements of the two were so fast that sound could not keep up with them.

In the end, the Fighting King used a perilous move. In a life-and-death duel, it was always the disadvantaged side that took risks.

Shiiiiik.

Gwaak!

He caught the incoming sword blade with his bare hand. None of those watching had expected him to dare attempt to seize the Sado Alliance Leader’s blade with his bare hand.

At the same time as he caught the blade, his elbow flew toward Baek Jagang’s chest.

Papapak!

Even though Baek Jagang twisted, pulled, and pushed the blade with lightning speed, the palm was not cut.

Shwaaaaaak.

The elbow grazed past Baek Jagang. It was a move he had staked everything on, but Baek Jagang narrowly avoided the attack.

The instant their bodies crossed, the Fighting King’s headbutt aimed for Baek Jagang’s face. It was a move as unexpected as catching the sword with his hand.

Peoeok!

Before anyone knew it, the hilt of Baek Jagang’s sword, which had slipped free from the Fighting King’s hand, struck his face.

At the same time, Baek Jagang’s sword flashed through the air like lightning.

Seogeok.

With a sound that announced the end, the movements of the two men stopped. It was the price of his recklessness in catching the Sado Alliance Leader’s sword with his hand and even attempting a headbutt.

A long cut had been carved across the Fighting King’s chest.

But not a single drop of blood flowed. What had been cut was his clothing.

This time, Baek Jagang’s gaze turned to the Fighting King’s hand. He had clearly cut his palm before cutting his chest, yet not a single drop of blood had appeared.

Still, as though he felt pain, the Fighting King grimaced and rubbed his chest. Then he slowly took off his outer robe.

Revealed beneath it was a treasured garment.

“The Immortal Divine Armor. The greatest protective garment, one that cannot be cut by any sword.”

Then he raised both hands to show them.

“The Fighting God. Gauntlets that may likewise be called the finest. Consider it my courtesy toward you.”

As Geom Mugeuk had expected, he had come wearing protective treasures. And not just any, but the Immortal Divine Armor and the Fighting God. If compared to martial artists, they were the supreme protective treasures occupying the positions of Cult Leader and Alliance Leader.

“That is fine. To face me, you ought to be wearing at least that much. However, seeing that protective garment makes me curious about one thing.”

Baek Jagang released a new aura.

After one great tremor, a bluish light began to coil around his sword. It was the first time since the battle began that his sword had held sword force. At the same time, an energy different from anything before began to ripple around Baek Jagang’s body.

With those small eyes that were all the more frightening because they were small, Baek Jagang said,

“That protective garment had better truly be impossible to cut.”

Chapter 373: Hot, Cold, and Lonely.
-----------------------------------
The Immortal Divine Armor had upheld its name until now by never being cut.

But could he guarantee it would not be cut by the Sado Alliance Leader’s sword?

For a moment, the Fighting King forced himself to erase the doubt that had risen in his heart.

“Would immortal be called immortal for nothing?”

At that, Baek Jagang asked in return.

“Do you believe there is such a thing as immortality in this world? There is only the human heart that wishes to be immortal.”

Love, dreams, hope.

Baek Jagang never believed in such things. He was a thoroughgoing realist.

“If you are so coldly rational, then why do you believe in the Young Cult Leader?”

Baek Jagang knew. The Fighting King was trying, by any means possible, to plant suspicion in his heart and shake him. In a fight between himself and the Fighting King, not even a speck of his concentration could be allowed to waver.

“I do not believe in the Young Cult Leader. The one who believes in the Young Cult Leader is my disciple.”

“Your disciple is being deceived by the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader.”

The Fighting King’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk. Like the others, he appeared in black and white, but only his two eyes were tinged red. A black-and-white body and red eyes; because of that, Geom Mugeuk looked like a demon.

“Look at that young cult leader’s eyes. Do you see the ambition and desire dwelling in them?”

Had it been anything else, he would have ignored it, but because Geom Mugeuk had been mentioned, Baek Jagang answered the Fighting King’s question.

“It seems your eyes see a blazing heat. In my eyes, I feel only the chill of a frozen snowfield.”

The Fighting King was seeing heat, and the Sado Alliance Leader was seeing cold.

Geom Mugeuk joked with them.

“You have both seen well. My left eye is hot, and my right eye is cold.”

As he said that, he looked at Bi Sain standing beside him and smiled. Such composure amid this urgency was possible only because he had absolute faith in the Sado Alliance Leader.

To be honest, Bi Sain could not feel heat or cold from Geom Mugeuk. What he felt was only that mischievous look in his eyes, as if wondering what nonsense he should spout next.

But when he looked at those mischief-filled eyes, he could read one emotion.

Geom Mugeuk asked Bi Sain.

“Surely you see something in my eyes too?”

Bi Sain nodded and answered honestly. It was something he could not have conveyed if not for a chance like this.

“You look lonely.”

At first, he had not felt it, but now he did. That Geom Mugeuk was a lonely person. Perhaps it was because he, too, knew well what loneliness was.

“As expected, a friend sees best. How lonely must I be for the Demonic Cult’s Young Cult Leader to come to the Sado Alliance’s Young Alliance Leader and ask to be friends?”

“I hope your loneliness does not lead to a strange ending.”

“Our ending is to laugh and fool around together until we die of old age.”

The Fighting King cut into their conversation and spoke coldly.

“Whether it is hot or cold, in my eyes I see greed. Can you be certain your greed is smaller than mine?”

“At the very least, it is not greed that seeks to steal what belongs to others, as yours does. Let us just say I have a personal errand.”

But I will not allow that errand to swallow up my life. The reason I live both hotly and coldly is not solely to kill Hwa Mugi.

It is for my life.

It is because I want to live together with the people I like.

When this fight is over, I will cleanly forget the Fighting King and go back to meet my people. I will not fret, worrying and agonizing over what will come next or who will appear.

Even after I kill Hwa Mugi someday, I will continue living as the Young Cult Leader, and I will ascend to the seat of the Heavenly Demon. I do not know what else will happen in that process, or what kind of life will await me after I become the Heavenly Demon.

Only one thing is certain. A life spent pouring everything into one villain was enough once, before my regression.

While eating with the Demon Venerable, I will say I am stepping out for a moment, then go kill Hwa Mugi and return. While hunting with Father, I will briefly go kill him instead of a tiger.

If they ask where I have been, I will smile and answer that I just took care of a small matter.

May that become my life in this lifetime. I will strive to the point of death to make such a life.

The people here could never know Geom Mugeuk’s heart. That heart was conveyed only as a feeling of heat, cold, and loneliness.

“What will you do if that personal errand is what bewitches your disciple?”

Until the very end, the Fighting King tried to provoke Baek Jagang.

The very moment Baek Jagang glanced at Bi Sain—

Swaaaaak!

A palm force flew in a surprise attack from the Fighting King’s hand.

But Baek Jagang twisted his body and avoided the incoming strike. Though the palm force brushed past right in front of his face, Baek Jagang did not even blink.

Baek Jagang spoke in a calm tone.

“The only thing certain in this place may be the greed you hide beneath that armor.”

Baek Jagang aimed his sword, sword aura flowing along its blade, at the Fighting King.

“There is no need to show me. I will personally tear that treasured armor apart and see what is inside.”

The moment his words ended, blue sword aura scattered through the air and flew toward the Fighting King.

Red aura gathered on both fists of the Fighting King as he struck back. Tremendous aura was released from his fists as well.

With a thunderous boom as aura collided with aura, two different colors mixed together. The red and blue aura that embroidered the air and spread magnificently felt like art created at the tip of a painter’s brush.

Pwaaaaang!

Blue clusters of light split apart from the first collision and stretched out in dozens of strands. They were not being destroyed and scattered. They all changed direction at once and flew toward the Fighting King.

Because it was an attack he had not expected, the Fighting King had no time to evade. He raised his protective aura to the utmost.

‘With the Immortal, I can withstand it!’

The aura that had separated into dozens of strands and flown forward merged into one before the Fighting King, creating a new shape. What the blue aura formed was a wolf.

In that instant, Geom Mugeuk understood. That wolf form was identical to the wolf in the Sado Alliance Leader’s hall. The lonely wolf that protected the Sado, the Blue Wolf.

Tyrant King Quakes the Heavens Sword Art, Fourth Form: Blue Wolf Form.

With an ear-rending wolf howl, the enormous wolf swallowed the Fighting King whole.

At the very moment the aura struck the Fighting King, the Sado Alliance martial artists all cried out in admiration. At the Blue Wolf’s howl and the force that spread around them, their entire bodies trembled. Every hair on their bodies stood on end, and they felt the urge to shout. It was the first time they had seen the Alliance Leader’s powerful fighting spirit in real combat.

Peopeopeopeopeok!

The sound of something bursting came from the Fighting King.

Soon after, beyond the settling cloud of dust, the Fighting King’s figure came into view.

The Fighting King had crossed both hands in front of him to block the aura, and blood was flowing from his mouth.

Dudududuk.

The Immortal Divine Armor had been cut, and blood was flowing down the Fighting King’s body. It was a single move from Baek Jagang that had pierced through both the Immortal Divine Armor and the Fighting King’s protective aura.

The Fighting King looked down at the severed Immortal Divine Armor with startled eyes. The words the Sado Alliance Leader had spoken rang in his ears.

—Do you believe there is such a thing as immortality in this world?

If the Immortal Divine Armor was in this state, then the Twin Souls that protected his wrists and shins had been torn to shreds altogether.

Of course, it was only because he had even those that the Fighting King was able to remain standing.

The Fighting King clenched his teeth.

He had thought he was behind by about one move, but that was not it. It was clearly two moves, and considering the hidden moves still unrevealed, he might be behind by three or even four.

‘He is stronger than I thought.’

Geom Mugeuk was thinking the same thing.

It was also the reason the Sado Alliance Leader dreamed of unifying the martial world.

His father, the Sado Alliance Leader, and Jin Paecheon, said to be the most domineering among all past Martial Alliance Leaders.

By coincidence, the overlords had all gathered in one era. He even thought that perhaps the birth of Hwa Mugi was an inevitability drawn forth by these three people.

Baek Jagang looked at the blood flowing behind the Immortal Divine Armor and spoke quietly.

“Your greed is shedding tears.”

At the force extending from those small eyes, which seemed to ask whether it was regret or fear, the Fighting King clenched his fists.

Baek Jagang began to press the Fighting King.

As auras collided, explosions rang out one after another.

With his protective armor damaged, even greater shocks were inflicted on the Fighting King.

But the Fighting King did not retreat.

Red energy flowed from both his eyes as he drew his internal energy to the extreme.

The Fighting King charged in and threw his fists.

Papapapapapapapapak!

Linked Wind-Cloud Fist, Third Form: Lightning Wild Dance.

Dozens of fists, swift as light, embroidered the air.

Baek Jagang was avoiding them all. They were so fast that those watching could not tell which fist he avoided or how.

Only Geom Mugeuk saw the two men’s movements clearly.

“Ah!”

Admiration came out on its own. The Fighting King’s move was truly incredible. If he had not been an enemy, it was a move worthy of praise.

And toward the Sado Alliance Leader, who evaded all of those strikes deserving praise, he felt awe.

The Fighting King’s attack did not end there. A linked technique connected to Lightning Wild Dance was unleashed.

Linked Wind-Cloud Fist, Fourth Form: Red Chain Binding Edict.

The final fist of Lightning Wild Dance spread wide. From the Fighting King’s palm, red aura in the shape of an iron chain was released.

Chararararak!

The chain wound around Baek Jagang in an instant.

‘It’s done!’

No matter how formidable the Sado Alliance Leader was, if he was caught in Red Chain Binding Edict, he would be unable to move until the Fighting King’s next move landed.

Linked Wind-Cloud Fist, Fifth Form: Wind-Cloud Genesis!

Shiiiiik.

The Fighting King’s blow, imbued with the will to kill, flew toward Baek Jagang’s face. If Wind-Cloud Genesis struck the face, where protective aura was relatively weak—

‘It’s over.’

At that moment, the Fighting King saw it. There was no sword in Baek Jagang’s hand as he was bound by the chains.

“!”

The next instant—

Puuk!

Baek Jagang’s sword, which had plunged down silently from the air, pierced and lodged itself into his shoulder from above.

Tyrant King Quakes the Heavens, Sixth Form: Formless Shadow Form, had been unleashed.

The sword pierced through the Fighting King’s bone and flesh.

“Uaaaagh!”

As the Fighting King’s true qi was severed—

Papapapapang!

With a series of thunderous booms, the Red Chain Binding Edict that had been tightening around Baek Jagang shattered to pieces and vanished.

Pwaaaak!

The sword embedded in his shoulder pulled itself free and flew into Baek Jagang’s hand.

Blood spurted from the Fighting King’s shoulder.

The Fighting King hurriedly pressed his acupoints to stop the bleeding, but the wound was deep. He had not been able to face him even with an uninjured body, and now he was in a state where he could no longer endure.

What the Fighting King chose was flight.

He began to flee desperately. Despite his grievous wound, he ran at a terrifying speed.

Baek Jagang followed after him even faster.

Peoeok!

Baek Jagang hurled the Fighting King, who had been fleeing desperately, to the ground.

Kwaang!

Though he rolled across the ground from the great impact, the Fighting King sprang back up and began to run again. Those watching also moved after the two of them. In an instant, they had traveled several dozen jang.

Bi Sain sealed Hyeok Sagun’s paralysis acupoints, roughly tucked him under his side, and moved with them.

His master was fleeing, and he himself was being dragged along miserably.

But hope had still not disappeared from his eyes, which should have been filled with resentment.

Once more, the aura Baek Jagang launched struck the Fighting King in the back.

Peok!

The Fighting King collapsed face-first onto the ground.

Giving up on running any farther, the Fighting King sat there, panting.

“I promised not to let even a single hair be harmed. This fight ends here.”

At that desperate moment, as Baek Jagang prepared the final blow—

The Fighting King instead smiled.

“Then… you have lost.”

Just then, as the Fighting King wore a meaningful smile—

About a hundred enemies appeared from all directions. This was the reason he had fled all the way here. For the final move he had hidden in this place.

The person standing at the front was Sahon, the Fighting King’s trusted hand and foot.

Those who appeared with him were not adults, but children.

There were children not yet ten years old, and children of thirteen or fourteen. Some looked to be about seventeen or eighteen at most.

Geom Mugeuk understood.

They were the very children who had been abducted earlier.

There was no intelligence in the children’s eyes. They had been brainwashed by some special method.

Strange bags hung from the children’s chests.

They leaped into the air in all directions at the same time. At the same time, the bags hanging from the children’s chests burst, and white powder flew everywhere.

Peoeok! Peoeok! Peok! Peok!

The amount of powder that burst from a single bag was not ordinary. But over a hundred bags had burst at once, so the surroundings were instantly covered in powder.

“It is Yellow Springs.”

Yellow Springs was a poison for mass slaughter, the most strictly forbidden poison in the murim. The bag that emitted this poison had been created by the late Poison Overlord.

The Fighting King had made the children wear them. It was a plan devised to lay all the blame on the Demonic Cult’s Poison King.

When he first revealed himself, he had used the children to prevent them from wiping him out in a single strike. The Fighting King was the sort of man who, if it meant winning, would use not only children, but even infants.

The warriors of the Sado Alliance all took out poison-warding beads and bit down on them at once.

“Poison-warding beads won’t stop it.”

Earlier, Geom Mugeuk had raised a whirlwind and burned away the Poison Overlord’s poison, but now it was beyond anyone’s control. Too vast an amount had spread in every direction in an instant, so even Geom Mugeuk could do nothing about this dreadful poison.

Bisa-in threw himself forward to protect the Alliance Leader first.

“Please, hurry and avoid it.”

But the Sado Alliance Leader had already been exposed to the poison.

“It will be said that the Demonic Cult’s Poison King killed you!”

There were only three people here who had taken the antidote in advance: the Fighting King, Hyeok Sagun, and Sahon. Hyeok Sagun was someone the Fighting King absolutely needed in order to become the leader of the Sado Alliance.

The Fighting King smiled in satisfaction. Somehow, he would be able to endure by fleeing from the poisoned Sado Alliance Leader’s attacks. Time was on his side, after all.

It was enough as long as he won, even like this. To seize victory by any means necessary—this was the moment he grasped the greatest victory of his life.

At that very moment, the smile vanished from the Fighting King’s lips.

In his eyes, he saw Sahon looking around with a flustered expression. The Fighting King looked around as well.

Nothing happened.

The children were still standing as they were, and the Sado Alliance warriors exposed to the poison had not collapsed. They should all have been dead by now.

Belatedly, the Thirteen Wolves of Sado and the warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps subdued the blood points of the dazed children first.

The Fighting King had never once truly shown his emotions, but this time was different. He was shocked and flustered.

‘Why?’

Why was no one collapsing?

Baek Jagang and all the other Sado Alliance warriors present were just as astonished. They all knew just how vicious a poison Yellow Springs was. Geom Mugeuk, too, had no idea what was going on.

‘No way?’

As Geom Mugeuk looked around, his gaze turned toward the air. Once his eyes fixed there, everyone else’s gaze naturally followed his.

A person stood on the branch of a giant tree, arms crossed, looking down at them. He was a young, handsome boy. At his waist hung twelve pouches, each marked with one of the animals of the twelve zodiac branches.

“Poison King!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s startled cry, a faint smile formed at the man’s lips.

It truly was the Poison King. The claim that the Demon Physician and the Poison King were coming had been a lie, yet somehow, the real Poison King had appeared here.

As the wind blew and made the hems of his clothes flutter, the Poison King began to descend slowly through the air, as though a heavenly deity were alighting upon the world.

Chapter 374: Sell It for Travel Money.
--------------------------------------
Everyone’s gaze turned toward the Poison King.

In the martial world, the Poison King was a mysterious being shrouded in all manner of rumors.

They said that if you fell victim to the Poison King, you would die without even realizing you had been poisoned.

They said that if your eyes met his, you would go blind on the spot.

They said he had poisoned hundreds to death with a single drop of poison.

They said he even slept inside a vat filled with poisonous substances.

They said he looked like a hunchbacked old monster; no, that he was not a man at all, but a middle-aged woman.

He was, in every sense, the subject of countless misunderstandings and distortions.

And today, the Thirteen Wolves of Sado and the martial artists of the Extreme Blade Corps were seeing the Poison King in person. They were astonished. Never in their dreams had they imagined the Poison King would be such a young-looking, beautiful boy.

Even beneath the gazes pouring toward him, the Poison King remained composed and aloof.

The Poison King’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk. The look in his eyes as he regarded him for a moment was deep.

“I greet the Young Cult Leader.”

Ordinarily, he was someone who would have skipped the greeting and gone off to dig up poisonous herbs beneath that tree over there. But today, for Geom Mugeuk’s sake, he showed a side of himself utterly unlike usual.

“I greet the Poison King.”

Geom Mugeuk also cupped his fist respectfully and bowed.

The Poison King, exuding a solemn atmosphere that overwhelmed the surroundings. It felt unfamiliar, and yet new.

After first attending to Geom Mugeuk, the Poison King next paid his respects to the Sado Alliance Leader.

“It is an honor to meet such a distinguished person.”

“I have long heard of the Demon Venerable’s reputation. It is good to meet you.”

The Poison King also exchanged calm greetings with Baek Jagang.

Though the Poison King was usually shut away within his own world, at this moment he stood at the boundary between that world and the world outside.

Baek Jagang was more tense than when he had fought the Fighting King. He was confident he would not die to the Fighting King, but he had no such confidence when it came to the Poison King.

He might be able to kill the Poison King, but in the end, it felt as though he himself would die as well. The Poison King was that kind of terrifying existence.

But today, instead of wariness toward the Poison King, he conveyed his gratitude.

He owed him a great debt. Even without asking, he knew who had detoxified Yellow Springs.

“There will come a day when I repay the debt I owe today.”

“It was done for our cult’s Young Cult Leader, so please do not trouble yourself over it.”

Was it truly for my sake?

Geom Mugeuk was certain it was not. The Poison King knew that he was immune to all poisons.

Thus, he had detoxified Yellow Springs not for him, but to save these people. Or perhaps for the children.

The Poison King before the regression and the Poison King now were the same person. But he was living an entirely different fate.

Geom Mugeuk knew. All of us stand upon a boundary. Just as the Sword Venerable before the regression, who had tried to become Cult Leader, and the Sword Venerable now, who was immersed in martial training, were not different people, changing one’s fate did not require some grand catalyst.

There were others who had come with the Poison King. Sangseon, who served him, and five Poison Fangs also appeared with him.

Sangseon had nine poison pouches, and the remaining Poison Fangs had seven or more. Truly, the peerless masters of the Thousand Poisons Forest had come together.

“How in the world did you come here?”

Sangseon answered in his stead.

“We obtained confidential information that Yellow Springs, a poison of mass destruction whose use is forbidden in the martial world, had been moved in large quantities toward the Sado Alliance. Strategist Go requested an investigation.”

They had come at Go Wol’s request. Since Go Wol would have been concerned solely with his own safety, he must have regarded Yellow Springs as a tremendous risk factor.

Of course, the Poison King did not have to step forward. Go Wol was not even affiliated with Tongcheon Pavilion, and was merely Geom Mugeuk’s personal strategist. He could have sent only his subordinates to investigate Yellow Springs.

And yet he had come personally like this for Geom Mugeuk’s sake. He must truly have hated coming out.

‘Thank you, Poison King.’

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Poison King.

The Poison King was walking toward the children whose blood points had been sealed.

There were several standing beside him as well, yet the Poison King deliberately walked toward the child standing close to the Fighting King.

The Fighting King glared coldly at him, but the Poison King did not so much as flinch; he paid him no mind at all. It felt as though he were saying, If you can kill me, try it. No, please try.

The Poison King flipped up the child’s eyelids and pulled out his tongue to examine it.

“He did not use Gu poison. He planted suggestions in the children by another method.”

After that, he examined the pouches the children wore on their chests.

“He made something truly bizarre. Sangseon, take one of these with us. Bring it back and study it. Still, isn’t the bag a bit large for children to carry?”

Those words contained a rebuke for having made children shoulder such a task.

Geom Mugeuk knew. He knew how much the Poison King despised those who used poisons of mass slaughter indiscriminately. Because he cherished and loved poison arts more than anyone, he loathed those who used poison carelessly to an extreme degree. And on top of that, they had even used children.

Right now, the Poison King was surely feeling an intense desire to kill the Fighting King. If this had not been a matter of the Sado Alliance, the Fighting King would already be dead.

The Poison King walked past the Fighting King as if to make a point, murmuring as though to himself.

“From what I heard earlier, it seems someone was even trying to frame an innocent person.”

Passing before him while saying such a thing was both disregard and provocation.

The Fighting King had already been driven to the very end, but even in this situation, he could not attack the Poison King. His conflict was palpable. A deep conflict over whether or not to strike the Poison King, who had ruined his plans.

But in the end, he could not attack.

Because he knew better than anyone just how terrifying a master was housed within that extremely youthful appearance. This was the Poison King whom even the Poison Hegemon had feared. No matter how brave he was, he wanted to avoid dying with his five viscera and six bowels melting away.

Geom Mugeuk felt it anew. Why the martial artists of the murim feared the Poison King the most. Why the Poison King possessed the strongest power of war deterrence.

This time, the Fighting King’s gaze turned toward Baek Jagang and Bi Sain.

It was regrettable. If he had killed those two, Sado would have fallen into his hands.

It was truly regrettable that he had lost the fight, and that with this defeat he would lose everything.

In the end, the place his resentful gaze turned was Geom Mugeuk.

‘It’s because of him.’

The Poison King would not have come either, if not for that Young Cult Leader.

From the very beginning, everything had gone wrong because of that Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult. A hatred rose within him, insisting that even if he died, he had to kill that man alone.

“I have one final request. Allow me to fight the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult.”

Baek Jagang refused firmly.

“Not a chance.”

The Fighting King provoked Geom Mugeuk.

“Will you remain a cowardly Young Cult Leader?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and smiled. When Bi Sain, who was standing beside him, laughed along as well, Geom Mugeuk asked.

“Why are you laughing?”

“I laughed because that man knows far too little about you. Ah, how wonderful it would be if you were someone who fell for such obvious provocation.”

Would it really be wonderful? Bi Sain’s head asked, and his heart answered. No.

“How shameless. You look at those children and still dare put the word cowardice in your mouth?”

Even at Bi Sain’s mockery, the Fighting King continued to provoke only Geom Mugeuk.

“Geom Mugeuk, fight me!”

Everyone looked at Geom Mugeuk. With clear, deep eyes, he shook his head.

“The reason that won’t do is because the Poison King here is not someone who would stand by if I were harmed. It would be less painful for you to simply die by the Alliance Leader’s sword. If you fall victim to the Poison King’s poison, you will die feeling the agony of your entire body melting. Isn’t that right, Poison King?”

When Geom Mugeuk turned to look back at the Poison King, he was no longer there.

He was crouching some distance away in front of a martial artist of the Extreme Blade Corps.

“Move your foot, if you would.”

When the martial artist of the Extreme Blade Corps, startled, moved his foot, there was grass behind it.

“Ah, I saw correctly.”

The Poison King carefully dug up the poisonous herb.

Geom Mugeuk smiled faintly as he watched the Poison King return to his own world. Everyone likely assumed there must be some other meaning behind the Poison King’s actions, but no one would know. Right now, he was sincerely absorbed in digging up poisonous herbs.

At that moment, the Fighting King unleashed a surprise palm force.

An explosion thundered.

The place the Fighting King’s palm force headed was Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk extended one hand, neutralized the Fighting King’s palm force, and continued looking at the Poison King.

The Poison King was still digging up the poisonous herb, and Geom Mugeuk watched him.

The two of them thoroughly ignored the Fighting King.

“You bastard! I will kill you no matter what!”

The Fighting King’s composure shattered.

He threw the dagger at his waist. It was truly astonishing flying dagger technique, but the dagger did not reach its target.

The one who deflected the dagger was Baek Jagang. He no longer stood by and watched the Fighting King struggle in desperation.

“Look. Where is this grand thing called immortality that you believed in? There is only greed and regret.”

With even the Poison King having appeared, he had no intention of dragging the fight out.

Baek Jagang immediately pressed him.

Shaaaang!

Boom!

The Fighting King, who had suffered a great wound to his arm, could not withstand Baek Jagang’s attack and was helplessly pushed back. After striking him hard to scatter his true qi—

Overlord Heaven-Suppressing Sword, Fifth Form: Ring of Annihilation.

Baek Jagang’s sword qi, flying through the air, split into dozens from all directions around the Fighting King. There was nowhere to evade.

And just as that final move was about to shoot toward the Fighting King—

Whoooosh.

A black hole formed beneath the feet where the Fighting King stood, and he vanished as if being sucked down into it.

Everyone was startled by the sudden event.

What remained on the ground were the severed Immortal Divine Armor he had been wearing, the tattered protective gear, blood-soaked clothing, and the gauntlets called the Fighting God. He had vanished naked, leaving behind everything that had been on his body.

‘Someone rescued him!’

Baek Jagang raised his qi and surveyed the surroundings. But no matter how he searched, he could not sense any other presence. Someone had used an astonishing sorcery to extract him.

Baek Jagang looked at Geom Mugeuk. His gaze asked whether he could sense anything, but Geom Mugeuk also shook his head.

“The secret art that took him away is no ordinary one.”
