In the end, this meant that he too believed Geom Mugeuk’s words. That he had invited the Unorthodox Alliance Lord because there was a reason to invite him.

Geom Mugeuk knew how great a decision Jin Hagun had made. He had staked his position as heir.

“If you’re expelled, I’ll protest in front of the Martial Alliance and demand that they reinstate you.”

Where Jin Haryeong had just imagined the main gate of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, Jin Hagun imagined the main gate of the Martial Alliance.

He pictured Geom Mugeuk standing before the Martial Alliance with a banner that read, “Reinstate My Friend Jin Hagun as Heir!”

It would be mortifying even to imagine, but—

‘That’s exactly the sort of thing this man would do.’

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Hagun,

“Thank you. I’ll never forget what you’ve staked on this decision for the rest of my life.”

This was what Jin Hagun had learned from him.

Did they really need words between them? Geom Mugeuk still said things like this. Thank you. I won’t forget.

Once they had decided to help with this matter, Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong naturally turned their gazes toward Geom Mugeuk.

“So? What’s your plan?”

* * *

The first person to visit Jin Paecheon was Jin Haryeong.

“What brings you here?”

“I came because I wanted to see you, Grandfather.”

Though Jin Paecheon was known for his overbearing nature, there was no doubt that he doted on his granddaughter. Besides, she had once been so sharp and difficult, but recently she had clearly begun to grow more mature.

“There was a gathering of the younger generation today, wasn’t there?”

“Yes, it went well.”

“Are there still young heroes who like you these days?”

“Of course. There were some today, too.”

“Was there no one among them you fancied?”

“It’s difficult to find a man as splendid as you, Grandfather. You were popular with the female heroes when you were young, weren’t you?”

A bright smile spread across Jin Paecheon’s lips. It was a smile rarely seen by others, reserved exclusively for his granddaughter.

“Your words are unusually sweet today. You must have something to ask of me.”

“Don’t misunderstand me. I’m speaking from the heart.”

“If you like someone, I have no intention of indulging my own desires. I’ll accept him. You know that, don’t you? I even accepted a servant as a prospective grandson-in-law.”

Jin Paecheon watched his granddaughter’s reaction as he mentioned Geom Mugeuk. He was worried that she might be unable to settle her feelings because of Geom Mugeuk.

“I should have held on to him back then.”

Hearing the regret in her voice, Jin Paecheon stared intently at his granddaughter.

“I’m joking, Grandfather.”

“You’d better be.”

Would Jin Paecheon not feel regret as well? If Geom Mugeuk had at least been an ordinary man from the Demon Cult, perhaps he might have forced the matter through somehow.

No, what was he thinking? How could he marry his granddaughter to a demon cultivator? His lingering regret existed only because the boy named Geom Mugeuk was so extraordinary.

At that moment, Jin Hagun entered the Alliance Lord’s Hall.

After respectfully greeting Jin Paecheon, Jin Haryeong asked,

“It’s been hard to see you lately, Big Brother.”

The two of them pretended that they had happened to meet by chance.

“I’ve heard you’ve been completely immersed in cultivation these days.”

“What brings you here?”

“I came because I wanted to see Grandfather. What about you, Big Brother?”

Jin Hagun then said to Jin Paecheon,

“The Demon Cult’s Young Lord is waiting outside. He says he has come to ask the Alliance Lord a favor.”

If all three of them arrived together, it would look like they had conspired beforehand, so they had come separately.

Jin Haryeong convincingly acted surprised. She trembled for no reason at the thought of Geom Mugeuk standing outside that door. No acting was necessary.

“Before you let him in, there’s something I’d like to ask of you, Alliance Lord.”

“Speak.”

“I don’t know what favor the Demon Cult’s Young Lord intends to ask, but if it is something you can grant, please lend me your support.”

There was a reason she was making a request she normally would not have made.

“You know about the child abduction case we dealt with during the Vanquish Demons Unit’s operation last time. On the way here, I asked the Young Lord about it, and he said he had broken the brainwashing placed on those children and sent them back to their respective homes. Did you know that?”

Jin Paecheon nodded. He had already received a report on the matter. He had been overjoyed to hear that the children had been rescued safely. That was why he had secretly been pleased to hear that Geom Mugeuk had come.

“That incident was the Vanquish Demons Unit’s responsibility, so in the end, I incurred a debt to the Young Lord. I’d like to repay it on this occasion.”

Jin Paecheon readily accepted his grandson’s request.

“Very well.”

A short while later, Geom Mugeuk entered the hall.

He bowed deeply and greeted them with due courtesy.

“Alliance Lord, have you been well?”

“Welcome.”

Jin Paecheon studied Geom Mugeuk with a calm gaze. His eyes were clearer and deeper than when they had met before.

‘His martial cultivation has risen even further since then.’

The more he looked at him, the more he wanted him. And that made his regret all the keener.

Geom Mugeuk greeted Jin Haryeong as though they were meeting for the first time as well.

“Miss Jin, it’s been a long time. Have you been well?”

“It’s inconvenient without a servant.”

Geom Mugeuk laughed at her joke and replied,

“If it’s you, Miss Jin, I can serve as your attendant anytime.”

Even knowing he was only teasing, his words made her heart flutter.

“I heard about your exploits in the Unorthodox Alliance. I heard you rescued all the children. That was truly remarkable.”

She praised Geom Mugeuk’s achievement once more. Today’s gathering was not about obtaining her grandfather’s permission, but about winning his favor.

“I was merely fortunate. The Unorthodox Alliance also gave me their full assistance.”

After exchanging greetings, Geom Mugeuk calmly explained why he had come.

“I received great help from the Unorthodox Alliance Lord while handling the matter there. So I invited the Unorthodox Alliance Lord to my regular tavern in front of our cult.”

Jin Paecheon said nothing. His aura began to sink.

The surroundings instantly turned cold, as though someone had flung open a window in the middle of winter.

As the Alliance Lord’s gentle gaze flew toward him like a sharp icicle, Geom Mugeuk continued speaking.

“The Unorthodox Alliance Lord said he didn’t want to cause any unnecessary misunderstanding with you, Alliance Lord. So I told him I would come and explain things to you to make sure there were no misunderstandings. That is why I came. It’s nothing serious, so please don’t misunderstand.”

Jin Paecheon’s expression hardened, and his gaze grew even colder. To him, it sounded as though the Demon Cult intended to join hands with the Unorthodox Alliance.

Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong thought that, so far, things were going well. If Geom Mugeuk had not been the one standing before him, Jin Paecheon would already have thundered at him.

Jin Haryeong spoke coldly in her grandfather’s place.

“So you intend to hold secret talks with the Unorthodox Alliance. Since you’re bound to be found out anyway, are you confessing first?”

“It’s true that we would be found out eventually. But this isn’t a secret meeting. It’s a personal matter between the Unorthodox Alliance Lord and me. We’ll meet at a tavern where everyone can see us, so the entire martial world will learn of it.”

Geom Mugeuk emphasized that it was not a secret meeting.

“If you truly intended to meet him in secret, would you choose a tavern in Maga Village?”

“Why did you choose that place?”

The question had not been part of the plan. It reflected nothing more than her personal curiosity. It was also the place he had once said he wanted to invite her to.

“The place I intend to take the Unorthodox Alliance Lord is called the Wind and Pleasure Tavern. The owner’s name is Jo Chunbae. He’s kind, skilled, and knows how to create a pleasant atmosphere. When you sit upstairs, drink a cup of liquor, and look outside, you can understand why you have to go on living.”

Jin Haryeong was curious.

What did he see there?

“The tables there are sturdy enough that even a martial artist won’t break them. The customers who come there may be surprised when the Unorthodox Alliance Lord arrives, but they won’t be afraid.”

“How is that possible?”

Geom Mugeuk answered with complete sincerity.

“Because my demonic path is there.”

A brief silence followed.

Who could understand the profound meaning contained in those words?

Yet precisely because she could not understand them, Jin Haryeong wanted to visit the place even more.

She returned to the original plan and showed a coldness unlike her true feelings.

“You’re trying to beguile us with plausible words, but this is something we cannot permit.”

There was a reason Jin Haryeong stepped forward to oppose him. If she showed even the slightest sign of agreeing with Geom Mugeuk, her grandfather would only become more worried and enraged.

Jin Hagun, meanwhile, stood silently. Since he had already said that he owed the Young Lord a debt, he merely showed a faintly troubled expression.

“Whether you join hands with the Unorthodox Alliance is your business, but at the very least, don’t deceive us!”

Her shrill voice rang through the Alliance Lord’s Hall.

“Deceive you?”

“You say this is merely a private meeting between you and the Young Lord, but there’s no way the leader of the Divine Cult would fail to come when the Unorthodox Alliance Lord is visiting, is there?”

Jin Paecheon was concerned about that as well. In the end, it would become a meeting between the Heavenly Demon and the Unorthodox Alliance Lord.

“You’ll exchange messages through telepathy, won’t you? Their martial arts are so profound that Grandfather is the only one who could notice it. In the end, you and the Unorthodox Alliance could plot something in secret.”

She subtly emphasized that no scheme could be carried out recklessly as long as her grandfather was present.

“That’s a misunderstanding.”

Jin Haryeong said to Jin Paecheon.

“Grandfather, never give your permission.”

Jin Haryeong became enraged before anyone else, leaving Jin Paecheon no chance to get angry himself. Her outburst, coming from someone who seemed likely to take Geom Mugeuk’s side, had the desired effect.

“Haryeong, calm yourself for a moment.”

“This is an act of deception against you and the Alliance!”

“I said that’s enough!”

“I’m sorry, Grandfather.”

Jin Paecheon asked Jin Hagun, who had been silently observing the situation,

“What do you think?”

Jin Hagun offered an entirely new perspective.

“What are you plotting? Do you intend to lure the Evil Path Alliance Leader here and kill him?”

For a moment, Jin Paecheon was inwardly startled. He had been so focused on the possibility that the Demonic Cult and the Evil Path Alliance were joining hands to scheme against them that he had never considered the Demonic Cult might intend to kill the Evil Path Alliance Leader.

It was a question that was entirely plausible, so Jin Paecheon watched Geom Mugeuk closely for his reaction and answer.

For a moment, Geom Mugeuk was flustered and could not reply.

“I mean, are you planning to use this opportunity to attack the Evil Path Alliance?”

“Don’t make such a ridiculous accusation.”

“If the Evil Path Alliance Leader were to die in some unfortunate accident during this gathering, the Young Alliance Leader would naturally take his place. And aren’t you close to the Young Alliance Leader of the Evil Path Alliance?”

Jin Haryeong picked up where Jin Hagun left off. This was the most important moment in today’s plan.

“Then you’ll seize control of the Evil Path Alliance! Now I understand why you tried so hard to grow close to the Young Alliance Leader.”

Jin Paecheon found the speculation quite convincing. Who could guarantee that things would not turn out exactly that way? Besides, Geom Mugeuk, who was always so self-assured, seemed somewhat cowed, as though she had struck the heart of the matter.

“Am I wrong?”

“I came only to ask for permission. If you do not wish to grant it, you need only say so. There is no need to make such groundless speculations.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jin Paecheon.

“If the Alliance Leader shares your granddaughter’s opinion, then I shall take my leave.”

Jin Paecheon sensed something suspicious in the way he tried to withdraw without arguing the matter any further. He became convinced that there was undoubtedly some hidden motive behind this unprecedented proposal.

‘The balance between the Orthodox, Unorthodox, and Demonic factions is going to be shattered?’

Suddenly, Jin Paecheon recalled the appearance of the Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin.

Whenever he thought of that man, the first thing that came to mind was his cold, arrogant gaze. That ambition-filled gaze, which made it clear he would never be content with merely becoming the Demonic Cult’s leader.

‘Is your gaze still the same?’

Geom Mugeuk respectfully cupped his hands and turned away.

“I shall take my leave.”

Just as Geom Mugeuk was hurriedly turning around—

“Wait.”

The shocking declaration that followed came from Jin Paecheon.

“I will attend this gathering as well.”

Startled, Geom Mugeuk hurriedly said,

“No, there’s no need for you to come. You only need to refrain from misunderstanding this as a gathering between the Demonic Cult and the Evil Path Alliance. There is no need for you to attend!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s insistence, Jin Paecheon spoke firmly, as if making a declaration.

“I said I’m going!”

Chapter 380: I Held Them All Off Even When I Was Alone.
-------------------------------------------------------
Jin Paecheon expressed his firm will to attend.

Geom Mugeuk said weakly, wearing a bewildered expression.

“We will invite you to this gathering as well, Alliance Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk was faithful to the act until the very end.

At this outcome, Jin Haryeong was beyond surprised—she was in awe. She had played a major role in bringing about this result, but she had never truly thought it would turn out this way.

‘In the end, it all went according to that man’s plan.’

What if that man were an enemy? Would she or her elder brother be able to withstand him?

The vague fear she had felt before struck her keenly once again today.

In any case, the plan they had made together had succeeded. And now, the Murim Alliance Leader’s visit to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had become reality.

Jin Haryeong now returned from being the vanguard of the three-person operation to being the granddaughter of the Murim Alliance Leader.

“How will you guarantee the Alliance Leader’s safety?”

It was the Murim Alliance Leader’s procession—would only one or two peerless masters accompany him? But since they had issued the invitation, the side responsible for his safety was the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“Our cult will take responsibility. Having invited such an honored guest, how could we be negligent?”

“Who will protect him from you?”

Geom Mugeuk took it as a joke and laughed. But Jin Haryeong was serious.

“Promise me you’ll protect him. That you’ll take responsibility and make sure nothing happens.”

Her eyes said it.

‘You know, don’t you? That all of this was done because I trusted you.’

She trusted Geom Mugeuk more than the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Truly. She had done this prepared even to be driven out and left standing in the rain before the main gate of the Demon Cult.

Geom Mugeuk looked into her eyes and answered.

“I promise.”

Only after feeling the sincerity dwelling in those clear, deep eyes did Jin Haryeong finally feel relieved.

‘Grandfather, I’m sorry for deceiving you. But I believe this is right.’

She believed Geom Mugeuk’s words that they could prevent war. She also believed that, in the end, this choice would become a path for her grandfather’s sake.

Geom Mugeuk spoke respectfully to Jin Paecheon.

“I will speak to our cult’s strategist and have the schedule coordinated. Thank you for making such a great decision.”

Thinking that the three of them would meet, Baek Jagang’s figure also came to Jin Paecheon’s mind.

‘Come to think of it, it’s been a long time since I’ve seen him too.’

He considered Baek Jagang a step below Geom Ujin, but he knew well that Baek Jagang was no ordinary man either. One concern naturally arose.

“But if he hears that I’m going, won’t the Sado Alliance Leader change his mind?”

“I’ll go and tell him.”

“You’ll go in person?”

“This is too important a change to convey by letter.”

This was a matter of seating the Murim Alliance Leader and the Sado Alliance Leader in front of the Demon Cult’s main headquarters. If necessary, he would have to go back and forth ten times, even a hundred times.

Geom Mugeuk gave a respectful salute.

“Then I will take my leave. I will see you at the tavern before our cult.”

“Very well. Let me take a look at what your Demonic Path is like.”

Jin Paecheon sent Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong out with him to see him off.

Left alone, Jin Paecheon spoke into the empty air.

“The strategist will scold me.”

Then a deep male voice came from the air.

“Do you truly think the Demon Cult Leader will kill the Sado Alliance Leader?”

“Who knows? How could I know what is in that man’s heart? The Geom Ujin I saw over ten years ago was at least not a man who would resort to such petty tricks. But now that the world has changed, how can I know what sort of man he has become?”

Jin Paecheon slowly walked over and looked out the window.

Before the Alliance Leader’s Hall, he could see Geom Mugeuk, Jin Hagun, and Jin Haryeong standing side by side.

There were many reasons he had decided to attend this gathering.

There was the concern that the Demon Cult and the Sado Alliance might join hands and play some trick, and there was also his curiosity about that young martial artist named Geom Mugeuk. Just how had he drawn out that far-from-compliant Baek Jagang all the way to the front of the Demon Cult?

Moreover, he already knew that Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong felt great goodwill toward Geom Mugeuk. In the end, he had come to think that he ought to examine Geom Mugeuk more closely with his own eyes.

But if those had been the only reasons, he would not have made the decision without even consulting the strategist. The greatest reason Jin Paecheon had resolved to go was this.

He felt that the time had come to meet the Heavenly Demon Geom Ujin once again.

When they had met in the past, Jin Paecheon had read in Geom Ujin the ambition to unite the murim under one rule, and he had suspected that one day, he might start a war.

That was why he had devoted all his strength to protecting the orthodox murim.

Now, before he grew any older, he thought he had to confirm it.

‘If his eyes are still the same.’

He could not let that gaze turn toward Jin Hagun. It was a gaze his grandson could not endure.

* * *

As soon as they left the Alliance Leader’s Hall, Jin Hagun let out a sigh.

“Why are you sighing? This is a situation where even cheering wouldn’t be enough.”

Pretending not to hear Geom Mugeuk, Jin Hagun ignored him and spoke to his sister.

“Perhaps we’ve pushed the Alliance Leader into danger with our own hands.”

It felt like being swept up in the heat of the moment, playing madly all night, and only coming to one’s senses in the morning. Whenever he associated with Geom Mugeuk, he always felt this way.

“Aren’t you worried?”

By contrast, Jin Haryeong was calm.

“Worried about what?”

“That we might have been deceived by that man.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk cut in.

“Please have that conversation through sound transmission!”

But even those words were ignored as Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong continued their conversation.

“He could have deceived us.”

“Right?”

“It could be a plot to harm Grandfather and the Sado Alliance Leader in one place.”

“And yet you don’t look worried at all?”

“Because you’re worrying for my share too, Elder Brother. If not, with my personality, I probably wouldn’t be able to sleep properly starting tonight. In that sense, thank you for being born first.”

Jin Hagun shook his head as if she were hopeless, then glanced at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk was looking up at the sky.

“How is the sky when you look up at it from before the Murim Alliance Leader’s Hall?”

“It’s the same. Whether you look at it from here or from there, the sky is the sky. That’s why I have no desire for this place.”

They were words that held Geom Mugeuk’s true feelings. Whenever he had the chance, he kept telling them. That he had no greed. So they should not suspect him.

Again and again. Even if it was troublesome, he would keep saying it. Suspicion was inevitable; the trouble was a choice.

“Still, seeing it together with you two makes it better.”

Jin Hagun recalled the look Geom Mugeuk had given him earlier. That provocative look telling him to cross the line drawn in his heart.

‘I crossed it as you intended. And it went as you intended.’

That was why he kept making jokes like this.

“Is the plan to kill us all complete now?”

A faint smile formed at the corner of Geom Mugeuk’s mouth as he continued looking up at the sky.

“In a similar situation, I said the same thing to the Sado Alliance Leader. That it isn’t my way. My way is to send a challenge letter. We fight once, and the side that loses steps down cleanly. I’m not so fond of anything that I’d scheme to seize it…”

Geom Mugeuk did not continue.

If he looked back at himself before his regression, these words would have suited him.

I have no affection for people or for the world.

Perhaps that was why he was who he was now. Because of the regret and lingering sorrow over that empty life in which he had lived only for revenge, granting no meaning to people, to the world, or even to himself.

Because he had seen the end of loneliness, he now intended to go all the way in the opposite direction. Even if greater regret waited there, he would gladly walk that path.

“I prefer playing around. I like a seat on the second floor of Pungryu Tavern better than sitting on that lofty Grand Preceptor’s chair.”

Now both Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong believed those words. That was why they had plotted this together with him today.

Before leaving, Geom Mugeuk spoke to the two of them.

“Do you know why I was able to carry out this matter with confidence?”

“Why?”

“Because I trust my father. Because I trust that no matter what situation I unfold, he will not turn it into the worst possible outcome. So if you cannot trust me, trust your Alliance Leader.”

“!”

Geom Mugeuk gave the two a brisk cupped-fist salute.

“I won’t forget your help this time. And be sure to come to our cult with him.”

Even if an order came down telling her not to come, Jin Haryeong intended to follow along, even if she had to hide in the luggage compartment of a carriage. She truly wanted to visit Pungryu Tavern.

That was why this parting at this moment felt so regretful.

“Are you going back to the Divine Cult?”

“No. I said I’m going to the Sado Alliance.”

“Ah, right. You really do live busily.”

The moment Geom Mugeuk smiled faintly, there was a flash, and he vanished from sight. Just as when he had come, he disappeared like smoke.

“And he just leaves like that?”

Jin Haryeong stood there for a long while, staring at the place where Geom Mugeuk had disappeared.

‘I miss him already.’

* * *

As Jin Haryeong had said, it truly was a busy life.

From there, he even spared the time he should have spent sleeping and ran without rest until he arrived at the Sado Alliance’s main headquarters. Truly, he was training his Rapid Step properly.

Geom Mugeuk first stopped by the inn Bi Sain often visited. Today as well, he was eating there.

—If you eat noodle dishes too often, you’ll gain weight.

At the incoming sound transmission, Bi Sain’s chopsticks paused for a moment. At the only voice in the world that would nag the young alliance leader of the Sado Alliance for being a picky eater, a smile reflexively formed on his face. As always, Geom Mugeuk could be seen in the alley across the street.

—What brings you here? I thought I’d only see you at your tavern.

—Something came up that I must urgently report to the Alliance Leader.

—Then shouldn’t you go report to him directly?

—How disloyal! You should be there with me.

—Isn’t it because it would take too long to get to the Alliance Leader without me?

Waiting, standing by, receiving permission. Only after passing through all sorts of security could one finally reach the Alliance Leader’s Hall. It was the same at the Murim Alliance. In that sense, Jin Hagun and Bi Sain were shortcuts to the Alliance Leader’s Hall.

—You noticed?

Geom Mugeuk’s laughter rang through the sound transmission.

Of course, Bi Sain knew that was not the reason. He knew Geom Mugeuk had come to find him first in order to place him at the center of events as always.

—What is it?

—Hear it together with the Alliance Leader.

—How much are you trying to startle people this time?

—It’s not that sort of thing.

—Very well.

As Bi Sain moved to stand, Geom Mugeuk stopped him.

—Finish your meal slowly before you get up.

—How am I supposed to swallow my food when you’re waiting there?

—All the more reason to eat well. Since ancient times, a martial artist’s strength comes from rice, does it not? You can wait a hundred days and see whether I get up without eating. Tell me no matter how big an accident you caused and see whether I get up without eating.

Bi Sain shot to his feet and shouted.

“Did you cause trouble?”

* * *

Geom Mugeuk had come to the Alliance Leader’s Hall with Bi Sain.

“So you’re saying the Murim Alliance Leader has also decided to come to this gathering?”

“Yes, that is correct.”

“This has grown into something big.”

“You can think of it as gaining one more drinking friend.”

“And that drinking friend is Jin Paecheon.”

The Sado Alliance Leader, Baek Jagang, disliked Geom Ujin as well, but he disliked Jin Paecheon even more.

“Three people who loathe one another terribly are gathering?”

What had been a private drinking meeting between two people had become a three-party conference of the Orthodox, Unorthodox, and Demonic factions.

Since he had become the Sado Alliance Leader, the three of them had never once met in one place.

“Was this your original plan?”

“No. At first, I intended only for my father and you, Alliance Leader, to meet. But I changed my mind midway.”

“Because inviting the Murim Alliance Leader was easier than asking for his understanding?”

“That was part of it, but a more important reason came up.”

“What reason?”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Because I thought that if not for this chance, the three of you might never have an opportunity to meet together in this generation.”

“!”

Judging by the fact that no goosebumps rose around his ears, Geom Mugeuk was telling the truth.

“What does it matter if we don’t meet?”

“I felt that if it were me, I would regret it when I looked back later. To think that we had dominated an era, and yet the three of us had never once gathered in one place.”

Come to think of it, Baek Jagang realized that he had never given much thought to a three-party conference. He had assumed that when he met them again, it would be on a day when someone would die.

Bi Sain, who had been listening silently beside them, suddenly had this thought.

‘Is that why you come running to me whenever there’s a chance for us to meet?’

In that regard, in their generation, there was no need to worry that the three of them would fail to meet. That Geom Mugeuk would try to call them out and meet at every opportunity.

“Is that what you came here to tell me?”

“This gathering began thanks to you, Alliance Leader, so of course I had to come and tell you in person.”

“If I say I don’t want to? If I say I can’t stand that stuffy old man from the Murim Alliance?”

“Even so, I must do my best to escort you. Whatever you desire, just say the word. I will take responsibility and see it done.”

What did he desire?

What he desired was to raise Bi Sain to be even more impressive than Geom Mugeuk before him. And then to fill Geom Ujin’s insolent eyes with envy.

“I’ll attend. I’ll see you at the tavern.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly and paid his respects.

“Thank you very much for understanding. As expected, Alliance Leader, you are a decisive man.”

Geom Mugeuk bowed politely and was about to turn away when Baek Jagang asked one final question.

“What is your ultimate purpose in this gathering?”

Geom Mugeuk looked back at him and said,

“When Sain here, Unit Leader Jin of the Murim Alliance, and I get together, it’s quite entertaining.”

Bi Sain’s lips twitched as he listened.

Shouldn’t you ask us whether it’s entertaining or not?

He wanted to banter like that, but he could not bring himself to speak so in front of the Sado Alliance Leader.

“Wouldn’t it be entertaining if the three of you gathered as well? My purpose is for this meeting to be an enjoyable one.”

“An enjoyable meeting? We’ll be lucky if the atmosphere isn’t murderous.”

“That would be entertaining in its own way.”

Baek Jagang warned him of one danger.

“Those people who targeted us this time. You said there was someone behind them, didn’t you?”

“That’s right.”

“What will you do if they take advantage of this opportunity?”

Geom Mugeuk answered calmly.

“It will be all right.”

“Why are you so sure?”

“Even when each of you was alone, you blocked them all. This time, the three of you will be together, won’t you?”

A strange smile formed at Baek Jagang’s lips.

‘For now, I suppose I’ll have to be satisfied with second place in raising a disciple.’

Those words also reflected Baek Jagang’s resolve to at least make him triumph over the Murim Alliance’s successor.

Bi Sain, of course, had no way of knowing the meaning contained in his master’s burning gaze toward him.

“Then I will see you that day.”

Bi Sain came out after Geom Mugeuk to see him off.

“You truly aren’t worried.”

“Why should I worry? Now that the three of them have given their permission, the matter is out of my hands.”

Even under a gaze that asked if he wasn’t being far too shameless, Geom Mugeuk did not flinch.

“Let us live in our own murim.”

When he first heard those words, they had felt like empty clouds, but now he could sense a little substance to them. The fact that there was a murim of their own, one belonging to the young.

“Between the matter of escorts and coordinating the schedule, it will take some time. What will you do until then?”

“During the day, I’ll play with the Demon Supremes, and at night, I’ll train. That’s how I’ll spend my time. Don’t stop me from having fun!”

“How nice. You have people to play with.”

“Don’t you have five more people to play with than I do?”

“!”

For an instant, Bi Sain was startled. Five more than the Eight Demon Supremes made thirteen; he must mean the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance. Bi Sain had not once thought of playing with the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance.

“Then I’ll see you later.”

In a flash, Geom Mugeuk became a speck far off in the sky and vanished.

Beneath the sky where he had disappeared, the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance stood in the distance.

Suddenly, a thought came to him. Geom Mugeuk must have started with the Demon Supremes from this same state. No, that wasn’t right. The Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance at least had loyalty. Geom Mugeuk must have become close to the Demon Supremes from a far more difficult and awkward state than this.

Yes. If he was going to imitate him, he ought to do it properly.

“Today, set aside your escort duties. Let’s buy some wine and go somewhere with a fine view. We can share all the stories we’ve fallen behind on. Oh, and is there anyone among you who can dance?”

As startled gazes focused on him, Bi Sain looked up at the sky.

“The weather is too good to play alone.”

Chapter 381: To Me, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.
-------------------------------------------------
"...And so, I invited the Martial Alliance Leader to this meeting as well."

As soon as I returned to the sect, I reported to Father what had happened.

Chief Military Advisor Sama Myeong was also present at today's meeting. After all, he was the one responsible for coordinating the meeting schedule and guard matters.

At my words that I had even invited the Martial Alliance Leader, Father shook his head as if dumbfounded, while Sama Myeong simply stared at me in silence.

"Our Military Advisor had anticipated it would turn out this way, hadn't he?"

Of course, I had naturally expected this. This was the inevitable flow of history. That was what I had wanted to say.

"Which matter are you referring to? Are you speaking of the Young Lord, who suddenly left the sect to save the tavern owner, inviting the Sado Alliance Leader to our main sect? Or are you referring to your visit to ask the Martial Alliance Leader for understanding, only to invite even the Martial Alliance Leader?"

Come to think of it, I had gone out to save Jo Chunbae and his wife and ended up here. I hadn't done it consciously. Perhaps all of this had been born from Jo Chunbae's fate.

"I apologize. I have burdened you with a great matter when you are already busy."

"Do you even understand that it is a great matter?"

If the person who had caused this weren't the Young Lord, and if this weren't a meeting with Father, he might have burst out shouting, asking if I knew what kind of mess I had created.

"The Young Lord has accomplished what no one else could."

"Is that praise?"

Sama Myeong's expression said it all. As if it were praise! Of course, because we were in front of Father, he had said something contrary to his true feelings.

"It is praise."

He had the look of someone itching to pour out a nagging lecture starting with, "However..."

I understood Sama Myeong. Having lived his whole life as a military advisor, he disliked variables and changes. Especially since this matter was likely the greatest incident in his entire career as a strategist.

"There is much to prepare. Especially since this will be the first trilateral meeting, all sorts of chaos are anticipated. The biggest problem among them is the guard issue. Both the Martial Alliance and the Sado Alliance sides will try to bring as many experts as possible. The more people there are, the higher the probability of accidents occurring. I mean that if one wrong step is taken, it could lead to war."

Yet we couldn't tell them to come with only minimal guards either. Thus, advance coordination was absolutely necessary, and the person who would have to wage a war of nerves with both sides' strategists was none other than Sama Myeong himself. I felt sorry for him, but what could I do? Fate had led us here.

I shifted my gaze from Sama Myeong and looked at Father, who was silently looking down at me.

If this were before my regression, I would have trembled at that gaze. I wouldn't have even been able to meet his eyes. That was why my relationship with Father had only regressed rather than improved.

Father was looking at me without much thought. He was probably clicking his tongue, thinking, "What am I going to do with that troublemaker?" Unnecessary worries crept in—had I disappointed him further? Did he regret making me the Young Lord? Was he thinking of my older brother?

I must never forget that imagination, when unfurled alone, is a thing called misunderstanding that tears all its wings to shreds.

"Are you very angry?"

"Of course! Who gave you permission to hold a trilateral meeting? Military Advisor, contact the Martial Alliance and the Sado Alliance immediately and tell them it's canceled!"

Father's voice echoed loudly through the Cult Leader's hall.

However, there was no irritation or anger in Father's eyes as he looked at me.

I could tell. Father was getting angry on my behalf to ease Sama Myeong's mood. That scolding was also for my sake.

"You can't, Father!"

"What do you mean, can't!"

Sama Myeong cautiously stepped forward.

"Please calm yourself, Cult Leader. Since the Young Lord has already given his word, I believe it would be best to proceed as planned."

This way, I could preserve Sama Myeong's dignity and give him a chance to take my side.

"Military Advisor, thank you."

"It is fortunate. The Young Lord already has a military advisor, after all."

With an expression that said exactly how hard it must be to live as my military advisor, he shook his head vigorously. That was our Chief Military Advisor, one who never let things go.

"You go too far!"

With Sama Myeong cracking a joke like this, the mood lightened somewhat.

I looked at Father again.

What could he be thinking right now? Did he want to see the two Alliance Leaders? Even after watching him so intently, Father was truly difficult to read.

Anyway, I had already stirred up trouble. There was something I absolutely had to tell Father.

"This meeting may be a meeting of three people, but it will have a great influence on us three as well. Where else could we learn? It is a once-in-a-lifetime meeting between a Cult Leader and the Alliance Leaders."

And I relayed to him those same words I had spoken to others.

"I acted trusting in you, Father."

In the end, that characteristic sneer formed at the corner of Father's mouth. Even though I hadn't seen it in a long time, it suited him well.

"Then I shall take my leave."

As I turned and walked out, the back of my head burned.

So why do you dream of unifying the martial world? I will stop you until the very end, Father.

Indeed, the gazes burning into the back of my head weren't two, but three, were they?

Before leaving the Heavenly Demon Hall, I shouted into the empty air.

"Uncle Hwi! I'm sorry."

I hadn't forgotten to convey my apology to Hwi, my bodyguard. He was another person who would be on edge because of the trilateral meeting.

* * *

"Elder!"

After leaving the Cult Leader's hall, I immediately headed to Namdojong, where Hyeolcheon Doma was.

I ran to Hyeolcheon Doma's quarters as if reuniting with a long-lost family member. Since I had only seen Gwonma last time before leaving the sect again, it had been a while since I had reunited with Hyeolcheon Doma.

There was no one in the courtyard.

So I walked slowly and peered inside through the window. Then, an unexpected sight greeted me.

Hyeolcheon Doma was lying on the bed, asleep.

"It isn't even time for him to sleep yet."

I was about to turn back when I flinched. Hyeolcheon Doma taking a nap? That had never happened before.

I turned back and carefully opened the door, entering inside. I walked noiselessly, in case I might wake him.

But at the last moment, I turned my steps from the bed toward the bookshelf. I stood before it and was examining the new books Hyeolcheon Doma had bought, when...

"Didn't you come in because you were worried about me?"

I turned to see Hyeolcheon Doma looking at me, his eyes open.

"I wasn't?"

As if he weren't. He wouldn't know how startled I had been in that instant. When I entered the room, I had sensed Hyeolcheon Doma suppressing his breath, so I had stealthily changed direction.

"I was ready to scare you senseless if you approached to check my breathing."

"Why would I check your breathing? Is Elder someone who would pass away in bed? You would go out fighting gloriously on the battlefield, leaning on the Heaven-Annihilating Great Blade."

"You brat! Do you speak so easily of death to an old man?"

But contrary to his words, an uncontrollable smile formed at his lips. For that was the death he dreamed of as well.

"But why are you napping when you've never done so in your life?"

"I sleep occasionally. Wait until you're old, too."

Was it just my imagination? His voice lacked the strength of his usual crotchety self.

"Did you handle your business well?"

"I stirred up trouble."

"It would be strange if you didn't stir up trouble, running around like that. What kind of trouble?"

For now, only Father and Sama Myeong knew of this matter; it was top secret, unknown to others.

"I invited the Sado Alliance Leader to the entertainment house."

So how surprised could he have been?

"Who did you invite?"

"The Sado Alliance Leader."

"The Sado Alliance Leader I know? Baek Jagang?"

"You know him?"

"I know him."

He truly was a man with far-reaching connections and acquaintances.

"That can't be? That suspicious man accepted your invitation?"

With an expression of disbelief, Hyeolcheon Doma raised himself from the bed and poured some cold tea from the tea tray beside him to drink.

"I invited one more person. That's why I'm worried."

Hyeolcheon Doma answered nonchalantly.

"If you invited the Sado Alliance Leader, who would dare cause trouble? As long as it's not the Martial Alliance Leader."

A brief silence fell.

Startled, Hyeolcheon Doma looked at Geom Mugeuk and blinked.

"No?"

When he received no answer, Hyeolcheon Doma asked again.

"It isn't, is it?"

"..."

"Surely not?"

"Why wouldn't it be?"

As he spoke, he deliberately clutched his head in mock agony.

"I don't even know why I'm like this!"

Though he joked around, Hyeolcheon Doma did not react. The gaze he directed at me was deep.

"The Martial Alliance Leader accepted the invitation as well? How on earth? Even for you, that's..."

"I don't think they accepted because of me."

"Then why?"

"The three of them must have harbored a desire to see one another at least once. Otherwise, could this matter have come to pass?"

As Hyeolcheon Doma nodded silently, he revealed a cold aura and spoke.

"The two Alliance Leaders are coming to our main sect? This is a chance to take care of both sides at once."

After speaking as if it were his own thought, Hyeolcheon Doma quickly added.

"What are you going to do if the Cult Leader thinks this way?"

Hyeolcheon Doma believed that Father might harbor such thoughts.

"At least this time, he won't."

"How can you be sure?"

Honestly, I couldn't be certain. I simply trusted Father.

"Because they are guests invited by his son."

"What if he values the great cause more than his son?"

"Then please stop him, Elder. Only you can stop him."

We looked at each other in silence.

Just then, Seo Daeryong entered.

"Huh? Young Lord!"

Seo Daeryong approached me gladly upon seeing me.

"When did you arrive?"

"Today. What's this? Your eyes have changed?"

I could tell at once that Seo Daeryong had achieved great advancement in martial arts in the time I hadn't seen him. Until now, his skills had steadily improved bit by bit, but seeing him today, he felt distinctly stronger.

Then, a startling truth was revealed.

"Yesterday, Master opened my Ren and Du meridians for me."

Only now did Geom Mugeuk understand. Hyeolcheon Doma hadn't been joking around; he had truly been tired and napping.

Medicine ingredients were in Seo Daeryong's hands. It seemed he had gone to the Demonic Physician to have a tonic prescribed.

"Let me examine your condition."

I didn't ask, "May I examine you?" Because he knew how worried I was, Hyeolcheon Doma didn't resist when I took his wrist and sent a stream of inner energy into him.

"I'm fine."

Fortunately, he was fine. His martial arts were so strong and his inner power so profound that he had opened the Ren and Du meridians without needing to use his Innate True Qi.

Opening the Ren and Du meridians.

A process that ordinary experts must go through to reach the level of a transcendent master. I had thought I would do it for Seo Daeryong someday since taking him as my direct disciple, and that day had already come. It was proof of how hard Seo Daeryong had trained.

Geom Mugeuk gloated on Hyeolcheon Doma's behalf.

"You know, don't you? Opening the Ren and Du meridians is so dangerous that it's only done for one's flesh and blood."

"I know."

"Then that's that."

Seo Daeryong's eyes were burning. I could tell without looking how grateful he must have been, given his personality.

"I'll go warm the medicine I brewed. The Demonic Physician said this medicine is the most effective."

Hyeolcheon Doma nagged Seo Daeryong.

"You brat, how much is your monthly salary that you go buying medicine?"

"I may not look it, but I am the Lord of the Yellow Heaven Pavilion. I earn a lot of money."

Seo Daeryong went outside to warm the medicine.

When I looked at Hyeolcheon Doma with touched eyes, he raised his voice needlessly.

"I didn't do it for him. If he's my disciple, that much is expected."

Now, hearing him say "my disciple" no longer felt awkward.

"I like you the most, Elder."

Hyeolcheon Doma lay back down on the bed, feeling awkward at the sentiment.

"Enough. I'm going to sleep more."

As if I would just leave because he said so. I plopped down at the foot of the bed.

"Actually, though I put on a bold front, how could I not be worried? What if the meeting I envisioned turns into a truly dangerous situation? What if Father really holds up a severed head with blood dripping from both hands and smiles at me?"

"Why complain to me about it?"

"Then who else am I supposed to say this to?"

"Go tell that mask-wearer. Or that drunkard, or that poison-user."

I pretended not to hear and continued.

"Was I only afraid of Father? What if Elder dislikes them? What if you have hidden grudges with the Sado Alliance Leader or Martial Alliance Leader that I don't know about? What if you can't endure it when we meet and try to kill them?"

Hyeolcheon Doma spoke with his eyes closed.

"If it were that deep a grudge, I would have sought them out long ago."

"Right?"

"You never know. One of the Demon Lords might unleash emotions they've been suppressing that day."

"No, that can't happen!"

As I was pretending to be in agony, Seo Daeryong came in with the warmed medicine.

"Master, take your medicine!"

"I should eat that medicine instead. I'm so anxious I'm going to die first."

As I tried to snatch the medicine, Seo Daeryong escaped using footwork.

"This is Master's!"

"Since when did you care for the Elder this much? Pavilion Lord Seo, what about me! Me! The former Lord of the Yellow Heaven Pavilion, the Young Lord of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult! The one you serve as your right-hand man!"

"Until Master finishes all this medicine, you only have one arm!"

Watching Seo Daeryong respectfully offer the medicine to Hyeolcheon Doma, a smile naturally formed on my lips. In my life before the regression, these two had never even exchanged a single word.

Over the present scene, I could see the image of Seo Daeryong entering my office with that gloomy expression when we first met. Overlapping that was the image of him drunk, clinging to Hyeolcheon Doma's leg. And the image of him overjoyed at receiving a great blade as a gift.

And over this scene, what other image would overlap? When all those images overlapped for the final time, what picture would remain?

After Hyeolcheon Doma took the medicine and lay back down, Seo Daeryong and I quietly left the room.

Then Hyeolcheon Doma spoke with his eyes closed.

"Everything will work out."

At that heavy yet calm single sentence, my heart grew at ease. Truly at ease.

Even though I had regressed, even though my life looked perfect no matter how hard I tried, I too was someone who needed to hear such words. That I was doing well, that everything would be okay. To me, Hyeolcheon Doma was that kind of person.

"Inspector Seo, let's sleep with our Elder today."

"Very well."

Swept up in the mood, Seo Daeryong showed no fear.

At that, Hyeolcheon Doma's eyes shot open and he shouted.

"Who decided that! Go! Sleep at home!"

Hearing Hyeolcheon Doma's shout, I finally felt that I had returned home. If someone asked how one could walk such a perilous path, I would answer that it was because there was a place to return to.

"I said go, you brats!"

If I had intended to listen obediently, I wouldn't have brought it up in the first place.

With an excited face, I quickly ran out and shouted.

"I'll go get my sleepwear! Let's go, Right Hand!"

Chapter 382: Lord Soma Has a Big Face.
--------------------------------------
Others might not believe it if they heard, but we really did bring our sleeping clothes and return.

“Are we allowed to do this?”

“Of course we are!”

“Now that I think about it, there’s some Netherworld Pavilion business I need to handle urgently.”

Having belatedly come to his senses, Seo Daeryong tried to escape from our midnight adventure, but I was not about to let him go so easily.

“When will we ever get to do something like this again? And think about it carefully. He told us to go, but do you think he meant it? He must be wondering, Why aren’t those brats coming? When I play with them, I feel younger, and I’m this happy.”

Would he really? Half convinced and half doubtful, Seo Daeryong moved his feet. There was a reason he, who feared his master so much, was pushing himself today.

“I never dreamed I would have my Ren and Du meridians opened.”

Seo Daeryong was caught up in a feverish excitement. Gratitude toward his master, worry for him, the thrill of having opened his Ren and Du meridians, the joy of seeing me again after so long—all of it was tangled together, leaving him in a state of exhilaration. It felt like if he caused trouble today, he would truly cause it properly.

When we returned to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s residence, he had fallen asleep. This time, he really was asleep. We stood by the window and watched him lying on the bed, sleeping soundly, then quietly left the place.

‘Sleep well, Elder.’

Together with Seo Daeryong, I walked through the inner compound of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“It’s been a long time since I walked like this with you, Young Cult Leader.”

“That’s because our Pavilion Master Seo is so busy.”

“You should at least show your face before saying that.”

I laughed awkwardly, and Seo Daeryong laughed along.

“Time really does pass quickly. Each day feels difficult, but when I turn around, several days have gone by, and before I know it, a month has passed. Is that how it’s supposed to be?”

After asking without thinking, Seo Daeryong shook his head.

“I’m older than you, yet here I am asking the Young Cult Leader about the passage of time.”

I wanted to tell him. Your young years have yet to reach even a quarter of their true speed. They are still only at the third star of Swift Steps. When time reaches Great Accomplishment, it sweeps away every memory and runs far into the distance.

In that sense, shall we spend some precious time?

“Shall we go to our Extreme Evil Soma?”

Seo Daeryong let out a cry of, “Good heavens!”

“You might as well say you hate me. Better yet, challenge me to a duel and beat me senseless!”

“Is Lord Soma that frightening?”

“Then is he not frightening?”

“You lived with him for a while before, didn’t you?”

It was when we dealt with Yaryul Han’s subordinates.

While the Poison King and I carried out our operation, Seo Daeryong had stayed with the Extreme Evil Soma for a short time. He had personally gone to the marketplace to buy side dishes and cooking ingredients, then made meals for the Extreme Evil Soma.

“You got close back then, didn’t you?”

“Close? I couldn’t even meet his eyes! I kept wondering when you would come, Young Cult Leader, when you would come. Do you know how anxious I was?”

“If you talk to him, you’ll realize he’s a good person. If he were that terrifying, would I like him this much?”

I could feel Seo Daeryong’s resolve waver slightly.

“Imagine looking back on today ten years from now. Ah, how enjoyable it would have been if I had gone with the Young Cult Leader to see the Extreme Evil Soma back then. Why did I hesitate so much and get so scared?”

When I finished speaking and looked back, Seo Daeryong was already running far away. Having opened his Ren and Du meridians, he ran faster than before.

Yes, if it were that easy, how could he be the Extreme Evil Soma?

“At least help me move the bed before you go!”

Far away, Seo Daeryong stopped.

“The bed? What bed?”

“You wouldn’t know because you haven’t been there, but there’s nothing in Lord Soma’s residence. If we’re going to sleep there, we need to bring a bed.”

Seo Daeryong blinked.

“So you’re taking a bed to the Valley of Villains?”

“We can’t visit in the middle of the night and ask him to bring out a bed, can we? And we can’t sleep on the bare floor either. I want to sleep comfortably in my own bed.”

“But you’re really taking the bed?”

We really took it.

Seo Daeryong and I carried the bed from my residence. I held the front, and Seo Daeryong held the back.

The demonic practitioners standing guard stared at us in surprise. On top of the bed were neatly folded clean bedding and even sleeping clothes, so it drew even more attention.

“You could move this by yourself, couldn’t you?”

“It’s heavy.”

“Heavy? You’re someone who could float it through the air with Object-Seizing Across the Void. I’ll just carry it myself.”

“I can’t make the Netherworld Pavilion Master do something like that.”

“This is more embarrassing! In any case, I’ll only move it for you and then leave right away. I made that clear! You can’t say otherwise later.”

When we arrived at the entrance to the Valley of Villains, Seo Daeryong recalled the past.

“Do you remember? When you first came to meet the Extreme Evil Soma, Young Cult Leader, I was waiting here.”

“I remember. You even brought a sword, ready to rush in and save me if anything happened.”

“That was how it was, and now I’m going all the way into Lord Soma’s residence.”

“You brought your sword, right?”

“Please don’t joke like that. My heart feels like it’s about to burst as it is.”

Seo Daeryong took several deep breaths to calm his trembling chest.

“Don’t be nervous. You’re someone who will become a Demon Venerable, aren’t you?”

“When you say things like that, I get even more nervous! The fact that I’m going to become a Demon Venerable is still the most shocking and nerve-racking thing in the world to me. Even now, I sometimes have nightmares. Dreams where I’m living as a Netherworld Pavilion investigator, endlessly gloomy and full of complaints about the cult. Dreams where, when I first went in to meet you, someone else was sitting in front of me.”

“Even then, it would have changed.”

“What?”

“Your fate didn’t change because of me. You changed it yourself.”

Seo Daeryong fell silent, perhaps thinking about something.

At last, we entered the Valley of Villains.

Not only the Faceless Ones guarding the entrance, but even the Faceless Ones passing by looked at us strangely.

—Everyone is looking at us like we’re strange. That yellow-striped mask over there just laughed.

—Should I get rid of him?

—The number of people we need to get rid of keeps increasing.

In that way, we arrived at the Extreme Evil Soma’s residence.

* * *

“Lord Soma, I’ve come to impose on you for the day!”

As he watched Geom Mugeuk enter cheerfully, the Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes smiled from within the hollow openings of his white mask.

Who in the world would come to him and say they wanted to stay the night? Who would come carrying even a bed and sleeping clothes?

“Were you driven out of your home?”

“I’ve caused some major trouble lately, so I’m on the verge of being driven out.”

“You are welcome anytime, Young Cult Leader.”

“If you keep saying that, Lord Soma, you’ll be driven out with me.”

The Extreme Evil Soma laughed aloud. It had been a long time since I had heard his laughter.

“Your mask has changed.”

“As expected! You noticed.”

It seemed that after Cheongmyeon left, a new Faceless One had started making masks.

“Is the mask more comfortable?”

“Please tell Cheongmyeon. Tell him it is far more comfortable than the one he made.”

“I’ll be sure to tell him.”

Because that would be how the Extreme Evil Soma conveyed his longing to Cheongmyeon.

This time, Seo Daeryong greeted him respectfully.

“It has been a long time.”

Seo Daeryong was inwardly surprised. He had heard there was nothing here, but he had not thought there would truly be nothing at all. There was not even a speck of dust. Ah, there was something that caught the eye. Long lines drawn on the wall came into view. Horizontal lines and vertical lines. Why had those been left as they were in this spotless room?

“Your gaze has improved.”

When the Extreme Evil Soma said this with eyes colder than anything, Seo Daeryong was confused.

‘That’s praise, right? This is a compliment, isn’t it?’

He was so frightened of the Extreme Evil Soma that it sounded like, “Your eyes look good, so let’s have a bout.”

“Recently, my master gave me a great teaching. It is thanks to him.”

The Extreme Evil Soma only nodded once in silence.

Geom Mugeuk asked the Extreme Evil Soma.

“Where should we put the bed?”

“Wherever is comfortable for you.”

“Then we’ll put it here.”

We placed the bed against one wall. It was the first moment that furniture was placed in the Extreme Evil Soma’s empty room.

Why had I brought a bed? Because I wanted the Extreme Evil Soma to sleep in one now.

The whiteness of the walls was related to the martial cultivation method he practiced, but there was a different reason why he slept sitting up.

Before my regression, the Extreme Evil Soma had wanted to become friends with me. There was something he had once said.

—How could one who bears the name Extreme Evil sleep comfortably? It is the smallest and only conscience I possess.

At the time, I had thought it was bravado spoken because he wanted to get close to me. I had assumed he surely had a separate, splendid bedchamber somewhere.

After my regression, as I formed a new relationship with the Extreme Evil Soma, I came to understand that those words had been sincere.

Back then, I had not told him. No, even if I had known he was sincere, I would not have said it. At the time, I had not opened my heart to anyone.

Now it was time to tell him.

‘Sleep comfortably now.’

Because he was living a different life. Because he would continue living a different life from now on.

That was why today’s bed was the first step toward developing my relationship with the Extreme Evil Soma another stage further.

“Could you lend me this side of the wall altogether? I think it’ll be annoying to move it every time.”

The Extreme Evil Soma looked at Geom Mugeuk for a moment. At this sudden request, he readily nodded.

“Do as you wish.”

“Thank you. Next time I come, I’ll bring a tea table to put beside the bed. And here, I’ll put a small wardrobe to store clothes to change into. This wall is mine now.”

Seo Daeryong watched the scene in astonishment.

‘You’re moving your household in entirely? Into Lord Extreme Evil Soma’s room?’

He had originally intended to say farewell and leave, but curiosity arose in him as to why Geom Mugeuk was doing this.

Seo Daeryong said to the Extreme Evil Soma,

“If you feel this is tyranny on the Young Cult Leader’s part, please report it to the Netherworld Pavilion. I will investigate at once.”

It was the joke of Seo Daeryong’s lifetime, one he had put his heart into.

Knowing how much courage he must have mustered, Geom Mugeuk forced himself to hold back his laughter and kept a straight face.

The Extreme Evil Soma silently stared at him, his gaze seeming to say he did not know how to take it.

‘What did I just say? I joked with the Extreme Evil Soma? Seo Daeryong, have you lost your mind?’

Seo Daeryong’s voice transmission flew to Geom Mugeuk.

—Save me! Please smooth this over!

Of course, Geom Mugeuk had no intention of helping him out of this amusing situation.

—It seems opening your Ren and Du meridians made your guts bigger too. Who should we appoint as the next Netherworld Pavilion Master?

Just as Seo Daeryong’s panic reached its peak, the Extreme Evil Soma spoke.

“It should be fine. We will paint all the furniture white as well.”

At his flat reply, Seo Daeryong forced a smile and said,

“I think white suits this room well.”

With those words, he was just about to hurry and say farewell.

The Extreme Evil Soma approached Seo Daeryong.

‘Why is he coming? Was that joke unforgivable after all?’

The Extreme Evil Soma stood beside Seo Daeryong, who flinched.

“Considering the furniture arrangement, I think the wardrobe would be better placed over here.”

“Would it?”

The Extreme Evil Soma was merely standing beside him, yet Seo Daeryong felt like he could not breathe.

‘How was the Young Cult Leader able to become so close with someone this frightening?’

When one looked at the relationship between the Young Cult Leader and the Demon Venerables, it went beyond admiration and became almost awe-inspiring.

Seo Daeryong stealthily took one step to the side. In his heart, he wanted to go all the way to the opposite side, but he did not dare.

‘ I was insane. Why did I come here?’

To be honest, half of it had been curiosity. What would the Extreme Evil Soma’s residence be like? The other half had been filled with bravado and bluster.

I’m the Netherworld Pavilion Master now, and I’ve even opened my Ren and Du meridians, so shouldn’t I be able to come here at least once?

Able, my foot. Why would you walk into hell with your own two feet?

Just as he was looking for an opportunity to say farewell.

The Extreme Evil Soma glanced at Seo Daeryong. The two eyes within the holes of his mask certainly seemed to be smiling, but that made them all the more frightening.

In any case, now that their eyes had met, this was his chance.

‘I will be taking my leave now!’

Just as he was about to say those words, the Extreme Evil Soma spoke first.

“I was greatly indebted to you back then.”

“Pardon?”

At the gaze that seemed to ask, Have you forgotten already? Seo Daeryong’s mind went blank, and he was rendered speechless.

“I mean that you worked hard cooking for me, when I am such a finicky eater.”

It was about the time when he had cooked for him in the past. Seo Daeryong had once joked, when the Extreme Evil Soma was not there, that he seemed to have a small appetite, only to be caught and terrified then as well. You still remember that?

“Not at all. It was only what I ought to have done.”

He even put on a smile and was about to say farewell, but this time Geom Mugeuk began telling him about what had happened while he had been away from the cult.

“I don’t know where I should begin.”

Earlier, he had told the story while sitting on the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s bed, but now he spoke while sitting on the bed he had brought himself.

How he had killed the one who had infiltrated the Sado Alliance, how he had come to invite the Sado Alliance Leader, and how he had even come to invite the Murim Alliance Leader as well.

Seo Dae-ryong had heard some of it earlier, while there were parts he had not caught in detail because he had been warming the medicine. As he listened, he found it so interesting that he forgot he was supposed to leave and became absorbed in Geom Mugeuk’s story.

When there was a brief lull in the conversation, Seo Dae-ryong sent a sound transmission and asked.

—But why are you telling him everything in such detail?

He had asked because he thought there must be a reason, and there truly was.

—That way, if some situation arises later, he’ll be able to deal with it on his own. Information is power.

He was not telling the Blood Heaven Blade Demon or Geugak Soma in such detail because he wanted to chatter. He was laying it all out like this in preparation for the one-in-ten-thousand chance of what might happen in the future.

At any rate, he had heard the story well enough. Now that he had heard it twice, there was absolutely no need to hear it again.

“I’ll be…”

Just as he was about to say he would take his leave, this time Geugak Soma brought up the subject of martial arts first.

When that conversation ended, Geom Mugeuk asked after the mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

The story of how Yeo Jeong, the woman who loved Geugak Soma, was expanding her courtesan-house business throughout the Central Plains was so interesting that Seo Dae-ryong feared someone might say, “You may leave now.”

Then they also discussed the state of affairs in murim.

Seo Dae-ryong learned something for the first time. Geugak Soma possessed far broader knowledge in various fields than he had expected, and he was someone who enjoyed conversation.

In the end, when the topic of the masks from the Valley of Villains came up again, Seo Dae-ryong could not hold himself back. Had he not been trained by Geom Mugeuk’s glibness? His head and heart held out, but his mouth did not.

“I may not look it, but my head and face are rather small, so masks suit me well.”

Geugak Soma stared at him silently. Beside him, Geom Mugeuk put on an unnecessarily startled expression, creating an atmosphere of terror.

“No, I don’t mean that your face is large, Lord Soma.”

That’s not it! Geom Mugeuk looked even more shocked.

Geugak Soma sprang to his feet and approached Seo Dae-ryong.

‘I should have left earlier.’

Seo Dae-ryong squeezed his eyes shut.

Geugak Soma passed by Seo Dae-ryong, went outside, and soon came back in.

In his hand was a white mask. It was a newly made, brand-new mask.

“Try it on.”

Seo Dae-ryong’s heart pounded like mad.

If the mask doesn’t fit, I’ll tear off everything that sticks out from it with these hands! Is that what this is?

Fortunately, the mask fit. It was a little tight, but he forced himself to act as though it fit perfectly.

“It fits exactly!”

“It is a gift.”

“Thank you.”

Seo Dae-ryong was bewildered. He truly had not expected Geugak Soma to give him a mask as a gift.

After listening to the two of them talk until dawn, Seo Dae-ryong dozed off.

He had thought there was no way he would ever sleep in Geugak Soma’s room. And yet he had dozed off while sitting on the floor, his back leaning against the bed.

When he woke, Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma were standing side by side by the window, gazing at the faintly brightening dawn.

They were not even conversing through sound transmission. They were simply standing together in silence.

More than the sight of the two of them talking earlier, the sight of their backs as they stood looking silently in the same direction remained etched in Seo Dae-ryong’s heart.

Then Geugak Soma turned around.

Startled, Seo Dae-ryong closed his eyes and pretended to be asleep.

‘I have to go. Please, I have to go.’

As he lay there pretending to sleep, Seo Dae-ryong actually fell asleep.

When he opened his eyes, the sun was high in the sky.

Before him, Geugak Soma was looking down at him. He was so startled he nearly screamed. Desperately holding back his cry, he looked around and saw that Geom Mugeuk had already left.

Yes, that man was a devil.

A devil who would leave him behind in hell for the sake of amusement. Yes, in the first place, who had dragged him into this white hell?

Geugak Soma asked bluntly.

“When exactly are you going to leave?”

Clutching the mask he had received as a gift, Seo Dae-ryong finally uttered the words he had wanted to say all night.

“I’ll be taking my leave now.”

Chapter 383: When Having a Drink with Spring Greens.
----------------------------------------------------
Seo Daeryong left the building and gulped down the breath he had been holding.

To think he had fallen asleep in the room of the Extreme Evil Demon. It still did not feel real, as if he were dreaming.

He could not even remember how he had gotten out of that room and made it all the way here. In any case.

‘I’m alive!’

Seo Daeryong looked up at the sunlight pouring down. It felt as if he had barely survived a battlefield.

‘Still, how could he just leave me behind like that?’

Just then, the Faceless Guest guarding the entrance to the building spoke to him.

“The Young Cult Leader left word that you should stop by his residence before you go.”

“Thank you.”

Seo Daeryong quickened his steps toward Geom Mugeuk’s residence.

‘I’m going even if he tells me not to. I’m challenging him to a duel. Master must have foreseen this and opened my Ren and Du meridians for me!’

And so Seo Daeryong ran like the wind, entering Geom Mugeuk’s residence as he shouted.

“Let’s have a bout!”

In that instant, Seo Daeryong flinched and fell silent.

When he saw the person in the courtyard turn to look at him, he cried out without realizing it.

“Good heavens!”

The person who had turned around was none other than the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign. He had just shouted a challenge to the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign.

“Pavilion Master Seo?”

“Sword Sovereign!”

Seo Daeryong respectfully cupped his fist, and the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign also greeted him with the courtesy due to a pavilion master.

“You wish to fight me? I would be glad to.”

Completely immersed in martial training, she was the sort who would run ten thousand li for a spar.

“No, no. That was not directed at you, Sword Sovereign.”

“Then was it directed at the Young Cult Leader?”

It was far too awkward to answer that it had been. He had thought he had escaped one battlefield, only to find himself on another.

‘Ah! I’ve fallen into a double trap!’

Because he felt this way, he did not notice that the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign was looking at him with admiration.

The aura he possessed when she had first seen Seo Daeryong could not compare to what he had now.

‘Has there ever been a demonic martial artist who grew this quickly?’

Moreover, as she looked at his somehow crooked yet lively eyes, one person naturally came to mind. In his younger days, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had given off exactly this sort of feeling. With that great saber at his side, the resemblance was even stronger.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk came out from inside carrying a bowl of water.

“Our Pavilion Master Seo has grown a great deal, hasn’t he? It’s about time he crossed blades with you, Sword Sovereign.”

Seo Daeryong nearly jumped out of his skin, waving his hands so fast they were almost invisible.

“No, that is a misunderstanding! What are you saying?”

Seo Daeryong looked at Geom Mugeuk and cursed him with his eyes.

‘Are you really going to do this to me?’

Geom Mugeuk handed the water he had brought out to the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign.

“Pavilion Master Seo and I are close, so we often joke around.”

The Ilhwa Sword Sovereign smiled. Of course, she knew perfectly well what their relationship was like. That was why she also knew that the person Seo Daeryong had challenged earlier had been Geom Mugeuk. It was not strange at all. After all, this Geom Mugeuk was someone who could get along familiarly even with the owner of a tavern in Maga Village.

Just then, the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign bade Geom Mugeuk farewell.

“Thank you for today’s spar.”

“Not at all. I learned a great deal.”

If only that had truly been the case. Geom Mugeuk had grown even faster than she had. Whenever she sparred with him, she felt the same emotion the Fist Demon felt when looking at a cliff.

The Ilhwa Sword Sovereign also bade Seo Daeryong farewell.

“Let us meet again, Pavilion Master Seo.”

“Until then, please take care, Sword Sovereign.”

As the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign was about to leave, she added as if something had just occurred to her.

“Ah, I heard that the flowerpot I once gave the Young Cult Leader as a gift is now being tended by Pavilion Master Seo.”

Seo Daeryong flinched when she suddenly brought up the flowerpot.

“Is it still alive?”

“Of course. It is growing well.”

In my office, it’s taller than I am. That fellow is the number one there!

The Ilhwa Sword Sovereign had not brought it up to tease him. Her gaze deepened.

“Just as the flower will bloom from that bud, Pavilion Master Seo will also bloom in full.”

It was a blessing, an expression of her hope that Seo Daeryong, who had become so remarkable that one had to look at him anew, would henceforth make his name known as a martial artist. That was why she had mentioned the flowerpot.

After the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign left, Seo Daeryong asked Geom Mugeuk in a daze.

“She just praised me, didn’t she?”

“It sounded like a warning not to kill the flower.”

“Young Cult Leader!”

Only then did Geom Mugeuk smile and nod.

“The Sword Sovereign has properly acknowledged you.”

The emotion that brushed past his eyes lasted only a moment before Seo Daeryong began to complain.

“What good does that do me? My life is over now. Do you know what it feels like to open your eyes and find the Extreme Evil Demon staring at you? Have you ever even heard of such a thing happening in your life?”

“What are you talking about?”

“She asked when on earth I was going to leave.”

“I’ve never imposed on anyone like that before.”

“Thanks to a certain someone, I had to hear that from the Extreme Evil Demon. Even hearing that from an ordinary person would be nerve-racking and burdensome. Ah! My life is ruined now. How could you do this to me? How could you leave me behind?”

“I didn’t intend to.”

“You did. You were looking forward to the moment I opened my eyes in the morning and locked gazes with the Extreme Evil Demon!”

“If I had been, I would have hidden outside the window to watch.”

“Huh? That’s true.”

Seo Daeryong blinked. The Young Cult Leader he knew would definitely have teased him right there on the spot.

“This morning, I received word that the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign would be visiting.”

“Then you should have woken me up and brought me out.”

Then came a shocking answer.

“The Extreme Evil Demon stopped me.”

“What?”

“When she saw how deeply you were sleeping, she told me to leave you there.”

Seo Daeryong was startled.

“Really? That’s a lie!”

Seo Daeryong could not believe it, but judging by Geom Mugeuk’s expression, it did not seem to be a lie.

“Why would the Extreme Evil Demon do that?”

“I don’t know. Perhaps she took a liking to you.”

“So why on earth would she?”

“I told you, I don’t know.”

Though he said that, Geom Mugeuk could guess. The Extreme Evil Demon must have liked Seo Daeryong’s life: how a mere investigator had become the pavilion master of Hwangcheon Pavilion and the chief disciple of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Of course, it was also because she knew he was someone Geom Mugeuk cherished.

Seo Daeryong looked down at the white mask he was holding.

“Then did she give me this gift out of goodwill too?”

“Ask her yourself next time.”

“Next time?”

He was about to wave his hands and say there would be no next time, but stopped. Was it because they had spent a day together? Or was it because of what Geom Mugeuk had said? The Extreme Evil Demon did not feel as frightening as she had yesterday.

“That can’t be… What am I, after all?”

As he lowered his head deeply, Geom Mugeuk spoke in a calm tone.

“You are a cold-hearted, power-oriented man, yet a real man who loves only one woman. You are a rebel who raises his hand alone when everyone else stays silent, yet a pacifist. You are the winner of the martial arts tournament, who made a life-changing decision over a drink at an inn. And you are my right arm, with a good eye besides.”

The last phrase, “with a good eye besides,” was a joke he had exchanged with Seo Daeryong before, when Seo Daeryong had discovered the hidden Ten-Thousand Poisons Ultimate Divine Pill.

“You still remember all of that.”

“They’re words that describe you. Of course I remember.”

Seo Daeryong’s chest grew hot.

The person before him never gave him a moment to rest. Was our relationship really only this much? If we went a little farther, wouldn’t something else be waiting for us? He pushed him from behind and struck at his heart. Don’t grow weary of relationships. Let’s not fall into the swamp of boredom.

He was someone who was always joking and making him flustered, but his words and actions always carried a deep meaning.

Just look at yesterday and today. In a single night, he had helped Seo Daeryong form deep ties with two Demon Venerables. If not for Geom Mugeuk, how could he ever have naturally built relationships with them? Would he even have had the chance to say a word to two Demon Venerables?

At times like this, if Geom Mugeuk boasted even a little, Seo Daeryong could joke back in turn. But Geom Mugeuk, as if he were about to go somewhere again, washed himself, changed clothes, and hurried to leave.

“Where are you going now?”

“If the cult only had about three Demon Venerables, I wouldn’t be this busy.”

Meeting the Demon Venerables itself was preparation for the tripartite summit, and at the same time, martial training. At his current level, he could now gain a new insight into martial learning from a single joke exchanged with a Demon Venerable.

“Did you tell the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign everything that happened while you were away from the cult?”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“You truly are diligent.”

He had to be. Had he not experienced it many times already? The side that defended was always at a disadvantage. Those who plotted schemes searched for openings in their opponents and dug relentlessly into their weaknesses.

“You must be the busiest Young Cult Leader in history.”

“You have a lot to do too, don’t you?”

“Me?”

“When they come, we must show them that the cult’s discipline is in order. You are the person most responsible for that.”

As Seo Daeryong nodded with a serious expression, messenger hawks flew one after another over their heads.

They could also see messenger hawks flying in the opposite direction. There were definitely more wings coming and going than usual.

“It has begun, hasn’t it?”

At Seo Daeryong’s question, Geom Mugeuk nodded.

While the two of them were conversing on the ground, the strategists’ exchanges for the tripartite summit were busily coming and going across that sky.

* * *

The Drunken Demon’s reaction was different from what I expected.

Ordinarily, he would have reacted like this.

—You came last again. Yes, I’m a fish you’ve already caught. No, I don’t even count as a fish. I’m just a pebble put in the fishbowl for decoration, aren’t I?

But today, his reaction was different.

“You’re here?”

What was with this relaxed response? Was he actually sober? But he reeked of alcohol.

“What is this? Do you have to smile like that to use the final secret technique of your exclusive martial art?”

“What nonsense are you talking about?”

“I’m asking why you aren’t angry that I came late.”

“My favorite younger brother has come to see me. Why would I be angry?”

I backed away.

“Who are you? So those bastards weren’t only targeting the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable.”

“Sit down. It’s nice to see you after so long. Let’s drink all night today.”

“This is strange. Really strange.”

Just then, Yeo Bin, one of the three great drunkards, brought food over.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk saw it. The instant the Drunken Demon glanced at her, he could tell where the change in him had come from. A subtle current flowed between the two of them.

‘Something happened between them!’

As soon as Yeo Bin withdrew, I asked the Drunken Demon.

“Did something happen with Martial Artist Yeo?”

“No.”

“You’re going to tell me anyway, aren’t you? Then start with what you want to say. As much as you like.”

“If I say it the moment you arrive, won’t I look too frivolous?”

“Don’t worry. The most talkative man in the world is sitting in front of you. What happened?”

Then the Drunken Demon told me what had happened.

“I was drinking alone on a boat I’d set afloat on the lake, and she flew over and got on. Then she asked me to drink with her.”

“And then?”

“She said she wanted to have a drinking contest with me.”

It was completely unexpected.
