Perhaps because he was getting along with her elder brother and Geom Mugeuk?

This time, Jin Haryeong examined Baek Jagang carefully.

He felt completely different from the Heavenly Demon. He was different from her grandfather too.

Rather, of the three men, he was the one whose true nature was hardest to discern from outward appearance alone.

However, that black martial uniform told a few stories.

He wore such attire to this kind of occasion? Normally, one would think that.

"I am not someone who cares about outward things."

But she could not sense any such intention at all.

Instead, she got this feeling. The resolve that if trouble broke out here, he would fight in the attire most convenient for combat.

It was a feeling where all intention was swallowed by that resolve, and it was entirely natural. A person focused solely on himself rather than justification or face. Because of that, he too looked truly strong.

Having looked around at the people on this floor, she unconsciously let out a light sigh. She was astonished to find herself in such a place.

Did Jin Paecheon, who was nearby, hear her sigh?

Or was it because he thought of his dead son and daughter-in-law?

Jin Paecheon had never boasted about his grandchildren to the martial artists of the Martial Alliance or the Orthodox faction.

For the Alliance Leader to boast about his grandson and granddaughter like a besotted fool.

This was something the prideful and honor-conscious Jin Paecheon would never permit.

Thus, today's boasting was truly a boast he had held back for a long time. It was a boast directed at the two people before his eyes, and also at the Orthodox experts on the first floor who were pretending not to know.

Jin Paecheon turned to look at Jin Haryeong.

"This old man thinks you are the Greatest Beauty Under Heaven."

Having said this before everyone, Jin Haryeong's face burned red.

'No! Grandfather! Please!'

She forced herself to answer calmly.

"There are so many beautiful people in the world. The others will tease me."

"How dare anyone tease my granddaughter?"

Then Bisain and Jin Hagun's gazes turned to one person as if they had been waiting. Even Geom Muyang, who was meeting Jin Paecheon for the first time today, looked at Geom Mugeuk, who was standing nearby, with an expression that said, "Who else would tease her but this fellow?"

Jin Paecheon asked the protagonist of their gazes.

"What do you think?"

Jin Haryeong was inwardly nervous about what words would come from Geom Mugeuk's mouth. He would obviously tease her with a jest.

"I think the same. If a tournament to select the Greatest Beauty Under Heaven were held, Young Lady Jin would certainly win. Even if the Alliance Leader did not judge, she would win!"

Jin Paecheon put on a satisfied smile.

The successors smiled faintly at Geom Mugeuk's answer. He could have simply answered that she was the most beautiful, yet he had to mention the tournament and even the judging.

There was probably no one else in this world who would answer the Martial Alliance Leader's question in such a manner besides Geom Mugeuk.

Jin Haryeong found Geom Mugeuk's answer amusing, yet at the same time she felt happy. Even though there was a more beautiful Yi An, she was grateful that he said this before her grandfather.

"Thank you for thinking so highly of me."

Of course, her true feelings were different.

'While thinking that the Greatest Beauty Under Heaven is Yi An.'

She sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk, and a reply came back.

'Even if your grandfather judged, Yi An would probably take first place.'

'Grandfather, this person is teasing me! I should tell on him to Grandfather.'

Today, Jin Paecheon was busy. He had made up his mind. This time, he began to boast about Jin Hagun.

"Raising sons may be useless, but a son is still a son in his own way, is he not?"

As he said this, he looked at Jin Hagun, who was standing behind him.

"Full of chivalry and responsibility. Where else could one see such an excellent son? I am proud of you."

Of course, to him Jin Hagun was his grandson, but he called him a son to compare him with the other successors.

Jin Hagun was flustered. Jin Paecheon was not someone who normally looked after him like this. Fearing that he might do something vain using the fact that he was his grandson, he had treated Jin Hagun even more strictly and fiercely. Having heard the words "I am proud of you" after so long, Jin Hagun was deeply moved.

With Geom Mugeuk and Bisain as today's comparisons, how could he say that he was better than those two? But for this moment alone, he set aside humility.

"It is thanks to Grandfather's excellent teachings and guidance."

After speaking, he wondered when he had last said such words to his grandfather. Had he ever? Surprisingly, he couldn't even remember.

It had been a long time since he had heard the words "proud," but he too had never properly expressed his gratitude.

Jin Hagun sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

"Did you know? I cannot even remember when I last expressed my gratitude to Grandfather before this."

Geom Mugeuk had become someone to whom he could comfortably convey his honest feelings at such moments.

"Aren't we those sons he mentioned earlier? Those sons who are useless no matter how you raise them."

That was probably why. Because he was someone who read his heart and comforted him. Someone who even showed him the path forward.

"But if we come to our senses once more, won't we do it properly?"

Jin Hagun felt that this meeting alone was meaningful simply for having newly perceived his relationship with his grandfather.

Baek Jagang thought that Bisain was still lacking compared to Geom Mugeuk, but he had never once thought Jin Hagun was lacking. Second place was permissible, but third was absolutely unacceptable.

'No matter how much he boasts, he cannot compare to our Sain.'

He resolved to make that point clear before today's gathering ended.

In any case, after the three successors came up, the atmosphere on this floor changed. At the very least, conversation began to flow actively.

The Demonic Lords were harboring similar thoughts about Geom Mugeuk.

"As expected! It gets resolved only when the Young Cult Leader steps in."

Geom Mugeuk did not miss this favorable turn. He shouted toward the lower floor.

"Proprietor, liquor and food, please."

"Understood."

Jo Chunbae went up carrying liquor and side dishes.

He had gone in and out of this floor countless times, but he had never trembled as much as at this moment.

'If I spill this, I spill my life!'

His hands truly trembled, but he mustered all the courage he had.

He set down the liquor and side dishes at each seat. It was the most nerve-wracking and suffocating moment of his life.

"Please enjoy."

As he was turning to leave, Jin Paecheon asked Jo Chunbae,

"Do you know? Why the Young Cult Leader hosted this meeting at your tavern."

Jo Chunbae hadn't expected to receive such a question from the Martial Alliance Leader.

"I do not know either."

Jin Paecheon believed there must be another reason the meeting was hosted at this tavern of all places. How good could the liquor possibly taste? How good could the proprietor's personality possibly be? Even if they were the best, why here?

Then an unexpected person answered in his stead.

"My son's demonic path is here."

The most surprised person at those words was Geom Mugeuk. He had never dreamed his father would say such a thing.

A single phrase from his father's mouth.

My son's demonic path.

Geom Mugeuk was so moved. He was so overjoyed that his mind went blank.

Jin Paecheon looked at Geom Ujin in silence for a moment. He too had not expected Geom Ujin to answer for him. His son's demonic path was here?

Had it been any other words, he would not have continued the conversation, but since it was a matter of children, he asked out of courtesy.

"Cult Leader, do you know what kind of demonic path your son's path is?"

Everyone's gaze focused on Geom Ujin. Those on the first floor were likewise straining their ears to listen to their conversation. The Demonic Lords in particular were curious about what answer would come.

After a brief pause, Geom Ujin spoke.

"I am endeavoring to understand it."

The word "endeavoring" was heard differently by each listener. Most understood it as a roundabout way of saying he would not acknowledge it, but to Geom Mugeuk and those who knew him, it was different.

'He is trying to understand me.'

From the very fact that he had answered in his stead, Geom Mugeuk could feel his father's heart. It was not an answer directed at the Martial Alliance Leader. That answer was directed at him.

Geom Mugeuk's gaze fixed on his father's back.

'Thank you, Father.'

Perhaps as courtesy for the earlier question? Geom Ujin asked Jin Paecheon,

"Alliance Leader, how well do you understand your grandchildren?"

Because he had not expected such a question, Jin Paecheon was troubled over how to answer. He answered honestly. He might have been different with other matters, but he took matters of blood family seriously.

"I too am endeavoring."

He wondered if this was the first moment of empathy since they had sat down.

Baek Jagang abruptly asked Jin Paecheon,

"Then does the Alliance Leader know what level your grandchild dreams of reaching?"

In that instant, Jin Paecheon was flustered. He had never had a conversation about this.

"You said you were endeavoring, but I wonder if it is only in words."

It was Baek Jagang's polite counterattack. He was not one to simply take being left so dumbfounded and bewildered. Boasting about children? He hadn't even started yet.

Jin Paecheon tried to make excuses and glanced at Geom Ujin. Since he was the one who had brought up his son's demonic path, he hoped Geom Ujin would chip in a word.

If Geom Ujin said just one word, it would be better than him making pathetic excuses.

'Alliance Leader, you wouldn't know because you haven't experienced it, but having a proper conversation with a grown child is more difficult than you think.'

That would have been enough. He didn't even expect him to add that it's hard when they're young and hard when they're grown.

"Did we not form an Orthodox-Demonic united front together? Did we not form a bond over our bloodlines?"

Meeting Jin Paecheon's expectant gaze, Geom Ujin said calmly,

"These days, I converse with him often."

Absolute Regression

Chapter 389: Where Is the Young Cult Leader's Demonic Path?
-----------------------------------------------------------
It was the moment the Orthodox-Demonic Alliance was disbanding.

What the hell? You mean you’ve been talking with me a lot lately? Didn’t I make it clear I wasn’t looking at you because I wanted to hear that answer? And yet you stared straight into my eyes and backstabbed me like this?

Baek Jagang’s expression brightened as he launched a signal flare announcing the new formation of the Heretical-Demonic Alliance.

‘These accursed heretics!’

Regardless, Geom Ujin spoke again as if his words were sincere.

“We hunt together, fish together, and play Go as well.”

Geom Mugeuk was startled.

“Huh? We haven’t gone fishing, though?”

For a moment, Jin Paecheon’s eyes flashed coldly. Could it be that he made something up from nothing? Without even talking to his children? Despite the suspicious gazes directed at him, Geom Ujin remained calm.

“Do I have only one son?”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk had asked while guessing that his father had gone fishing with his older brother. Geom Ujin was not someone who said things that weren’t true. He had wanted to show at this gathering that he looked after his older son as well.

“This is too much! Please take me fishing too!”

Geom Ujin pretended not to hear and looked at Jin Paecheon as he spoke.

“The time spent with my son was good, but it was also time I needed for myself.”

The two men’s gazes collided in midair.

Those arrogant, haughty eyes that soured one’s mood just by looking at them. Because of that, one could tell. That it was not a lie. Because those eyes could not even feel the need to bother lying.

“Yes, children are good, and time for oneself is also good.”

Jin Paecheon’s gaze settled coldly.

“Then what will you do?”

The excited atmosphere instantly cooled.

It was a question asked not by a grandfather doting on his grandchild, but by Jin Paecheon, the Martial Alliance Leader.

Baek Jagang also watched silently to see what answer Geom Ujin would give.

Geom Ujin stared at the wine bottle placed before him. Depending on the answer, the wine in that bottle might not even have its first glass poured.

“Do you think you can stop us by saving such time?”

The moment the words ended, the air changed in an instant. It grew cold and heavy.

Bisain looked at Geom Mugeuk. Jin Hagun and Jin Haryeong also looked at Geom Mugeuk.

But instead of looking at them, Geom Mugeuk looked at his father’s back. Both his father and the Heavenly Demon Soul engraved upon his back were ones who did not look back. They only ever went forward.

The father who had turned back briefly for his son was now looking ahead once more. He was looking at the entire martial world.

He had asked the Martial Alliance Leader: Can you stop us?

The first to react were the orthodox experts on the first floor.

They simultaneously revealed their auras and prepared to strike at any moment. What the Demonic Cult Leader had said moments ago was a clear provocation.

The heretical experts also made thorough preparations. They strategized via voice transmission on how to move if the Demonic Cult and the Orthodox faction fought.

Hyeolcheon Doma stood with his arms crossed, looking at the orthodox experts standing before him. He wore a serious expression that seemed to be pondering whom to cut down first if a fight broke out.

But in truth, he was thinking about something else. He had faith that as long as Geom Ujin and Geom Mugeuk were up there, a fight would not break out.

He was now thinking of Seo Daeryong. Hearing the conversation on this floor, he realized that he had never once had an in-depth conversation with his own disciple.

‘I’ll have to meet with him separately soon.’

He surprised himself. And he did not hate it. In the past, he would have been repulsed. Because he had believed that such soft, mushy feelings only hastened one’s death.

He suddenly recalled words that Geom Mugeuk had once heard from the Cult Leader.

Such cheap sentimentality, perfect for getting oneself killed.

Yes, that was what he would have thought. Because he too had lived thinking like the Cult Leader. But these days, he often thought that among all the emotions he possessed, perhaps that was the most precious.

Hyeolcheon Doma turned his head at the fervor he felt from beside him.

Ilhwa Sword Venerable had already chosen her opponent. Should a chaotic battle break out, she intended to kill the Taeeul Divine Sword.

The Taeeul Divine Sword had also read her killing intent, and thus secretly hoped a fight would break out.

‘Not a single one of these Demonic Cult bastards must be left alive.’

Because that was his unwavering heart as always.

As the atmosphere grew murderous, Chwima opened a wine bottle and downed it in one long gulp.

A faint aroma of alcohol heightened the tension in the hall even further.

Of course, there were those who remained composed even in this situation.

The Poison King opened the window, rested his chin on the frame, and looked outside.

Someone might have sent a chilly aura his way for his insolence, but no one dared pick a fight with the Poison King. He could be killed, but only if everyone died alongside him. The Poison King had always been such a being.

While the tension stretched tight—

Geom Mugeuk sent a telepathic message to Baek Jagang.

‘Please intervene.’

‘Me?’

He found it unexpected to be asked for help, yet at the same time, it was not a bad feeling.

‘Then who would intervene?’

‘Wouldn’t we welcome it if the Orthodox and the Demonic fought?’

‘What if they fight and grow fond of each other?’

‘That would be troublesome.’

Among the three men present, Baek Jagang was the most flexible.

“Sain, pour me a drink.”

“Yes, Master.”

Bisain filled Baek Jagang’s cup.

Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon both understood his intention well. If they truly did not intend to engage in a life-or-death bloodbath here.

“Mugeuk, pour me a drink.”

“Yes, Father.”

Jin Paecheon did not back down either.

“Hagun, pour me a drink too.”

“Yes, Grandfather.”

And so the three men each took a drink.

Setting down his cup, Jin Paecheon asked Geom Mugeuk.

“Your Demonic Path is here?”

“That is correct.”

“Where is that Demonic Path?”

At Jin Paecheon’s question, everyone’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk. Not only Jin Paecheon but also Baek Jagang was curious. Just where in this tavern could his Demonic Path be?

An unexpected answer flowed from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“You are looking at it right now, are you not?”

“What do you mean?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Jo Chunbae, who had been unable to step down and was standing awkwardly nearby.

Only then did Jin Paecheon realize that Geom Mugeuk’s Demonic Path was Jo Chunbae.

“That innkeeper is your Demonic Path?”

“That is correct.”

The person in question, Jo Chunbae, was the most surprised. Though he had grown quite used to surprises lately, this was yet another record-breaking moment.

‘Have you ever become the Young Cult Leader’s Demonic Path, old boy? This man must be drunk. And in front of the Demonic Cult Leader, the Martial Alliance Leader, and the Heretical Alliance Leader, no less. Heh, he must be very drunk.’

This time, Jin Paecheon asked Jo Chunbae.

“Are you the Young Cult Leader’s Demonic Path?”

“How could I dare?”

Jo Chunbae was so flustered he did not know what to say.

“Tell me your thoughts. I wish to hear the thoughts of the person in question. I am not threatening you, so answer comfortably.”

‘He tells me to be comfortable, but how could this be a comfortable place?’

The flustered Jo Chunbae did not know what to do and suddenly looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk was smiling as he looked at him. As always, Geom Mugeuk’s eyes seemed to say, ‘Do not be afraid, for I am here.’

How could he repay the grace of a man who bestowed such great meaning upon an insignificant wretch like himself? Even giving his life would fall far short.

“I…”

The moment he began to speak, the surroundings grew so quiet that not a single breath was audible. He could feel everyone focusing on him.

‘Oh, my heart! Please stop pounding!’

Jin Paecheon said with a smile.

“Think of yourself as the man I saw this morning and speak. You spoke well then, did you not?’

As if I only spoke well? I even gave the Alliance Lord quite the scolding.

Jo Chunbae took a deep breath and began to speak. In truth, these were words he had wanted to say not to Jin Paecheon, but to the Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin. Since this would be his only chance to say them to him, Jo Chunbae summoned his courage.

“The Young Cult Leader opened a Hwangcheongak branch across from our tavern. He told us that if anything unfair happened, we should go there and report it. We had lived our whole lives being pushed around by martial artists, but we actually got to see Hwangcheongak martial artists and even filed reports there. These days, we’ve become close with them. When we have delicious food, we take it to the branch for them to eat.”

Because his voice trembled, it felt all the more sincere.

“The Young Cult Leader often came to my tavern to drink and told me this: ‘My Demonic Path is one who does not break tables.’”

The experts on the first floor hearing these words for the first time turned and exchanged glances. Had they heard wrong? What had he said does not break?

“After that, the people who got drunk, beat others, and made trouble gradually disappeared. The martial artists who treated the merchants here like servants also disappeared. Fewer people died in accidents, and fewer went missing. I do not know what the Demonic Path is, what the Orthodox Path is, or what the Heretical Path is.”

Jo Chunbae spoke with his eyes tightly shut. The only thing giving him strength to overcome his fear was his heart for Geom Mugeuk alone.

“However, if I were given a choice and had to pick just one among all the ‘Paths’ in the world, I would choose the Young Cult Leader’s Demonic Path without hesitation. I would choose it even without knowing what it is. I dare say that not only me, but everyone living in Magachon would do the same. Because when everyone else was far away, only the Young Cult Leader remained by our side.”

Since he had already spoken, Jo Chunbae revealed the feelings he had never once spoken before.

“I hope the Young Cult Leader does not accomplish great things in the future. Without a doubt, the Young Cult Leader cares for those he meets and moves for the sake of all. I worry that those deeds will be too hard on him. I worry that he will grow weary.”

Having spoken as if in a trance, Jo Chunbae bowed his head deeply.

“A lowly one such as I has overstepped his bounds. Please forgive me.”

His body trembled. Now that he had finished speaking, fear flooded in. He could not remember what he had said. Had he committed some unforgivable discourtesy?

Geom Mugeuk approached him and spoke softly.

“Thank you, Innkeeper. What innkeeper in the world would stand in this place and speak on my behalf?”

“Rather, it should be the opposite. What Young Cult Leader in the world would speak on behalf of a tavern keeper? For the Young Cult Leader, I would have no regrets even if I died in this place.”

Geom Ujin, Jin Paecheon, and Baek Jagang all watched Jo Chunbae’s blazing eyes in silence. The same was true for Geom Muyang, Bisain, Jin Hagun, and Jin Haryeong, as well as the Demon Venerables and the orthodox and heretical experts on the first floor.

Some understood the relationship between the two deeply, while some did not understand it at all. There were those who thought it was a trick or an act. There were those who found it pathetic.

It was at that very moment.

A clear, resonant ring sounded as Geom Ujin poured wine into the cup before him.

“Innkeeper, come and receive a drink from me.”

At Geom Ujin’s words, Jo Chunbae turned pale.

“How could I dare!”

He trembled, but whose command was this? How could he simply stay put?

Jo Chunbae ran over and respectfully received the wine.

“Drink it, then pour me a glass.”

After drinking, Jo Chunbae poured wine into Geom Ujin’s cup. His hands shook so much that the wine overflowed.

But that wine returned to the cup as if by a lie. Geom Ujin had drawn the overflowing wine back into the cup with a supreme void-grasping technique. Not a single drop fell to the floor.

It was then that Jo Chunbae met eyes with Geom Ujin for the first time.

It was a very brief moment, but it was a gaze Jo Chunbae would never forget.

It was right at that moment.

“Receive a drink from me as well.”

Surprisingly, the one who spoke was Jin Paecheon. The sincere heart that cared for Geom Mugeuk had been conveyed to the Alliance Lord’s heart. Therefore, these words were sincere.

“I too do not yet understand the Young Cult Leader’s Demonic Path. However, I believe I at least clearly know why this place.”

Jin Paecheon offered Jo Chunbae a drink.

Baek Jagang did not remain idle either.

He silently gestured for Jo Chunbae to come closer.

Baek Jagang said nothing. He simply poured wine for Jo Chunbae, then lightly clinked his own cup against Jo Chunbae’s.

Clink.

The clear sound of cups meeting resonated faintly throughout the tavern.

That sound served as a signal, and the taut atmosphere dissolved once more.

Geom Mugeuk said loudly.

“Father, Alliance Lord, we too shall have a drink.”

When Geom Ujin and the two Alliance Lords nodded their permission, Geom Mugeuk led the four others and settled into a corner on this floor.

“Innkeeper, bring us wine here first!”

“Yes, coming right up!”

Jo Chunbae brought the wine and cups first.

It was precisely then, just as everyone had sat together, about to take a drink and exchange greetings.

Something floated up from behind and slowly flew toward where they were sitting.

It was a stir-fried mushroom dish that had flown over via aerial absorption, and it landed precisely before Bisain.

And from behind came a single remark from Baek Jagang.

“It’s the mushroom our Sain likes.”

Bisain’s eyes widened. It was the most flustered she had ever appeared since arriving.

Then the duck dish at Jin Paecheon’s table could contain itself no longer.

The dish Jin Paecheon sent flew across the air and was placed before Jin Hagun.

“Hagun, it’s the duck you like. Don’t drink on an empty stomach.”

Then, even from a table where a dish would never seem to rise under any circumstances, a bowl floated up.

The dish that flew from Geom Ujin’s table set down before Geom Mugeuk.

“This is a dish I hate, though?”

Then Geom Ujin spoke.

“I sent a dish that I hate.”

From where Geom Mugeuk sat, he could only see his father’s back. It was as if the Heavenly Demon Soul was smiling.

“As expected! Blood cannot be deceived—our tastes are so alike! Still, the dish Father sent is the best!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s cry, Jin Hagun shouted.

“No! The dish the Alliance Lord sent is the best!”

Bisain also cried out louder, not to be outdone.

“No! The dish my Master sent is the best!”

At this new competition, Jin Haryeong smiled with an expression of helplessness. They say men are children no matter how old they get; they say that to study men, you study children; and these are the men protecting the martial world.

“Why are you smiling like that?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, she raised her cup high and spoke.

“Because I feel so secure. Now, let us all drink together!”

Absolute Regression

Chapter 390: What the Martial World Needs for Peace Now.
--------------------------------------------------------
Baek Jagang sending mushroom dishes to Bisain was purely to show off to Jin Paecheon.

I even know all the dishes my disciple likes.

But Jin Paecheon already knew the dishes his grandson liked.

‘Well, he is his grandson, so they must have eaten together often.’

In truth, Baek Jagang was enjoying this situation. He truly had not expected today’s meeting to flow in this direction.

The way Jin Paecheon boasted about his grandchildren as if untangling years of accumulated pride was unexpected, and the new side of Geom Ujin—whom he had expected to reveal only coldness and ruthlessness—was intriguing.

Was it true that blood could not be deceived? Geom Ujin’s unexpected actions had aspects that resembled Geom Muguk’s eccentricity.

Conversely, it meant that Geom Muguk’s blood might also contain Geom Ujin’s cold and cynical nature.

In any case, today he had clearly learned where Geom Muguk’s path was heading.

His path was not an answer like, “Wouldn’t this much of an answer sound plausible?” to the question, “What is your path?” The Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult was walking a firm path. Perhaps even more so than those older than him.

Baek Jagang’s gaze turned toward Geom Ujin.

‘Your son’s path is not your path.’

The two men’s paths were not merely slightly different. They were roads in opposite directions.

‘What do you intend to do now?’

He could find no answer in Geom Ujin’s eyes, which held only coldness.

In any case, Jin Paecheon would be harboring the same concern.

‘What do you think? Of this father and son.’

But right now, Jin Paecheon was focusing on something else.

“Cult Leader Geom.”

Jin Paecheon looked at Geom Ujin for a moment.

“Will you accept a drink from me?”

Jin Paecheon had been greatly moved hearing Jo Chunbae’s words. Geom Muguk had accomplished something that even the heroes of the righteous faction could not easily do. It was something that should have happened at a tavern in front of the Martial Alliance, not a tavern in front of the Demonic Cult.

From the Alliance Leader’s standpoint, how could he not be pleased when the next cult leader was such a person?

That was why he wanted to offer a drink to the one who had raised Geom Muguk. There was no other meaning. He still hated the Demonic Cult and the Cult Leader.

It was something he had said after steeling himself greatly, but Geom Ujin seemed to dislike it as well.

“I refuse.”

Geom Ujin did not reveal the reason for his refusal.

“You would accept the tavern keeper’s liquor, but not mine?”

Still without any answer, Geom Ujin filled his own cup.

Jin Paecheon could guess the reason.

‘He doesn’t want to get close. Because someday, he will kill me.’

The ambition for the domination of the murim felt in Geom Ujin’s eyes remained the same.

Even if they smiled face to face, even if they drank together, even if they were swept up in a heated competition boasting of their children, even if they sent ten side dishes flying brilliantly.

The fact that they would become enemies the moment they stepped out of the tavern would not change. Such pleasures were naught but an illusion.

Jin Paecheon also filled his own cup alone and drank.

‘Then what will you do? Will you walk that path even if it means blocking your son’s path?’

This time, Jin Paecheon looked at Baek Jagang.

Baek Jagang was fiddling with his liquor cup, lost in thought.

The enemy was not just one.

He must not underestimate those small eyes. If one were distracted by the Heavenly Demon, one might end up with one’s neck severed by that very Baek Jagang. He was one who possessed that much strength and ambition.

One could tell just by looking at that black martial robe. When the time came, how swiftly and decisively he would move.

There was a jest among the people that if the three leaders of the current generation had been born in different eras, three wars would have broken out. When the renowned elders of the murim joked about his belligerent nature, they always brought up this story.

‘And you want us—people like you—to go fishing and hunting with our grandchildren?’

Suddenly, a scene floated into Jin Paecheon’s mind: standing blankly with a fishing rod in hand, staring in a daze at the burning Martial Alliance.

‘They are not ones to be trusted.’

Jin Paecheon turned his head and looked at the one who had nevertheless created today’s gathering.

‘Can you walk that path without being devoured by your father?’

Unlike the heavy atmosphere of the adults, the atmosphere on the young people’s side was bright.

Of course, it was Geom Muguk who was leading the atmosphere.

“Now, now, let me introduce the new face first. This here is my elder brother, who lost the succession struggle and is coveting my position at every opportunity.”

In the past, he would have shot back with a sharp retort, but now Geom Muyang could only let out a hollow laugh.

“I am Geom Muyang.”

He greeted bluntly. Geom Muguk knew. Given his brother’s personality, being present at this gathering together was already him making every effort he possibly could.

Jin Haryeong had heard much about Geom Muyang through her brother.

There was a time when all martial artists believed this person would become the successor.

Then she had heard the news that the Demonic Cult’s succession struggle had ended without a single drop of blood spilled.

At first, it had been truly hard to believe. After properly getting to know Geom Muguk later, she thought it might be possible, but how could such a thing be accomplished by one person alone? It must have been possible because this Geom Muyang was also an extraordinary person.

Jin Haryeong’s gaze, which had been looking at Geom Muyang, turned toward Geom Muguk, sitting beside her.

‘How can they be so different despite being brothers?’

Her thoughts seemed to have been conveyed to Geom Muguk.

“How do you see my brother?”

It was a question thrown to embarrass her, but Jin Haryeong received it calmly.

“I feel a sense of kinship.”

“What kind of kinship?”

“He’s in a situation similar to mine. I mean the miserable fate of being eliminated by the successor in the end.”

The punchline of her jest was directed at Geom Muyang.

“My own life is hanging by a thread, too.”

She wondered if it was all right to say such things to the Eldest Young Master of the Demonic Cult whom she had just met, but indeed, Geom Muguk’s influence was great. With him beside her, such banter came out naturally.

Fortunately, Geom Muyang did not appear offended.

Geom Muyang recalled old times.

The moment when Geom Muguk became the successor and he had asked when he would be killed.

That was why her joke resonated with him. The heart of one who walked alongside the successor was something only those who had experienced it could know.

“Will you accept a drink?”

It was unexpected. That he would offer her a drink. She accepted readily.

“Good. Let us drink, fellow sufferers.”

Geom Muyang filled her cup to the brim with liquor.

Jin Hagun, who had been watching, spoke.

“Didn’t you hear Grandfather just now? Who would dare touch his granddaughter, the world’s greatest beauty whom he wouldn’t even hurt if she were placed in his eye?”

Jin Haryeong, having received the drink, poured liquor for Geom Muyang and asked.

“What do you think? If the Cult Leader ordered you to kill me?”

Geom Muyang drank without answering.

Jin Haryeong said to Jin Hagun with a look that said, *See?*

“At least, it wasn’t something he could say with confidence at this table.”

Jin Haryeong also emptied her cup.

This gathering was so refreshing to her. To exchange jokes and drink with two people she would never have faced even once in her lifetime if not for Geom Muguk?

Jin Hagun stared at Geom Muyang expressionlessly. Honestly, he felt displeased. Geom Muyang should have said something like, “How could I kill such a precious person?” It was a joke involving his younger sister and her life, so his nerves were needlessly on edge.

Then Geom Muguk poured liquor for Jin Hagun and said.

“Please understand. My brother is originally taciturn. He resembles you greatly.”

Resembles me? In what way, exactly?

But then he thought about it, and it seemed he too would have said nothing if placed in the same situation.

This time, Geom Muguk filled Bisain’s cup. Geom Muguk’s hands and mouth were busy taking care of people.

“You are good because you are not taciturn.”

“This is the first time I’ve heard that I am not taciturn from you.”

Well, this wasn’t the only thing he was hearing for the first time.

—One who knows how to see people’s faces will know how handsome the original face beneath the scars is.

He had even heard that he was handsome.

“Young Cult Leader.”

“Why is that?”

Bisain asked with serious eyes.

“Why did you gather us in one place?”

The gazes of the four people turned toward Geom Muguk.

“What are those gazes? You think if it were me, there must be some grand hidden intention or plan? There isn’t. I simply thought everyone seemed too stiff, so I started this to help you loosen up. But the matter grew this big.”

“Then how will you end this meeting?”

“Won’t it be fine if we just enjoy ourselves to the fullest and then return? Given the personalities of those three over there, if one of them suddenly stands up and says, ‘Let’s go now,’ that will be the end.”

To everyone who was dumbfounded, Geom Muguk said.

“The three of you, please rest for even just today, and exchange some jokes. I think that is the most meaningful thing about this gathering. Because they are people who have never played in their entire lives. They probably don’t even know how to play.”

In truth, the same was true for Geom Muguk himself. He knew a life spent running while looking only at one path, but did he truly know what it meant to take care of himself and play for himself?

Jin Haryeong turned to look toward where the Cult Leader and the Alliance Leaders were. They were drinking, each lost in their own thoughts without even conversing. No matter how softly they spoke here, they would be hearing everything.

And then she thought. Perhaps, as Geom Muguk said, the three of them resting comfortably for even a moment might be a much more difficult task than devising a plan for the peace of the murim. And it might yield better results than that grand plan.

It was right then.

*Sreung.*

The sound of a sword being half-drawn came from the floor below. At that sound, silence fell upon the place in an instant.

Geom Ujin drank quietly; Jin Paecheon wore a slightly worried gaze; Baek Jagang smiled faintly instead.

Since he had to stop it before the fight escalated, just as Geom Muguk tried to rise from his seat.

Geom Muyang grabbed Geom Muguk’s shoulder to stop him and rose first. It meant he would handle it himself.

Geom Muguk looked at his brother with a surprised face at this unexpected sight.

Not only the others at the table, but Geom Ujin and the two Alliance Leaders also looked at Geom Muyang.

Geom Muyang walked to the railing, looked down, and politely introduced himself.

“I am Geom Muyang, the Eldest Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

The gazes were curious as to why it was him and not Geom Muguk who had stepped forward.

The one who had half-drawn his sword was Taeeul Shingeom. The contest of qi with Ilhwa Geomjon had been ongoing, and she, who did not fear his gaze, had been grating on his nerves the whole time. Yet it would hurt his pride to avert his gaze first.

He had already lost the battle of composure, and it was a warning that if she did not avert her gaze, he might draw his sword.

Geom Muyang spoke to everyone without specifically pointing out Taeeul Shingeom.

“I know well what kind of people are gathered here. I also know that if not for the Cult Leader and the Alliance Leaders, you would not be standing there.”

He brought up the matter so politely.

“Since you are such people, you must also know how difficult it was to make today’s meeting happen. The one who accomplished the impossible is my younger brother. So I wish to let my brother rest for today. I do not wish to let him step forward to stop a fight from breaking out here as well. Today, I shall be the one to let him rest.”

Geom Ujin tilted his cup, listening to his son’s words without speaking. Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang also listened quietly to Geom Muyang.

Taeeul Shingeom, who had half-drawn his sword, looked up at Geom Muyang with cold eyes.

Looking at him, Geom Muyang spoke. His manner of speaking, which had been extremely polite, changed to something provocative and intense.

“How far can you go?”

He had said *you* in the plural. He had not aimed his words at Taeeul Shingeom alone. If he provoked him in front of everyone, Taeeul Shingeom would have no choice but to escalate matters due to pride.

“I can go far. You may think what right I have to say this when I am not the successor, but precisely because I am not the successor… I can go all the way to the end.”

It was Geom Muyang’s will, a threat that if they touched him, he would see it through to the end.

“So you rest today as well. Have you not fought to the point of weariness your entire lives to reach that position?”

As he was about to turn around, he added one last thing.

“You saw my younger brother, who calls being a tavern keeper his path. That’s right. As you can see, he is a madman. I am that madman’s older brother. So unless you intend to go to the bitter end with madmen, do not cause trouble today.”

The tavern was quiet.

An even brighter light flowed from Ma Bul’s body. Ma Bul was smiling with pride. Because he felt that the time he had devoted to the Eldest Young Master had not been in vain.

Hyeolcheon Doma strode forward and sat at a table, saying.

“Proprietor, bring liquor here.”

He sat with his back to the masters of the orthodox and unorthodox factions. It was an expression of confidence—come at me if you dare—and also his will not to drink while looking at the faces of those orthodox and unorthodox bastards. The man known as the Hyeolcheon Doma had always been this sort of person.

Once Hyeolcheon Doma sat down, the Demon Venerables also divided themselves between that table and the seats beside it.

Clack.

The blade of the Taeul Divine Sword, which had been half drawn, slid back into its sheath. He had not intended to draw it in the first place, but with the Demonic Cult acting like that, how could he?

Unable to be overwhelmed in momentum, the masters of the orthodox and unorthodox factions also sat down in the seats closest to where they had been standing. Some confidently turned their backs, while others remained standing, still staring at their opponents.

‘What the murim needs for peace right now is only delicious wine and food!’

With that in mind, Jo Chunbae diligently carried out the wine and side dishes he had prepared. That was right. They had been standing around in a tavern without drinking, so of course a fight had nearly broken out.

As Geom Muyang returned to his seat and sat down, he cut Geom Mugeuk off first.

“Don’t say a word!”

“What do you think I was going to say?”

“Whatever it is, don’t say it!”

How much must he have suffered to be like that? I know that feeling well too. Bisa-in nodded and lifted his cup. Jin Hagun’s and Jin Haryeong’s cups struck it with vigor. It was a toast of shared sympathy.

“This is nice. Thanks to you, hyung, I get to rest like this. If I’d gone to stop them, there would’ve been trouble. I would’ve gotten worked up for no reason and said, ‘The Cult Leader and the Alliance Leader are staying still, so who do you think you are, drawing your sword on a day like today? Get up here.’ Then the guy beside him would’ve…”

Geom Muyang held out a cup to Geom Mugeuk, telling him to just drink.

“Let that mouth of yours rest a little.”

He spoke coldly, but how could Geom Mugeuk not know? The heart of his elder brother, who was looking out for him. How much resolve he must have needed to step forward just now.

Geom Mugeuk relaxed his posture, leaned comfortably back in his chair, and stretched out both legs.

“Yeah. Let’s rest a bit.”

It was comfortable. In that moment, he felt as at ease as he had lying on that beach within the Spacetime Transposition Art. No, perhaps even more at ease than that.

As Jin Haryeong watched him grinning with his eyes closed, she suddenly thought, though she did not know why, that he looked lonely. But that thought lasted only a moment.

The dish of duck placed in front of Jin Hagun began to slowly move across the table on its own.

While everyone watched, the duck dish made its way in front of Geom Muyang.

“If you wanted some, you could have said so.”

At Jin Haryeong’s words, Geom Muyang looked flustered.

“No! I didn’t do it.”

“Of course you didn’t!”

Everyone’s gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk at the same time.

Under the barrage of stares, Geom Mugeuk opened one eye slightly and said with a smile,

“My hyung likes duck too.”

Chapter 391: If We Fight, Let’s Fight Among Ourselves
-----------------------------------------------------
Geom Muyang silently looked down at the duck dish placed before him.

Was it because they shared the same blood? Even if he didn't see him often, there were times when he could see deep into his younger brother's heart. Right now was such a time.

The duck had come swimming up to his spot as if in jest, but Geom Muyang could tell. His younger brother was greeting him with gratitude.

Though Geom Mugeuk was skilled at all sorts of cringeworthy words, at this moment alone, he was conveying his heart without a word.

And who was calling whom stiff and taciturn?

Meanwhile, Jin Haryeong was putting herself in Geom Muyang's place as he had stepped forward for Geom Mugeuk.

If it had been her, could she have stepped up like that in her brother's place? Probably not.

Even if she had stepped forward, could she have dominated the atmosphere so powerfully? It would have been impossible without a sincere heart that truly cared for one's younger brother. From the start, Geom Muyang had resolved to go all the way.

Jin Haryeong wasn't the only one moved. Bisain also spoke in a low voice.

"Having brothers seems to be a wonderful thing."

Bisain had never once regretted being alone until now. But today, he felt a slight envy. At having brothers who would step forward for one another like that.

Then Geom Mugeuk, who had been keeping his eyes closed, spoke.

"If you regret it, take me as your older brother."

"I'd be the younger one!"

Geom Mugeuk was still smiling with his eyes closed.

"If you're not even sleeping, why are your eyes closed?"

"It's the first rest my brother has ever given me, so I must rest."

Then Geom Muyang spoke.

"I told you to rest that mouth. Eyes open, mouth shut."

"I'm in too good a mood for my mouth to rest."

Another thing Bisain envied was that those brothers had finished their succession struggle without shedding blood.

How much blood had he shed to become the successor? Succession struggles were all like that. Martial artists' lives were all like that. Everyone had been through it.

He had lived thinking so, yet right before his eyes were two people who were not like that. Jesting like that, as if asking whether life was really all like that.

Jin Hagun was quietly drinking. Perhaps because he was stirred by a strange emotion? The alcohol went down more smoothly than usual.

It was also because of Geom Muyang. He already knew Geom Mugeuk was an extraordinary person, so it wasn't strange for him to accomplish anything.

Yet, his heart had grown complicated at Geom Muyang's appearance just now. Admiration, jealousy, and shame were mixed. It was likely even more so because he had heard people say they resembled each other.

What about him resembles me? Aren't I better?

He had harbored such feelings inwardly, but the sight Geom Muyang had shown just now shamed his arrogance.

Then Jin Haryeong poured a drink into Jin Hagun's cup. She said nothing, but she understood her older brother's troubled heart.

"I look forward to the future."

It meant they still had a long way to go, but she truly looked forward to it.

What kind of murim would unfold when these people became Cult Leader and Alliance Leaders? Having seen all three in one place today, she felt even greater anticipation. It was different from when she had vaguely imagined it.

Meanwhile, the atmosphere on the first floor had completely relaxed.

In groups of threes and fives, those close to each other gathered and drank. It was a shabby inn, so they hadn't expected much, but both the alcohol and the food were excellent.

"The taste is quite good."

Jo Chunbae, who had already heard such praise twice, entered the kitchen with a broad smile. Moreover, he had heard it from both orthodox martial artists and demonic martial artists. Thinking of bragging about this to his regulars, he was already happy.

"Another bottle of liquor here!"

"Yes, coming right up!"

I am the guardian of murim peace! As Jo Chunbae was busily running about, Hyeolcheon Doma was filling Ilhwa Geomjon's cup.

"I'm sorry."

She was the one who had provoked the Taiyi Divine Sword earlier. She could have simply averted her gaze first, but her pride wouldn't allow it.

"You're still young."

"I'm not young. How old do you think I am?"

"At your age, you're young. If I were your age..."

Hyeolcheon Doma started to say something, then stopped.

"What would you do at my age?"

"Nothing specifically planned."

In truth, there was.

Without revealing what it was, Hyeolcheon Doma quietly drank his liquor.

"Couldn't you just live as if you were my age?"

"But I'm not that age; how could I live like that?"

Ilhwa Geomjon lightly struck the table three times and declared.

"From now on, Hyeolcheon Doma is the same age as Ilhwa Geomjon."

Chwima, who had been drinking nearby, cut in.

"You must have a lot of grievances built up toward your senior. You're trying to befriend him and let it all out, aren't you?"

Hyeolcheon Doma smiled faintly.

"I suppose so."

For some reason, Hyeolcheon Doma poured liquor for Chwima. He had always been one to say he couldn't stand the sight of a drunkard.

"I know you've put in considerable effort between this man and me."

He knew Chwima had played a large role in restoring the relationship between himself and Ilhwa Geomjon. Though he didn't know the full details, he had heard from Ilhwa Geomjon enough to know that Chwima had struggled greatly.

"Well, it's what friends do."

He subtly tried to take credit, but Ilhwa Geomjon wasn't having it.

"Friends? Don't even dream of it!"

"Didn't you just say you got younger, Senior?"

"Only with me."

Beside those three enjoying such a rare pleasant atmosphere was Dokwang. He was still leaning against the window, looking outside.

The blowing wind ruffled his hair. If there had been passing women, they would have all stopped in their tracks, but the outside was thoroughly controlled.

Mabul approached him.

"What have you been looking at since earlier?"

"I was just looking because I felt a bit stifled. The spring air is rather nice too."

Inside the Cheondok Forest, he researched and manufactured poisons at his residence, so he didn't know stifling, but when he was elsewhere like this, he felt stifled. It was also because he was used to wandering around the Cheondok Forest searching for poisonous herbs and poisonous insects every time.

"Are there no poisonous herbs you're looking for these days?"

"Why wouldn't there be? There are plenty of necessary ones among the poisonous herbs that bloom in spring."

"I'll visit you soon."

Dokwang's expression brightened. Mabul was truly a ghost when it came to finding poisonous herbs.

Mabul looked up toward the second floor.

"It would be nice to go together, but he's busy over there."

Dokwang's gaze also turned to the second floor. The rigid atmosphere of that floor had relaxed after the successors went up. How could he not know it was because Geom Mugeuk was at its center?

As Dokwang tried to turn his gaze back outside, his eyes met one of the demonic faction's experts.

The one clasping his hands in greeting was Goeak. Having fought alongside him against Tu Wang, he knew Dokwang had saved the martial artists of the Heretic Alliance. The surrounding demonic experts looked at him, but he didn't care in the slightest.

Dokwang also clasped his hands and paid his respects before looking outside again.

Seophon Majon approached Geugak Soma, who was sitting alone in the corner of the inn.

"May I sit?"

Geugak Soma nodded.

He hadn't touched either the alcohol or the food.

"I know you don't consume outside food, but let me fill your cup at least."

It had been the same when he was alone on the roof earlier, and Geugak Soma being alone now still concerned him. It was less a rational attraction than a pull born of some sense of kinship.

Two eyes within the white mask quietly stared at Seophon Majon, then abruptly spoke.

"I envy you."

"What do you mean?"

Then Geugak Soma looked up at the second floor. With that single action, his meaning became clear.

He meant that while he served the current Heavenly Demon, you were a demonic venerable who served Geom Mugeuk while still young.

"You truly enjoy difficult things, Lord Soma."

Knowing it meant serving Geom Mugeuk was no easy task, the two eyes within the eye holes smiled.

Gwonma stood with his arms crossed near the entrance. Tasked with today's safety, he didn't let his guard down for a moment. The unit leaders outside reported the security situation at regular intervals, and Gwonma kept all his senses sharp.

This was precisely why Geom Ujin trusted him the most. Because when a task was assigned, he carried it out unfailingly.

The Cult Leader and the two Alliance Leaders were still drinking separately.

After Geom Ujin had refused Jin Paecheon's earlier offer of a drink, an awkward atmosphere had persisted.

Indeed. Rather than exchanging awkward conversation with people they disliked, it was better to listen to the successors shooting the breeze.

Jin Paecheon was recalling what Geom Mugeuk had said earlier.

—Because you've never played in your entire lives. You probably don't even know how to play.

It was true. He truly had no memory of ever playing in his entire life. It had been a life of running toward the pinnacle from a young age.

'Now, of all times...'

Not only did he not know how to play, he didn't even want to.

'Working is playing.'

He had lived with such a mindset.

A life that had risen to the position of Martial Alliance Leader—how glorious was that? Yet it was regrettable that he had not even the smallest leisure in life.

If he had consciously tried, might he have been able to afford that leisure? If so, would he have been unable to reach this position? As nothing was as futile as 'what-ifs' in life, it was impossible to know.

Then Geom Ujin's gaze turned to Jin Paecheon. The two men's eyes met in the air.

Geom Ujin's insolent, haughty gaze softened very slightly.

"Will you give me a drink?"

Earlier he had refused the offer of a drink; now he was asking for one.

He could sense that whatever the reason, these were not words that came easily.

'Why did you have a change of heart?'

He didn't ask. Because he knew this was not a man who would answer anyway.

'And when you refused! Am I someone who gives when you ask and withholds when you decline?'

He didn't assert his pride like that either. In this moment of asking for a drink, it was Geom Ujin who had bent his pride.

Was it because he had heard Geom Mugeuk's words? That they wished for them to play and rest during this summit. Was that why he had changed his mind?

As Jin Paecheon picked up the bottle and approached, Geom Ujin also rose from his seat.

Jin Paecheon poured the drink while standing.

"Thank you."

Baek Jagang, watching the scene, neither offered a drink nor asked for one. He didn't stand apart alone, nor did he deeply involve himself.

The heretic between the orthodox and the demonic.

He was always at an appropriate distance.

This time, Geom Ujin looked at Baek Jagang.

Baek Jagang sensed from Geom Ujin's eyes that he had something to say to them.

Baek Jagang also picked up his cup and approached them.

And so the three stood at a close distance. A distance where they could reach out and touch each other.

With their skills, it was a distance where they could inflict a fatal wound on an opponent with a surprise attack. Therefore, this distance was also one of trust.

Geom Ujin had called them over not to play together. Rather, it was the opposite.

"Now let's talk business."

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang's expressions grew serious.

Geom Ujin released his qi to envelop their surroundings. It was to prevent their voices from flowing outside.

"You must know there is a mastermind behind this incident."

Of course. Jin Paecheon's grandson and granddaughter had nearly died, and Baek Jagang had even fought Tu Wang directly.

Soon, astonishing words flowed from Geom Ujin's mouth.

"If we must fight, let's fight among ourselves."

It meant not letting outsiders interfere in their own fight—in other words, combining their strength to eliminate those people.

"My cult intends to share all information related to them with you and make their punishment our top priority."

Jin Paecheon and Baek Jagang found it unexpected. Because they hadn't known Geom Ujin would lay down his cards first like this.

In negotiations, it was customary for the side that revealed their thoughts first to be at a disadvantage. And this was the Demonic Cult Leader, who couldn't possibly be unaware of that. Moreover, he was more prideful than anyone, so he could have been expected to wait for the other side to suggest joining forces first.

Geom Ujin had shattered all such expectations.

Had this person changed as well? Or was there another scheme?

Various thoughts flashed through Jin Paecheon's mind.

Could he trust this Demonic Cult Leader's words?

It was impossible to know.

As had always been the case until now, the time had come to receive help from it. That very thing which had kept him alive until now—his instinct.

Instinct did not tell him whether the Demonic Cult Leader's words were true or not. Instead, it turned his head back.

Far off, the figure of Geom Mugeuk came into view. Instinct told him to look at the boy. And he heard the whisper of instinct. *If you do not know, trust that child. That child who dragged you and the Lord of the Four Island Alliance to the tavern in front of the Demon Cult.*

Soon, Jin Paecheon revealed his honest feelings.

"To be honest, I have never once had a serious conversation with my grandchildren. That is right. I do not know what heights that child dreams of."

Jin Paecheon's eyes grew solemn as he turned toward Jin Hagun.

"I wish to see the path that child walks. It will surely be a beautiful and magnificent path. Yes, if we are to see that path, we shall have to beat down all those despicable wretches scheming from behind."

Jin Paecheon's gaze turned once more to Geom Ujin.

"Very well. I shall make it my highest priority to share all information related to those scoundrels within our own Alliance and to eliminate the masterminds."

It was the moment the Martial Arts Alliance decided to join hands with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

The gazes of Geom Ujin and Jin Paecheon turned to Baek Jogang.

He had already reached his conclusion. Because he did not talk much, he could not tell whether Geom Ujin was a man with a sense for detecting lies or not, but at least, in today's conversation, no alarm bells had rung in his ears.

"When the righteous and demonic move together, can the Four Islands stubbornly resist? Let us show them who they dared to touch."

His small eyes smiled. Though he spoke with humility, as if yielding a move to the righteous faction or the Demon Cult, his gaze was one that conceded to no one.

No further words were needed now.

The cups of the three men clashed in the air.

It was the moment an alliance of Righteous, Four Islands, and Demonic—unprecedented in the history of the martial world—was born.

From now on, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, the Martial Arts Alliance, and the Four Island Alliance would share all information regarding the masterminds and track down their enemies; should help be needed, there would even be support.

Geom Mugeuk had opened his eyes at some point and was watching the three toast. The other four were no different.

The moment the voices were blocked, they knew the three were having a very important conversation. That was why they could surmise from that toast that the tripartite alliance had been sealed.

This was exactly what it meant to trust one's father. No matter what Father thought of my demonic path, he was at least someone who knew what needed to be done in any given situation.

That was why his heart had been at ease even after opening this gathering. He had simply believed that Father would handle the aftermath. A father was a father, after all.

"Anyway, they are simply people who don't know how to have fun."

Everyone smiled at Geom Mugeuk's words.

"We cannot simply let such a historic moment pass. Instead of those who don't know how to enjoy themselves, let us celebrate properly. Drink our fill, sing songs, and…."

Geom Mugeuk grinned meaningfully and looked at Bi Sain.

"Why are you looking at me?"

Startled, Bi Sain beheld. A colossal storm cloud bringing a typhoon, a tidal wave surging to the heavens, lava spewing fireballs—no, a crisis graver than any of those, the greatest crisis of his life, was crashing down upon him.

Chapter 392: With an Even More Exciting Song!
---------------------------------------------
“At last, the time has come.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Bi Sain’s heart sank. How could he not know what those eyes, brimming with mischief, were demanding of him? He had the thought that what was bound to come had finally arrived.

“I don’t know what you mean.”

For now, he flatly denied it. Who would dare press him when that terrifying face claimed ignorance? Unfortunately, his opponent was Geom Mugeuk.

“I mean it’s time to keep your promise.”

Geom Muyang, Jin Hagun, and Jin Haryeong, who were with them, showed their curiosity, wondering what promise he meant.

At the same time, they all had another thought.

Poor man. What weakness has he been caught by this time?

But for the moment, curiosity outweighed sympathy.

The same was true of Geom Ujin, Jin Paecheon, and Baek Jagang, who were standing a little ways away.

Bi Sain spoke with his eyes.

‘Surely not? Here? Tell me it isn’t so.’

If it weren’t so, Geom Mugeuk would never have brought it up in the first place.

“I don’t think there could be a better time or place than now to keep that promise.”

By using the word promise, Geom Mugeuk heightened everyone’s curiosity even further.

Bi Sain’s heart began to pound. Even when he had waited beneath the sparring stage for his first match, his heart had not pounded like this.

Geom Muyang spoke in a roundabout way.

“I don’t know what this is about, but don’t needlessly put the Young Alliance Leader in a difficult position.”

“The one who made that promise was him.”

With an anxious expression, Bi Sain emptied his cup.

“You’re not going to give up, are you?”

“What would you do if you were me?”

What he disliked, he disliked. But what had to be acknowledged, had to be acknowledged. If their positions were reversed, and he had a chance to tease Geom Mugeuk like this?

“I wouldn’t give up.”

“If I let this chance slip by, I’ll probably regret today for the rest of my life.”

Bi Sain sympathized. The people gathered today were not people who would be easy to gather again in a lifetime.

But precisely because of that, he could not bring himself to say he would do it. Bi Sain offered another reason.

“The people behind today’s meeting may be watching.”

“They might be.”

“And you want me to do it in a situation like this?”

He meant, would it really be all right to show himself dancing?

“I’d rather they did see it.”

“What?”

“Even with you there, we are living well like this. We are living our lives properly. I want to show them that.”

He could tell it was not a joke, but sincerity.

“If we fail to live our lives properly, then even if we eliminate them later, we won’t have truly won. Let’s not grant them any meaning.”

As always, this was what Geom Mugeuk wanted. Ah, I’ll just step out for a moment. Where did you go? Ah, it was nothing, please continue what you were saying.

Bi Sain drank again. One could see him wavering, wondering whether he should just go ahead and dance.

Geom Mugeuk changed tactics.

“Young Alliance Leader.”

When Bi Sain raised his head, Geom Mugeuk looked at him with a gentle expression and said,

“It’s all right. I only said it to tease you.”

“Really?”

“No matter that it was a promise, wouldn’t it be difficult to keep it in a situation like this? It’s regrettable, but I’ll have to give up. Come now, don’t mind us and let’s drink.”

He ought to have been glad that he would not have to dance, but for some reason, he was not.

Unable to hold back any longer, Jin Haryeong asked,

“What on earth is going on?”

“You don’t need to know.”

At that moment, Bi Sain abruptly said,

“I promised I would dance in front of people.”

Jin Haryeong froze with a look of shock. Not only Jin Hagun, but even Geom Muyang was surprised.

As every gaze flew toward him at once, Geom Mugeuk waved his hands.

“Why are you looking at me! It was a promise the Young Alliance Leader made on his own.”

Naturally, Jin Haryeong did not believe him.

“No way! It must have been the devil’s temptation.”

Geom Muyang and Jin Hagun nodded at the same time.

“You must have pushed him from behind and made him fall into a trap!”

“The one always looking for a chance to push me from behind is that Young Alliance Leader!”

“No way! There is no way he would do such a thing.”

“How long have you even known him?”

This time, Geom Muyang stepped in.

“I apologize on behalf of my younger brother.”

Ironically, the two of them ended up helping Geom Mugeuk. Given Bi Sain’s personality, he could not hide it when they spoke like that.

“No. It is true that I said I would dance.”

Bi Sain drank three cups of liquor in rapid succession.

“I will dance.”

Everyone tried to stop him.

“Please restrain yourself!”

Starting with Jin Haryeong.

“Don’t get swept up by my younger brother.”

Geom Muyang tried to stop him as well.

“Don’t overdo it.”

Jin Hagun also tried to stop him. If he put himself in Bi Sain’s position? Ah, it was horrifying.

“I cannot become a petty man who fails to keep his promise.”

“I think it might be better to become a petty man instead.”

Jin Haryeong said this regretfully and looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“Look at how pleased he is.”

Then Geom Mugeuk delivered the decisive blow to her.

“So? You don’t want to see it?”

“……”

Bi Sain sprang to his feet. He had been swept up by the atmosphere and the liquor. If he had to do it someday? Fine, then he might as well do it today! That sort of youthful bravado had been added as well. He also wanted to show Geom Mugeuk that he would keep his promise. In the end, he charged toward a typhoon, a tidal wave, and lava.

Bi Sain strode over to Baek Jagang.

Since they had heard everything that had been said at Bi Sain’s seat, Baek Jagang wore an expression that said, Are you really going to do it?

“Some time ago, I made a promise to myself. That if I escaped that crisis, I would dance before people. I intend to keep that promise today.”

Everyone seemed to understand what had happened. Those words held another meaning as well. It had been that great a crisis, and in the end, he had overcome it together with Geom Mugeuk. It was not just any promise, and it would not be just any dance.

Baek Jagang silently stared into his disciple’s eyes.

‘So you had this side to you?’

Bi Sain’s face was flushed red. It was impossible to tell whether it was because of the liquor or his agitation.

“If you made a promise, you should keep it.”

Baek Jagang’s permission was given. As expected, he had an open and straightforward temperament.

Bi Sain returned and asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Please make the preparations.”

“You’re really going to do it?”

Instead of resentment, Bi Sain’s eyes showed the resolve of a man who had made up his mind.

“I am seeing another new expression from you.”

“I came this far because I was deceived by words like that from you.”

“There’s still a long way to go. I’ll see every expression you have.”

Bi Sain’s pupils trembled. Astonishingly, he now knew those words were sincere.

From the railing, Geom Mugeuk called down to Jo Chunbae on the first floor.

“Innkeeper, go and bring some musicians.”

“Yes, sir.”

Jo Chunbae was about to run out, but the Fist Demon stopped him and sent a sound transmission to the unit leaders.

The unit leaders darted away like arrows and brought musicians from a nearby pleasure house.

When the musicians arrived, Bi Sain went down to the first floor. Geom Ujin, Jin Paecheon, and Baek Jagang, who were on the second floor, stood by the railing and watched.

The four who had been with Bi Sain followed him downstairs.

All the experts drinking on the first floor looked at Bi Sain. Some were already drunk.

Bi Sain said to them,

“To commemorate today’s historic three-party meeting, I will dance once before you all.”

At that, everyone was startled. When the musicians came and settled in front of the tavern, they had assumed they had been called to liven up the mood. But the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance was going to dance?

The experts of the Sado Alliance looked up to the second floor. Baek Jagang, standing at the railing, merely watched in silence as though he had already given his permission. In that case, it was not something they could argue about.

An old master of the unorthodox faction asked,

“Then are you going to perform the Cheonbyeon Geuksalmu?”

The Cheonbyeon Geuksalmu, the Thousand-Transformation Extreme Killing Dance, was an unparalleled divine art of the unorthodox faction said to leave no survivor among those who witnessed it.

“No.”

“Then are you going to perform the Honwon Talbaengmu?”

The Honwon Talbaengmu was a dance of death said to drive anyone with weak internal energy mad if they saw it.

“No.”

“Then you mean to perform an ordinary sword dance?”

To ordinary martial artists, a dance meant a sword dance performed with a sword in hand.

“No. I will dance the kind of dance ordinary people dance. The kind danced on festive days.”

Not only the old master who had asked the question, but the others as well, did not understand his words at first. What did he mean, he was just going to dance? He was going to dance the kind ordinary people in the world danced? You? Here? Why?

“Our Young Alliance Leader is drunk.”

At the old master’s words, the surrounding experts looked up at Baek Jagang again. Their eyes asked whether he was not going to stop him, but Baek Jagang merely looked down in silence.

Not only the experts of the unorthodox faction, but those of the righteous faction as well, found it strange. However, they had all had a drink, and they were curious what sort of dance the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance would perform.

The Demon Venerables looked not at Bi Sain, but at Geom Mugeuk.

When the Blood Heaven Blade Demon shook his head and scolded him with his eyes, as if to say, Good grief, you brat, Geom Mugeuk put on an aggrieved expression. Of course, not a single one of the Demon Venerables believed that grievance.

The Poison King’s gaze in particular turned distant. ‘Ah, I got caught in that devil’s clutches and barked in the training ground.’

The windows of the inn were thrown wide open. Some watched from their seats, some standing, and some even came outside to watch.

Bi Sain stood in the middle of the road outside the tavern.

“Ah, what is to be done about this?”

Before it even began, Jin Haryeong’s face flushed first. She was more embarrassed than he was.

“We won’t be able to see Young Alliance Leader Bi anymore after this, will we? He may shut himself away in secluded training for a hundred years.”

Geom Muyang and Jin Hagun also joined forces against Geom Mugeuk.

“You wicked bastard!”

“This time, you have truly gone too far.”

“I told you, he made that promise on his own.”

Geom Mugeuk’s protest could not possibly work.

“Even so, you were the one who brought up that promise at this particular moment, weren’t you?”

Geom Muyang struck at the heart of the matter, but Geom Mugeuk had a decisive counterattack.

“So? Are you not going to watch?”

“……”

Geom Muyang, Jin Hagun, and Jin Haryeong all pretended not to hear and looked in different directions.

“Young Alliance Leader Bi! You are magnificent!”

As Geom Mugeuk cheered and clapped, the three people beside him also applauded.

The old masters of the righteous and unorthodox factions showed slight disapproval at the sight. They did not like the fact that the successors were getting along like this in the first place.

But they did not dare openly scold them in front of Geom Ujin, Jin Paecheon, and Baek Jagang.

Bi Sain closed his eyes.

He felt as though he were alone, as though he had been cast out into the world by himself.

At last, the sound of music began.

Because he was so tense, his body would not move. Ah, if this had been a fight, he would have died just now.

Jin Haryeong, who was watching, covered her eyes.

“Ah, I really can’t bear to watch.”

“Me neither.”

When Geom Mugeuk also covered his eyes, Jin Haryeong challenged him.

“Why are you covering your eyes?”

“Now that I’m actually about to watch it, I can’t bear to either.”

“You kill a man and then fear the blood?”

The musicians played the music again. How shocked must they have been as well? They had been brought over because they suddenly had to perform, and there were the Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult, the Murim Alliance Leader, and the Sado Alliance Leader, along with the Demon Venerables and the greatest masters of the righteous and unorthodox factions. And above all else, the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance was trying to dance to their music. The musicians were just as out of their minds as Bi Sain. Yes, surely this was a dream they were all having together.

The music flowed again, and this time, Bi Sain began to dance.

“Huh?”

Jin Haryeong lowered the hand she had used to cover her eyes.

Bi Sain danced better than expected.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been watching through narrowed eyes, also opened his eyes wide. He had been the one to make him do it, but he was just as embarrassed and nervous.

“He’s good?”

He was truly dancing quite convincingly. It was not some random flailing. His hands and feet were riding the rhythm rather well.

In truth, Bi Sain had learned dance from Sado Pallang, one of the Sado Thirteen Wolves. Sado Pallang, who had once swept through the back alleys of Hangzhou, had passed down his secret dance to him.

Whenever he had time, Bi Sain had practiced that dance diligently. He had anticipated that a day like this would come someday. He simply had not known it would be today.

Geom Mugeuk could tell. He had practiced, and practiced again.

This was the kind of person Bi Sain was. A person who always tried to keep his promises, and if he was going to do something, tried to do it well.

But Bi Sain’s dance soon reached its limit. He had not been born a dancer to begin with, and it was a dance he had learned in haste. He also lacked practice.

As the movements began to repeat, the thought that he was embarrassed made his hands and feet grow disordered. At times like this, he needed to be shameless, but he could not. His body shrank in on itself, and his true skill began to show.

The pitiful motions of a man who had lost himself were conveyed to everyone.

Geom Ujin raised his head slightly and looked up at the sky. Jin Paecheon cleared his throat.

Baek Jagang quietly closed those small eyes of his.

‘Should I have come alone?’

It was at that very moment, when the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions alike were feeling regretful. Someone suddenly leapt into the dance.

“Let’s dance together!”

The person who had barged in was Geom Mugeuk.

He began to imitate Bi Sain’s dance. There was no way a dance he was trying for the first time today would go properly, but Geom Mugeuk earnestly followed each movement.

He deliberately danced in an even more exaggerated and ridiculous manner, and laughter burst out here and there.

“This is an incredibly fun dance!”

Then, as if his excitement had risen even further, Geom Mugeuk shouted.

“A faster song, if you please.”

The musicians began to play a faster tune.

Geom Mugeuk’s joints bent this way and that. It was such a bizarre dance that one could not help wondering where on earth he had learned such moves.

Once again, laughter broke out here and there, and this time Bi Sain followed his dance.

Bi Sain’s hideous face was even more flushed.

No one would ever know how happy he had been when Geom Mugeuk leapt into the dance.

Even if someone had jumped in to save him when he was surrounded on all sides by enemies and moments from death, he would not have been this happy.

“Brother, dance with us!”

Geom Mugeuk ran over and tried to drag Geom Muyang out by force.

Shrrng.

Geom Muyang’s sword was half drawn.

Geom Mugeuk changed direction and tried to take Jin Hagun out instead.

Shrrrng.

The sword was drawn even farther.

At that moment, there was someone who leapt in of her own accord.

It was none other than Jin Haryeong.

‘No! Haryeong!’

Before Jin Paecheon could stop her, Jin Haryeong was already moving to the music.

It was not because she was good at dancing. She, too, was someone who found it extremely embarrassing to step forward in a situation like this.

But Geom Mugeuk had stepped up like that for his friend, and she wanted to step up and help as well. She wanted to share that embarrassment with them. The moment that thought came to her, her body impulsively sprang forward.

With her joining them, the atmosphere brightened. She danced lightly and gracefully, while Geom Mugeuk danced with pure excitement. Bi Sain, half dazed, danced one dance after another at random. The musicians began to play with even greater enthusiasm.

A tripartite conference itself was unprecedented, but martial artists of the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions gathering together to dance was surely also a first since the murim had come into being.

The radiance from the Demon Buddha’s body shone even brighter. Having traveled all across the Central Plains, the Demon Buddha tapped his foot to the rhythm as if to say that he, too, had once known how to have fun. Had there been no eyes watching, he might very well have jumped in himself.

Even the masters of the orthodox and unorthodox factions, who had at first looked on with displeasure, now seemed to find it like children’s antics and let their stiff expressions soften.

Baek Jagang was looking at Bi Sain, who was dancing with all his might.

Jin Paecheon had seen that kind of face many times before. The face that said, No matter how outstanding another man’s child may be, my child is the best.

This time, Jin Paecheon looked at Geom Ujin, who stood on the other side.

He was silently watching Geom Mugeuk dance.

‘I hope you let your son defeat you. If your first loss is to your son… would that not be acceptable, at least?’

Jin Paecheon’s gaze returned to the children dancing.

“Even when the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions gather, they can be that happy.”

Youth was such a wonderful thing. Why had he never played so joyfully when he was young? Fighting, and fighting again. This time, how many had he killed? Next time, whom would he kill? Had he spent all his youth like that? Without ever smiling so brightly, without ever enjoying himself so freely even once.

Then Baek Jagang said,

“Perhaps it is precisely because they are orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic that it is even more enjoyable.”

Jin Paecheon nodded.

Jin Haryeong adorably moved her hands and waist, as if telling them to try following her. Geom Mugeuk and Bi Sain copied her dance. When the three matched their movements, it looked pleasing, almost like a group dance.

Bi Sain did not realize it. He, who had never truly smiled because he did not want to reveal his hideous face, was now smiling more brightly than he ever had in his life.

Lost in the rapture of dancing, Geom Mugeuk shouted.

“Something even livelier!”

Was it because the wind blowing in, carrying the full breath of spring, cooled the sweat on their bodies?

The three dancers did not tire.

Chapter 393: Alliance Leader, Please Say a Few Words as Well
------------------------------------------------------------
Jinha-gun envied his younger brother’s courage.

His younger brother had always been like that, ever since they were children. Whenever there was something he wanted, he was the sort of child who would leap in first and think later. He was the kind of child who would boldly tell their grandfather that he intended to marry a servant.

He then looked at Geom Muyang, who was standing beside him. He was watching the three of them dance.

What could this person, who was said to resemble me, be thinking? Did he envy my younger brother as I did?

This time, Jinha-gun looked up at the second floor. Their grandfather’s expression as he watched his younger brother was gentle. To think he would see their grandfather wearing such a relaxed expression between the Demonic Cult Leader and the Alliance Leader of the Unorthodox Faction.
